Hordhac

'Bhavishya Malika' waa ururin boqollaal qoraallo ah oo la qoray qiyaastii 600 oo sano ka hor, waxaana qoray Panchasakhas (shan saaxiib/la-hawlgalayaal) oo ka tirsanaa Sayid Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Qoraalladan waxay ahaayeen kuwo qarsoon qarniyo badan, intii lagu jiray wakhtigaasna, dadka intooda badan ma ay ka warqabin. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, duufaantii weynayd ee ku dhufatay Odisha sanadkii 1999 ayaa soo jiidatay dareenka dad badan dhanka 'Bhavishya Malika' sababtoo ah waxaa la sheegay in wax-sheegidda ku saabsan duufaantaas horey loogu sameeyay qoraalladaas.

Si kastaba ha ahaatee, maadaama lagu qoray luqadda Odia, Bhavishya Malika waxay ku koobnaatay Odisha intii lagu jiray xilligaas. Ku dhawaad cidna oo ka baxsan Odisha ma ay lahayn aqoon ku saabsan qoraalladan. Laakiin si tartiib ah, wakhtiyadii way isbeddeleen. Sanadkii 2020, cudurka faafa ee COVID-19 ayaa mar kale soo jiitay dareenka dadka dhanka Bhavishya Malika. Waxay ahayd kaliya ka dib cudurka faafa in horumarinta iyo faafinta qoraallada Malika ay ka bilaabmeen meel ka baxsan Odisha, habsocodkanina wuxuu socday tan iyo markaas.

Sababtaas awgeed, dad aan la soo koobi karin ayaa bilaabay inay wax ka bartaan Bhavishya Malika sannadihii ugu dambeeyay. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, sida inta badan dhacda, iyada oo ay la socoto faafintan wanaagsan, fasiraad khaldan iyo macluumaad khaldan oo ku saabsan qaar ka mid ah aayadaha Malika ayaa sidoo kale bilaabay inay faafaan. Tusaale ahaan, dadka qaar ayaa bilaabay inay faafiyaan warar xan ah oo sheegaya in da'da Kaliyuga ay tahay 10,000 oo sano; in magaca boqorkii ugu dambeeyay ee Puri uu noqon doono Mukunda Dev; in dhacdooyinka ugu muhiimsan ee Malika iyo ficillada rabbaaniga ah ee burburka (Sanhaar-Leela) ee Sayid Kalki ay dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro Meena-Shani Yoga ee soo socota (xilliga u dhexeeya 2054-57); in gobolka Khandagiri uusan ku jirin Bhubaneswar laakiin uu ku yaallo meel kale; iyo in weli ay jiraan sanado badan oo ka hor inta aan la bilaabin Satyayuga, iwm.

Macluumaadka khaldan ee noocan oo kale ah ayaa bilaabay inay shaki badan ku abuuraan maskaxda cibaadaysatada. Sababtoo ah maqnaanshaha xal sax ah oo lagu xalliyo shakiyadan, iimaankooda Malika wuxuu bilaabay inuu yaraado. Ujeeddada ugu weyn ee maqaalkan ma aha oo kaliya in la siiyo akhristayaasha cusub macluumaad sax ah oo dhab ah oo ku saabsan Bhavishya Malika, laakiin sidoo kale in la xalliyo shakiga ka dhashay maskaxda cibaadaysatada. Waxaa la rajaynayaa in iyadoo la helayo macluumaadka saxda ah ee ku saabsan walxaha aasaasiga ah, kuwa aaminka ah ay awoodi doonaan inay sii xoojiyaan iimaankooda Malika.

1 Bhavishya Malika – Hordhac Gaaban

1.1 Hordhac

Intii u dhaxaysay qarniyadii 15aad iyo 16aad, shan awliyo oo waaweyn oo ku dhashay Odisha (Shishu Ananta Das, Yashobanta Das, Achyutananda Das, Balarama Das, iyo Jagannatha Das) ayaa qoray boqollaal qoraal oo ay ku qornaayeen tiro aan la soo koobi karin oo faallooyin ah. Qoraalladan waxaa loo yaqaanay 'Bhavishya Malika'. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu, oo dhashay 1486-kii, laguna tixgeliyo inuu yahay muuqasho laba-geesood ah oo Bhagawan Sri Krishna iyo Jaganmata Radharani ah, wuxuu xiriir qoto dheer la lahaa shantan awliyo. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu wuxuu shantan ugu yeeray 'Panchasakha' (Shan Saaxiib). Panchasakhas-kan waxay qoreen qoraallada Malika amarka Mahaprabhu laftiisa. Marka laga reebo Panchasakhas, awliyo kale oo waaweyn ayaa sidoo kale sii waday dhaqanka qorista Malika. Tusaale ahaan, cibaadaysanaha Salabega oo dhashay qarnigii 17aad, Saint Arkhit Das iyo Saint Hadidas oo dhashay qarnigii 18aad, Saint Bhima Bhoi oo qarnigii 19aad, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa oo qarnigii 20aad, iwm.

Faallooyin faahfaahsan ayaa lagu qoray qoraalladan Malika ah oo ku saabsan mowduucyo ay ka mid yihiin cimriga Kaliyuga, dhalashada Sayid Kalki, burburinta kuwa xunxun, ilaalinta cibaadaysanayaasha, aasaaska Dharma, iyo imaatinka Satyayuga.

1.2 Yaa ahaayeen Panchasakhas?

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, ayaa ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ବଳବନ୍ତ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମେ ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Balabanta Yaśhobanta Ananta Achyuta,
Jagannātha Dāsa Pañchasakhā nāme khyāta.

Macne:
Balarama Das, Yashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, iyo Jagannatha Das ayaa caan ku noqday Panchasakha.

Waxaa xusid mudan in taariikhda Panchasakhas ay tahay mid qadiimi ah. Dhammaadka xilli kasta (Yuga), Panchasakhas waxay soo degaan Dhulka, iyagoo ka dhashay qayb ka mid ah Sayidka, si ay u bixiyaan tabarrucadooda qiimaha badan ee hawsha Sayidka ee dib u soo celinta Dharma.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Udaya Kahani’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ପଞ୍ଚକୁଳରେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଆଜ୍ଞାରେ।
ଯୁଗ ଯୁଗରେ ଯେ ଜନମ ହେଉ,
କୃଷ୍ଣଲୀଳାମାନ ମୁଖରେ ଗାଉ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ambhe Pañchasakhā pañchakulare,
janama hoilu Kṛṣhṇa ājñāre.
yuga yugare je janama heu,
Kṛṣhṇalīḷāmāna mukhare gau.

Macne:
Aayaddan, Saint Achyutananda Das wuxuu leeyahay: "Annaga, shanta saaxiib, waxaan ku dhalannaa shan qoys oo kala duwan amarka Sayid Krishna. Xilli kasta, waxaan dhalannaa oo afkayaga ka heesnaa falalka rabbaaniga ah (Leelas) ee Krishna."

1.3 Muuqashada Panchasakhas ee Afarta Xilli

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Udaya Kahani’, wuxuu ku sharxayaa qaababka ay shanta awliyo ee waaweyn ku qaateen mid kasta oo ka mid ah afarta Yuga:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଦିଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ଅନ୍ତରେ, ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରବେଶ ହୋଇଲା ମହୀରେ।
ଅସୁର ବିନାଶ କରିବା ପାଇଁ, ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିଲେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣେ ଯାଇଁ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଋଷିତନୁ ବହିଲେ, କୃପାଜଳ ଋଷି ଗାଗର୍ବ ହେଲେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଯେ ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ନାରଦ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ କହିଲି ଭେଦ।
ରାମ ଜନମ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଗୁଣ କହିଲେ ନ ସରେ।
ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନଳ ନୀଳ ସୁଷେଣ, ଜାମ୍ବବ ହନୁ ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନ।
ତ୍ରେତୟା ଅନ୍ତେ ଦ୍ୱାପର ପ୍ରବେଶ, ଗୋପାଳ ମୂରତି ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବେଶ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନେ ହେଲେ ଜନମ, ସୁବଳ ସୁବାହୁ ସୁଦାମ ଦାମ।
ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଘେନି ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ହେଲେ, ଦ୍ୱାପର ଲୀଳାମାନ ସାଙ୍ଗ କଲେ।
ଅବନୀ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ନାମ ସ୍ଥାପିଲେ, ହରିନାମରେ ଜଗତ ମୋହିଲେ।
ସ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଶୂଦ୍ର ଆଦି ପାଷାଣ୍ଡ ଯେତେ, ହରିନାମ ଶୁଣି ହେଲେ ମୁକତେ।
ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ ହେଲୁ, ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନାମ ବହିଲୁ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୋଇଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ମେଳ ବଳରାମରେ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ନାମରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ, କହିଦେଲୁ ବାବୁ ତୋର ଆଗେଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Adi Ananta Yugaṭi Antare, Satya Prabesha Hoilā Mahire.
Asura Bināsha Karibā Pāiñ, Prabhu Janamile Brāhmaṇe Jāiñ.
Pañchasakhā Ṛuṣi Tanu Bahile, Kṛupājala Ṛuṣi Gāgarba Hele.
Mārkaṇḍa Je Swayambhū Nārada, Pañchasakhā Janama Kahili Bheda.
Rāma Janama Tretayā Yugare, Tretayā Guṇa Kahile Na Sare.
Tretayāre Naḷa Nīḷa Suṣeṇa, Jāmbaba Hanu Pañchasakhāmāna.
Tretayā Ante Dwāpara Prabesha, Gopāḷa Mūrati Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Besha.
Pañchasakhāmāne Hele Janama, Subaḷa Subāhu Sudāma Dāma.
Shrībatsa Gheni Pañchasakhā Hele, Dwāpara Līḷāmāna Sāṅga Kale.
Abanī Madhyare Nāma Sthāpile, Harināmare Jagata Mohile.
Strī Shūdra Ādi Pāṣāṇḍa Jete, Harināma Shuṇi Hele Mukate.
Ambhe Pañchasakhā Janama Helu, Ananta Achyuta Nāma Bahilu.
Jagannātha Jashobanta Hoile, Pañchasakhā Meḷa Baḷarāmare.
Emanta Nāmare Ambhe Janama, Kahidelu bābu tora āgeṇa.

Macne:

Taasi waa, waagii hore, dhammaadkii Ananta Yuga markii Satyayuga bilaabmay, Sayidka Sare wuxuu ku dhashay qaab Brahmin ah si uu u burburiyo jinniyada. Waqtigaas, shanta awliyo waxay ka muuqdeen jidhka Sayidka laftiisa iyagoo ah Saajiyiin: Kripajala, Garga (Gagarba), Markandeya, Swayambhu, iyo Narada.

Satyayuga ka dib, Tretayuga, markii Sayidku uu sameeyay ciyaaro aan la soo koobi karin oo ku saabsan muuqashadiisa Rama, shantan saaxiib ee isku midka ah waxay soo degeen Dhulka iyagoo ah Nala, Nila, Sushena, Jambavan (Jambeba), iyo Hanuman.

Dhammaadkii Tretayuga, Dwaparyuga ayaa yimid, Sayidkuna wuxuu ku dhashay qaabka wiilka xoolo-dhaqatada ah, Shri Krishna. Waqtigan, Subala, Subahu, Sudama, Dama, iyo Shribatsa waxay noqdeen Panchasakhas waxayna ka qaybqaateen ciyaaraha rabbaaniga ah ee Shri Krishna.

Intaa waxaa dheer, Kaliyuga, markii Sayidku uu ka muuqday muuqashadiisa Chaitanya, shantan saaxiib waxay dhasheen si ay u faafiyaan magaca quduuska ah (Hari-naam), iyagoo aasaasay waddo fudud oo sahlan oo badbaado ah dhammaan. Kaliyuga, waxay caan ku noqdeen Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, Jagannatha Das, Yashobanta Das, iyo Balarama Das.

1.4 Abuurta Faahfaahsan ee Panchasakhas ee Xilliyada Kala Duwan

Markii ardayga Ramadas uu su'aal ka keenay magacyada gaarka ah ee Panchasakhas ee afarta xilli, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu bixiyay sharaxaadkan faahfaahsan ee qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Udaya Kahani’.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ଋଷି କୃପାଜଳ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ସଖାଟି ଅଟଇ ନଳ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁଦାମ ସଖାଟି ହୋଇ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲାଇ।
ଗାର୍ଗବ ଋଷି ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ହେଲେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଜାମ୍ବବ ନାମ ବହିଲେ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁବାହୁ ବ୍ରଜ ଅଟନ୍ତି, ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ନାମ କଳିରେ ପାନ୍ତି।
ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସେ ଜାଣେ, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ଅଟେ ସେହି ସୁଷେଣ।
ଗୋପରେ ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ଜାତ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ନାମ ଶିଶୁ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସଖା ନାରଦ ଋଷି, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନୀଳ ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶି।
ଦାମ ଗୋପାଳ ଦ୍ୱାପର ଯୁଗରେ, ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ନାମ କଳିରେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଋଷି ଅଟେ ହନୁମାନ, ସୁବଳ ସଖାଟି ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ପୁଣ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ନାମ କଳିରେ ହେଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶ କଲେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Satya Yugare Ṛuṣi Kṛupājala, Tretayā Sakhāṭi Aṭai Naḷa.
Dwāpare Sudāma Sakhāṭi Hoi, Kaḷi Yugare Achyuta Bolāi.
Gārgaba Ṛuṣi Satya Yugare Hele, Tretayā Jāmbaba Nāma Bahile.
Dwāpare Subāhu Braja Aṭanti, Jashobanta Nāma Kaḷire Pānti.
Swayambhū Satya Yugare Se Jāṇe, Tretayāre Aṭe Sehi Suṣeṇa.
Gopare Shrībatsa Dwāpare Jāta, Kaḷi Yugare Nāma Shishu Ananta.
Satya Yugare Sakhā Nārada Ṛuṣi, Tretayāre Nīḷa Nāma Prakāshi.
Dāma Gopāḷa Dwāpara Yugare, Baḷarāma Dāsa Nāma Kaḷire.
Mārkaṇḍa Ṛuṣi Aṭe Hanumāna, Subaḷa Sakhāṭi Dwāpare Puṇa.
Jagannātha Nāma Kaḷire Hele, Pañchasakhā Nāma Prakāsha Kale.

Tarjumaadda Aqoonsiyada:
  • Achyutananda Das: Wuxuu ahaa Saaji Kripajala ee Satyayuga, Nala ee Tretayuga, iyo Sudama ee Dwaparayuga.
  • Yashobanta Das: Wuxuu ahaa Saaji Garga ee Satyayuga, Jambavan ee Tretayuga, iyo Subahu ee Dwaparayuga.
  • Shishu Ananta Das: Wuxuu ahaa Saaji Swayambhu ee Satyayuga, Sushena ee Tretayuga, iyo Shribatsa ee Dwaparayuga.
  • Balarama Das: Wuxuu ahaa Saaji Narada ee Satyayuga, Nila ee Tretayuga, iyo Dama ee Dwaparayuga.
  • Jagannatha Das: Wuxuu ahaa Saaji Markandeya ee Satyayuga, Hanuman ee Tretayuga, iyo Subala ee Dwaparayuga.

1.5 Awoodaha Gaarka ah iyo Khibradda Panchasakhas

Panchasakhas waxay ahaayeen awliyo wax walba og, naftooda garanaya, iyo Ilaah garanaya. Waxay ahaayeen Yogis sare, raadiyayaal, iyo cibaadaysanayaal, mid kastaana wuxuu ku fiicnaa dhinac kala duwan oo cilmiga ruuxiga ah. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu tan ku sharxayaa Malika-diisa:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଗମ୍ୟ ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ,
ଗାର କଟା ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଜାଣେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ଆଗତ ଅନାଗତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭଣେ,
ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ବଖାଣେ।
ଭକ୍ତିର ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମହନ୍ତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Āgamya Bhāba Jāṇe Jashobanta,
Gāra Kaṭā Jantra Jāṇe Ananta.
Āgata Anāgata Achyuta Bhaṇe,
Baḷarāma Dāsa Tattwa Bakhāṇe.
Bhaktira Bhāba Jāṇe Jagannātha,
Pañchasakhā E Oḍiśā Mahanta.

Macne:
Panchasakhas-kii ku dhashay Odisha dhexdooda:
  • Yashobanta Das wuxuu ahaa mid garanaya siraha qoto dheer ee wixii soo maray iyo wixii soo socda (Agam-Nigam).
  • Shishu Ananta Das wuxuu ahaa khabiir ku takhasusay cilmiga jaantusyada qarsoon iyo mashiinada (Yantra-Vigyan).
  • Achyutananda Das wuxuu ahaa mid arka wixii soo maray, wixii jooga, iyo wixii soo socda (Trikal-Darshi).
  • Balarama Das wuxuu ahaa aqoonyahan qoto dheer oo ku takhasusay xaqiiqada ugu dambaysa iyo falsafada (Tattwa-Vigyan).
  • Jagannatha Das wuxuu ahaa mid aan la mid ahayn dareenka cibaadada daacada ah (Bhakti-Bhaav).

1.6 Xarumaha Ruuxiga ah (Ashrams) ee Panchasakhas

Panchasakhas-ku waxay aasaaseen Peethas-kooda iyo meelaha ay ku cibaadaystaan shan goobood oo kala duwan oo ku yaal Odisha. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu tan ku sharaxay qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅଢ଼ଙ୍ଗେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ, ବାଲିପାଟଣାରେ ରହିଲେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସାତ ଲହଡ଼ିରେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ରହି, କ୍ଷେତ୍ରବରେ ବଳବନ୍ତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Nemāḷe Achyuta Aḍhaṅge Jashobanta, Bālipāṭaṇāre rahiḷe Ananta.
Sāta Lahaḍire Jagannātha rahi, Khetrabare Baḷabanta.

Macne:
  • Achyutananda Das wuxuu kursigiisa ruuxiga ah ka aasaasay Nemalo (degmada Cuttack).
  • Yashobanta Das wuxuu Peetha-diisa ka aasaasay Adhanga (degmada Jagatsinghpur).
  • Shishu Ananta Das (Ananta) wuxuu xaruntiisa cibaado ka aasaasay Balipatna (degmada Khurda).
  • Jagannatha Das wuxuu Ashram-kiisa ka aasaasay Sata Lahadi.
  • Balarama Das (Balabanta) wuxuu Shrikhetra (Puri) ka dhigtay meeshii uu deganaa.

1.7 Panchasakhas – Muuqashooyinka Rabbaaniga ah ee Sayidka

Panchasakhas-ku waxay marar badan ku sheegeen qoraaladooda Malika inay yihiin muuqashooyin qayb ah (Anshavatar) oo ka mid ah Sayidka Sare laftiisa. Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa 'Chumbak Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲିଣ କଳିଯୁଗେ ନାମ ସୁଦାମ ସଖା ଅଟନ୍ତି,
ଶ୍ରୀଅଙ୍ଗରୁ ଜାତ କଟିରୁ ଭକତ ଶୁଣ ହୋ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଚେତି।
ମୁଖୁଁ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୃଦୁଁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜାତ ବାହୁ ମୂଁଳୁ ବଳରାମ,
ରାଧାଙ୍କ ହାସ୍ୟରୁ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରେ ଘେନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Achyuta boliṇa Kaḷiyuge nāma Sudāma sakhā aṭanti,
Shrī-aṅgaru jāta kaṭiru bhakata shuṇa ho Bāraṅga cheti.
Mukhuṃ Jashobanta hṛuduṃ ambhe jāta bāhu mūṃḷu Baḷarāma,
Rādhāṅka hāsyaru Jagannātha Dāsa Bāraṅga heture ghena.

Macne:
Kaliyuga-da, Achyutananda Das waa saaxiibkii Sudama ee Dwaparayuga wuxuuna ka dhashay dhexda (Kati) Sayidka. Yashobanta Das wuxuu ka dhashay afka barakeysan (Shri-mukh) ee Mahaprabhu. Shishu Ananta Das wuxuu ka soo muuqday qalbiga Sayidka (Hriday), Balarama Das-na wuxuu ka dhashay gacmaha Sayidka (Bahu). Intaa waxaa dheer, Jagannatha Das wuxuu ka soo degay qosolka (Haasya) Hooyo Radharani.

Ma aha oo kaliya Shishu Ananta Das ji, laakiin Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ayaa sidoo kale wax ka qoray tan qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Chakada Madana'. Mahapurush-ku wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ନିରାକାର ଦେହୁ ପାଞ୍ଚଟି ଜ୍ୟୋତି,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅନନ୍ତ ପଞ୍ଚକ ମୂରତି।
ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ବଳରାମ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ ଏ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ଧାମ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Nirākāra dehu pāṃchaṭi jyoti,
Achyuta Anaṃta paṃchaka mūrati.
Jashobaṃta Jagannātha Balarāma,
janama hoilu e martya dhāma.

Macne:
Taasi waa, jidhka qaabka-iftiinka ah ee Sayidka aan qaabka lahayn, annaga oo ah shan Panchasakhas ah ayaa soo degnay, waxaanan ku dhalannay dhulkan annagoo wata magacyada Achyutananda, Shishu Ananta, Jashobanta, Jagannatha, iyo Balarama.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalka Malika, 'Barnatika':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଶରୀର ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତରୁ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନ୍ମ କହିଛି ଗୁରୁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shrī Kṛiṣhṇa sharīra paṃchabhūtaru,
paṃchasakhā janma kahichi guru.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dhalashada Panchasakhas waxay ka dhacday shanta cunsur ee jidhka Sayid Shri Krishna.

1.8 Wax-sheegyada Amarka Rabbaaniga ah

Aayadaha Malika waxaa loo tixgeliyaa inay yihiin ereyada Mahaprabhu laftiisa. Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୁଁ କହୁଛି ବୋଲି ମୋର କଥା ନୁହେଁ ଆଦ୍ୟୁଁ ଖଞ୍ଜିଛନ୍ତି ଧାତା,
କର୍ମବାମ ହେଲେ କଳ୍ପନା ଗ୍ରାସିବ ପ୍ରକୃତି କାଟିବେ ଚିତା।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Muṃ kahuchi boli mora kathā nuheṃ ādyuṃ khañjichanti dhātā,
Karmabāma hele kalpanā grāsiba prakṛuti kāṭibe chitā.

Macne:
Mahapurush-ku wuxuu yidhi: "Wixii aan qorayo, kuwani ma aha ereyadayda. Kuwani dhammaantood waa waxyaabo uu Sayidku go'aamiyay bilowgii hore."

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das wuxuu sidoo kale u sheegay ardaygiisa ‘Patta Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରଖ ଯାକୁସାର,
ଭାବବିନୋଦିଆ ହରିଙ୍କ ଏ ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ସିନା ଅଗୋଚର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Āhuri kathāe shuṇare Bāraṅga heturakha jākusāra,
Bhābabinodiā Hariṅka e bāṇī aṭe sinā agochara.

Macne:
"Arday Baranga, dhegayso! Kuwani ma aha kuwayga, laakiin waa ereyada Sayid Shri Hari."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Anakara Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

ଛତିଶ ସଂହିତା ଅଠସ୍ତରୀ ଗୀତା ବଂଶାନୁ ସପ୍ତବିଂଶରେ,
ଉପବଂଶାନୁ ଦ୍ୱାଦଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ବେନି ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଶତ ଖଣ୍ଡ ରେ।
ପଦ ପଦ୍ୟାବଳି ଲକ୍ଷେକ ଯେ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ସବୁ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ମହିମା,
ତୋ ଆଗେ କହିଲୁଁ ବ୍ରଜ କୁମର ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ସାରସ୍ୱତ ସୀମା।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chatisha saṃhitā aṭhastarī gītā baṃshānu saptabiṃshare,
upabaṃshānu dwādasha khaṃḍa beni bhabiṣhya shata khaṃḍa re.
Pada padyābali lakṣheka je graṃtha sabu Shrīkṛiṣhṇa mahimā,
to āge kahilu braja kumara brahma sāraswata sīmā.

Macne:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sheegay in qoraalladii uu curiyay ay ka mid yihiin 36 Samhitas, 78 Gitas, 27 Banshanucharitras, 24 Upabanshanucharitras, 100 qoraal Malika ah, iyo boqolaal kun oo aayadood (padas). Mahapurush-ku wuxuu intaa ku daray in dhammaan qoraalladan lagu curiyay sharafta ama nimcada Sayid Shri Krishna.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀରଙ୍ଗା ଚରଣ ବିନୁ ନ ଜାଣଇ ନୀଳାଚଳେ ମନ ମୋର,
ତାଙ୍କ କଥାମାନ ଉଲ୍ଲେଖ କରଇ ନ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଛାର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shrī raṃgācharaṇa binu na jāṇai nīlāchale mana mora,
tāṃka kathāmāna ullekha karai na kahe Achyuta chāra.

Macne:
Sloka-dan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sheegay in maskaxdiisu aanay ku xidhnayn wax kale oo aan ahayn cagaha Sayidka Sare. Maskaxdiisu waxay ku sii jirtaa Nilachala Dhama, Puri, taasi waa, cibaadada jacaylka ee Sayidka. Wuxuu intaa ku daray in wixii uu ku qorayo qoraallada Malika, ay dhammaantood yihiin ereyada saxda ah ee Sayidku yidhi; ma qorin wax isaga u gaar ah oo ikhtiyaarkiisa ah.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଲା ଯେପରି,
ଆମ୍ଭେ ଲେଖିଲୁ ପୋଥି ସେହିପରି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Prabhunkara ājñā helā jepari,
āmbhe lekhilu pothi sehipari.

Macne:
Taasi waa, si sax ah sidii uu Sayidka Sare u amray oo u faray, ayaan u curiyay qoraalka Malika si la mid ah.

Achyutanandaji wuxuu ku qoray 'Agata Bhabishya Prabandha Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ମୁଖରୁ ଯାହା ମୋତେ ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଉଅଛି,
ସେହି ପଦମାନ ସବୁ ପୁରାଣେ ଲେଖୁଛି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shrī mukharu jāhā mote ājñā heuachi,
sehi padamāna sabu purāṇe lekhuchi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, amarka Sayidka Sare, iyo jihayntiisa, ayaan u qorayaa Malika. Wixii ka soo baxay Sayidka, waxaan ku sharaxayaa sloka-yadaas Malika Purana.

1.9 Malika waa Qoraal Qarsoon oo Wax-sheegyadeedu ay Qarsoon Yihiin

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋଗୀମାନେ ଯୋଗ ଅନ୍ତ ନ ପାଇବେ ଆଉ କେବା ସାମରଥା।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Yogīmāne yoga anta na pāibe āu kebā sāmarathā.

Macne:
Xitaa Yogis-ka waaweyn iyo xikmadlayaashu ma heli karaan sirta arrimahan qarsoon iyagoo adeegsanaya Yoga, iskaba daa dadka kale.

Achyutananda Das wuxuu intaa ku daray inuu wax ka qoray dabeecadda sirta ah ee qoraalka:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଲଟ ପାଲଟ କକ୍ଷା ଏହି ଅଙ୍କ, କର ହୃଦେ ତୁହି ଠାବ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଲୀଳା ଶେଷ ଯେ ହୋଇବ, ମସିହାରେ ନାହିଁ ଠାବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Olaṭa pālaṭa kakshā ehi aṅka, kara hṛude tuhi ṭhāba.
Kaḷiyuga līḷā shesha je hoiba, masihāre nāhiṃ ṭhāba.

Macne:
Wax-sheegyada waxaa lagu qoray iyadoo lagu salaynayo xisaabinta tirooyinka, tirooyinkanna waxaa lagu sifeeyay iyadoo la rogo. Dhacdooyinku way dhici doonaan laakiin ma aha sida sanadaha kalandarka caadiga ah; waxay raacaan xisaabinno qarsoon oo calaamado ah.

Marka laga hadlayo dhalashada Kalki, wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହେବୁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସନ୍ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବୁ।
ଗୁପତ ଭକତ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଯାହା,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ଏ ଅଟେ ଏହା।
ଗୁପତରେ କେତେ ଖେଳ କରିବୁ,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କକୁ ଚଳାଇ ଥିବୁ।
ମୋର ଭକତଟି ଜାଣିବେ ସେହି,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ବୋଲିଟି ଯେ ଏହି।
ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ,
କହୁଅଛନ୍ତି ସେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Gupatare ambhe janama hebu,
gupatare san sāla chālibu.
Gupata bhakata jāṇanti jāhā,
gupata aṅka e aṭe ehā.
Gupatare kete kheḷa karibu,
gupata aṅkaku chaḷāi thibu.
Mora bhakataṭi jāṇibe sehi,
gupata aṅka boliṭi ye ehi.
Gupata gupata ati gupata,
kahuachanti se dīna Achyuta.

Macne:
Dhalashada Sayidku waxay noqon doontaa mid qarsoon. Xilliyada iyo sanaduhu waxay u socon doonaan si qarsoon. Kaliya cibaadaystayaasha qarsoon ayaa tan ogaan doona sababtoo ah tirooyinka waa la sifeeyay.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଆନେ ନ କହିବୁ ତୁହି,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଗୀତରେ କହଇ ମୋ ପାଦକୁ ଥାଅ ଛୁଇଁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata āne na kahibu tuhi,
dīna Achyuta je gītare kahai mo pādaku thāa chuiṃ.

Macne:
Taasi waa, kuwani ma aha oo kaliya ereyo sir ah, laakiin waa ereyo sir ah oo ugu sarreeya kuwaas oo Achyutananda Das ji uu qoray isagoo xusuusanaya Rabbiga oo ka fekeraya cagihiisa ubaxa ah.

Qoraal kale, Mahapurush wuxuu ku yidhi xertiisii Ramadasa:

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେଲେ ତୋହ ମୋହ ଦେଖା,
ତେରଜଣ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଥିବୁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା।
ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ ଅଟଇ ହେ ରାମ,
କହୁଅଛି ବ୍ରଜ ବାଳ ହେତୁରେ ତୁ ଘେନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kalki prakāsha hele toha moha dekhā,
terajaṇa madhye thibu āmbhe pañchasakhā.
Gupata ati gupata aṭai he Rāma,
kahuachi braja bāla heture tu ghena.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Rabbiga Kalki wuu soo degi doonaa, muuqashadiisa rabbaaniga ah ayaa dhici doonta, annaguna Panchasakhas waxaan heli doonnaa fursad barakeysan oo aan ugu adeegno oo aan ku sii jirno cagihiisa. Laakiin falalkan rabbaaniga ah ma dhici doonaan si qarsoodi ah oo kaliya, laakiin si qarsoodi ah oo aad u qoto dheer.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଶୁଣ ତୁ ରେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର,
ବ୍ରହ୍ମା ଶଙ୍କରକୁ ଭେଦ ତ ନଥିବ ନ ଜାଣିବେ ଇନ୍ଦ୍ର ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata shuṇa tu re Rāmachandra,
Brahmā Shaṅkaraku bheda ta nathiba na jāṇibe Indra Chandra.

Macne:
Taasi waa, O xer Ramachandra, ereyada Malika ma aha oo kaliya sir, laakiin waa sir ah oo ugu sarreeya. Xitaa Prajapati Brahma iyo Rabbiga Shankar ma awoodi doonaan inay fahmaan sirtooda, iskaba daa Rabbiga Indra iyo Rabbiga Chandra.

Sant Bhima Bhoi sidoo kale wuxuu mawduucan ka qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Stuti Chintamani':

Odia Sloka:

ସଂସାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ କେମନ୍ତେ ଜାଣିବେ ନର ଅଙ୍ଗେ ଦେହ ବହି,
ଗତ ଆଗତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଜଣାନାହିଁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Saṃsāra madhyare kemaṃte jāṇibe nara aṅge deha bahi,
gata āgata yugara byabasthā samastaṃku jaṇānāhiṃ.

Macne:
Taasi waa, ragga iyo dumarka aan tirada lahayn ee adduunka ma awoodi doonaan inay helaan macluumaad ku saabsan kala dambaynta xilliyada, dhammaadka Kali Yuga, Malika, iyo wax-sheegyadeeda. Kaliya kuwa Rabbigu uu siiyo nimcadiisa, kuwaas oo ahaa cibaadaysatada Rabbiga xilliyadii hore, ayaa awoodi doona inay ogaadaan wax-sheegyadan aadka u qoto dheer.

Hadda su'aashu waxay tahay, waa ayo cibaadaysatada Rabbiga ee xilliyadii hore kuwaas oo Rabbigu uu siin doono nimcadiisa oo rumaysan doona ereyada Malika, awoodna u yeelan doona inay ogaadaan, oo ay helaan aqoon sax ah oo ku saabsan dhammaadka Kali Yuga iyo Kalki avatar!

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu jawaabta tan ku bixiyay qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

କୋଟିକେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଏ ରସ ତିନି ସସ୍ରେ ଗଣାସେହି,
ମହିମା ପ୍ରକାଶ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ସେହି ଦାସ ଆନେ ମୁଁ କହନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Koṭike goṭie jāṇaṃti e rasa tini sasre gaṇāसेhi,
mahimā prakāsha nishche sehi dāsa āne muṃ kahaṃti nāhiṃ.

Macne:
Taasi waa, hal qof oo kaliya toban milyan (koroor) ayaa awoodi doona inuu ogaado wax-sheegyadan, tirada guud ee dadka noocan oo kale ah ee adduunka oo dhan waxay noqon doontaa 3,000 oo kaliya.

1.10 Wax-sheegyada Malika ma fashilmi doonaan

Bhavishya Malika waxay ka kooban tahay ereyada Rabbiga laftiisa, sidaa darteed ma jirto su'aal ah inay fashilmaan. Qoraalka Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ପୃଥିବୀ ଦୁଇଫାଳ ହେଲେ ହୋଇବ, ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମେ ଯିବ।
ପର୍ବତ ଶିଖରେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ମିଥ୍ୟା ନୁହଇଁ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତର ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ କ୍ଷମତା କାହାର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Pṛuthibī duiphāḷa hele hoiba, pūrba sūrya abā pashchime jiba.
Parbata shikhare phuṭiba kaiñ, Achyuta bachana mithyā nuhaiñ.
Achyutara bāṇī aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ khyamatā kāhāra.

Macne:
"Dhulka wuu kala qaybsami karaa laba, qorraxda ka soo baxda bari waxay ka soo bixi kartaa galbeed, ubax lotus ah ayaa ka soo bixi kara buurta dhaladeeda—laakiin ereyada Achyutananda waligood ma beenoobi karaan. Waxay la mid yihiin xariiq dhagax lagu xardhay, cidna ma awoodo inay beeniso."

Qoraalka ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’, wuxuu ku qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ସୀତା ଠାକୁରାଣୀ ଅସତୀ ହୋଇବେ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ତିଳେ ନ ଟଳିବ ପାଷାଣର ଗାର ଏହି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Sītā ṭhākurāṇī asatī hoibe parbate phuṭiba kaiñ,
Achyuta bachana tiḷe na taḷiba pāṣāṇara gāra ehi.

Macne:
Kaliyuga, dadku waxay xitaa ku tilmaami karaan Hooyo Sita inay tahay mid aan daahir ahayn, ubax lotus ahna wuxuu ka soo bixi karaa buurta dhaladeeda, laakiin ereyada Achyutananda Das waligood ma fashilmi doonaan, waayo waxay la mid yihiin xariiq dhagax lagu xardhay.

1.11 Qoraalka ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, isagoo soo xiganaya ereyada Rabbiga aan qaabka lahayn (Nirakar), wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଥା ଯେବେ ନ ହୋଇବ, ଆମ୍ଭ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ମିଥ୍ୟା ହେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

E kathā jebe na hoiba, ambha Shrīmukha mithyā heba.

Macne:
Aayaddan, Rabbiga aan qaabka lahayn laftiisa ayaa yidhi: "Haddii ereyada Malika aysan rumoobin, markaa afkayga quduuska ah ayaa la beenin doonaa." Sababtoo ah kuwani waa ereyo ka soo baxay afkii Rabbiga laftiisa, waligood ma fashilmi karaan.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale ku qoray 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ବାଣୀ ପଥରର ଗାର ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମକୁ ଯିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ ଏହି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shrī Achyuta bāṇī patharara gāra parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
pūrba sūrya abā pashchimaku jiba mo bachana satya ehi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, qorraxdu waxay ka soo bixi kartaa galbeedka halkii ay ka soo bixi lahayd bari, ubax lotus ah ayaa ka soo bixi kara buurta dhaladeeda, laakiin ereyada Achyutananda lama beddeli karo. Ereyada Achyutananda waa dhagax lagu xardhay; waa runta dhabta ah.

Mid ka mid ah muuqashooyinkii Achyutananda Das ji, Sant Hadi Das, oo ku dhashay qarnigii 18aad meel u dhow Chatia ee degmada Jajpur ee Odisha, ayaa sidoo kale qoray qoraallo badan oo Malika ah.

Qoraalkiisa Malika ee 'Kali Chautisha', wuxuu ku qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳିବ ନାହିଁଟି ଏ କଥା ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହୋଇବ ଦିନେ,
ଟାକି ରହିଛନ୍ତି ଭରସା କରି ଭକତମାନେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ṭaliba nāhiṃṭi e kathā nishche hoiba dine,
ṭāki rahichaṃti bharasā kari bhakatamāne.

Macne:
Taasi waa, waxyaabaha ku qoran qoraallada Malika gabi ahaanba lama beddeli doono – hubaal way dhici doonaan maalin maalmaha ka mid ah. Dhammaan cibaadaysatada waxay rumaysad ku sugayaan dhacdooyinkan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Agata Bhabishyat Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ ବାରମ୍ବାର,
ବୁଝାଏ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଧୀର।
ନମଣ ଏହାକୁ ଯେ ଆନ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ କରେ ଧ୍ୟାନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶଯିବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା,
ସାରିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକାଡୋଳା।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Achyuta dāsa bārambāra,
bujhāe bhakataṃku dhīra.
Namaṇa ehāku je āna,
Achyuta dāsa kare dhyāna.
Kali kalmaṣha nāshajiba,
Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Anaṃta Achyutanka līlā,
sāribe prabhu chakāḍolā.

Macne:
Wuxuu qoray inaan si dulqaad leh ugu sharaxayo cibaadaysatada marar badan. Cibaadaysatadu waa inaysan u qaadan ereyadan inay been yihiin, sababtoo ah Achyuta wuxuu qoray qoraalladan isagoo ku jira xaalad feker qoto dheer. Dembiyada iyo dhibaatooyinka Kali Yuga waa la burburin doonaa, falalka rabbaaniga ah ee Rabbiga Kalki waa hubaal. Mahapurush wuxuu intaa ku daray in ereyadiisa gabi ahaanba lama beddeli doono.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଅମୃତ ଭକ୍ଷି ଯାଇପାରେ ପ୍ରାଣ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ନୋହିବ ଆନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Amṛita bhakṣhi jāipāre prāṇa,
Achyuta bachana nohiba āna.

Macne:
Taasi waa, xitaa wax aan suurtagal ahayn sida luminta nafta halkii laga heli lahaa dhimasho la'aan iyadoo la cabbo nektar (Amrit) ayaa suurtagal noqon karta, laakiin ereyada Achyutananda Das waligood ma khaldami karaan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray 'Udaya Kahani':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଆନ ନୋହିବ,
କହିଲେ ଅପ୍ରାଧ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପାଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Achyuta bachana āna nohiba,
kahile aparādha nishche pāiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, ereyada Achyutananda Das waligood ma khaldami doonaan. Qof kasta oo ereyadan ku tilmaama been wuxuu hubaal noqon doonaa ka qaybqaataha dembigaas.

Mahapurush wuxuu ku qoray 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ମୋ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗାଇଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହଇ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mo shrīmukha bāṇī anyathā nohiba parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
Nemāla baṭaku laya lagāiṇa dīna Achyutī kahai.

Macne:
Taasi waa, ereyadaydu waligood ma noqon doonaan si kale, xitaa haddii ubax lotus ah uu ka soo bixi lahaa buur! Ereyadan waxaa lagu qoray xaalad feker qoto dheer oo hoos timaada geedka Nemal banyan (Nemal Vata).

Qoraalka Malika ee 'Chakada Madana' laftiisa, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀଫାଟି ଜଳୁପଛେ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଅନଳ,
ଯାହା କହିଛନ୍ତି ତାହା ସର୍ବଦା ଅଚଳ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mahīphāṭi jalupache pralaya anala,
jāhā kahichaṃti tāhā sarbadā achala.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dabka ka dillaaca ka dib marka dhulka uu kala furmo wuxuu keeni karaa burbur (Pralaya) dhulka oo dhan, laakiin ereyadaydu waligood ma beenoobi karaan. Ereyadaydu waa kuwo aan la beenin karin oo aan la beddeli karin.

2 Ujeeddada Malika – Badbaadada Cibaadaysatada

Badanaa, dad badan ayaa cabsada ka dib markay akhriyaan wax-sheegyada Malika waxayna u diidaan inay yihiin "taktik cabsi gelin ah." Si kastaba ha ahaatee, fikirkan waa gabi ahaanba khaldan. Ujeedada koowaad waa ilaalinta ruuxiga ah iyo hanuuninta cibaadaysatada inta lagu jiro kala guurka xilliyada.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ହେତୁ ରସାଇବା ପାଇଁକି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶାହାସ୍ତ୍ର ପୁରାଣ କଲା,
କଳିକାଳଠାରୁ ବଳି କାଳ ଯହିଁ ହକ କଥାଟା ଲେଖିଲା।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Hetu rasāibā pāiṃki Achyuta shāhāstra purāṇa kalā,
kalikālaṭhāru bali kāla jahiṃ haka kathāṭā lekhilā.

Macne:
Dhammaadka Kaliyuga, si loo soo kiciyo miyirka hurda ee cibaadaysatada, wuxuu qorayaa qoraalladan Malika. Ujeedadu waa in Kaliyuga qoto dheer, marka mugdiga jaahilnimada uu meel kasta ka jiro, cibaadaysatada Rabbiga ay gaari karaan Mahaprabhu Kalki iyagoo isticmaalaya Malika, taasoo u oggolaanaysa midowga cibaadaysatada iyo Ilaah. Sababtan awgeed, wuxuu ku qorayaa wax-sheegyo waaweyn qoraallada Malika laga bilaabo Kaliyuga ilaa Sangam Yuga (Xilliga Kala Guurka) iyo xitaa Ananta Yuga (Adi Satyayuga) ee soo socda.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଅବନୀକୁ ଚେତେଇବା ପାଇଁ ଚେତାବନୀ,
ଅବନୀ ହିତେ ଲେଖା ଏ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Abanīku cheteibā pāiñ chetābanī,
Abanī hite lekhā e bhabishya bāṇī.

Macne:
Ujeedada wax-sheegyada aan ku qoray qoraallada Malika waa in adduunka digniin la siiyo. Wuxuu caddeeyay in wax-sheegyadan loo qoray wanaagga adduunka.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale ku qoray ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ପୂର୍ବର ବାସନା ମୂଳେ ଜ୍ଞାନ ଉଦୟ ହେଲା,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହିତରେ ମାଳିକା ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଲେଖି ରଖିଲା।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Pūrbara bāsanā mūḷe jñāna udaya helā,
Bhaktanka hitare Māḷikā grantha lekhi rakhilā.

Macne:
Sababtoo ah 'vasana' (saamayn) hore, taasoo ah cibaadada Rabbiga ee dhalashooyinkii hore, aqoon ayaa ii soo iftiintay dhalashadan, ka dibna waxaan qoray qoraallada Malika si ay ugu faa'iideeyaan oo ay u wanaajiyaan cibaadaysatada.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale ku qoray ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ସତର୍କ ଘଣ୍ଟି ଯେ ଚେତାବନୀ ଏହି ଜାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa satarka ghaṇṭi je chetābanī ehi jāṇa.

Macne:
Erayada qoraallada Malika waxay ujeedadoodu tahay digniin; u garo inay yihiin qaab digniino ah.

Mahapurush wuxuu sii qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଏହି ଚେତାବନୀ ପୁଣ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ଭକ୍ତ ହିତେ ପୁଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Sāraswata grantha ehi chetābanī puṇa,
bāraṇa beḷe prakāsha bhakta hite puṇa.

Macne:
Aayadaha Malika waxay u yihiin digniino bini'aadamka. Waxaa loo qoray danta cibaadaysanayaasha dhammaadka Kaliyuga. Sidaa darteed, waa in ay noo caddahay in Malika aysan faafin cabsi, balse ay u gogol xaarayso badbaadada. Ujeedada kaliya ee Bhavishya Malika waa danta adduunka.

2.1 Malika ma aha dadka oo dhan

Waa inaan si fiican u ogaano in Malika aysan ahayn qof kasta. Panchasakha Mahapurushas waxay ka mamnuuceen xertooda qoraallada Malika inay erayadan ku faafiyaan nacasyada hortooda. Mahapurush wuxuu yiri kuwani ma aha qof kasta; dadka oo dhan ma awoodi doonaan inay fahmaan. Kaliya cibaadaysanayaasha Rabbiga ayaa awoodi doona inay rumaystaan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ କଳିପୁଣି ଆଗତ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ,
ଭକତ ହିତରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲି ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଭଣି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Lakshe pañchāashī grantha kaḷi puṇi āgata bhabishyabāṇī,
Bhakata hitare prakāsha karili dīna Achyuta je bhaṇi.

Macne:
Waxaan curiyay wadar dhan 185,000 oo qoraal ah, intaas ka sokow, waxaan qoray saadaalo badan oo soo socda. Waxaan daabacayaa dhammaan kuwan si ay uga faa'iidaystaan cibaadaysanayaasha.

Achyutananda Das wuxuu markaa ku yiri xertiisa Ramadas qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଏ ପରମତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ମୂର୍ଖକୁ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ତତେ ସପ୍ତବାର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି କହଇ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ତିନି ଅଠର ମାଳିକା କଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ରଖିଛି ଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଜନଙ୍କ ପୀୟୂଷ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra e paramatattwa mūrkhaku na kara prakāsha,
tate saptabāra niyama deuchi kahai dīna Achyuta.
Lakshe pañchāashī tini aṭhara mālikā kali prakāsha,
bāraṇa beḷaku e grantha rakhichi jñānī janaṅka pīyūṣa.

Macne:
Dhagayso, xerta Ramadas, kuwani waa erayo sir ah. Ha u sheegin nacasyada hortooda, haddii kale waxaad geli doontaa dembi. Qoraalladan waxay u yihiin digniino bini'aadamka kuwaas oo u noqon doona sida malabka cibaadaysanayaasha dhammaadka Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ହୋଇବ ଡାକିନୀ ଗୋଳ ପଡ଼ିବ ଚହଳ,
ଅବନୀ କମ୍ପିବ ସଦା ହୋଇ ଟଳଟଳ।
ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ମାନ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Hoiba ḍākinī goḷa paḍiba chahaḷa,
Abanī kampiba sadā hoi taḷataḷa.
Nānā utpāta māna antarikṣe heba,
Bhakta binā anya kehi jāṇi na pāriba.

Macne:
Dhammaadka Kaliyuga, inta lagu guda jiro aasaaska Dharma, waxaa dhici doona dagaal cabsi badan, waxaana dhulka ka jiri doona fawdo. Dhulku wuu gariiri doonaa. Qalalaase badan ayaa ka dhici doona jawiga. Laakiin marka laga reebo cibaadaysanayaasha, cid kale ma awoodi doonto inay fahanto in kuwani ay yihiin ficillada rabbaaniga ah ee burburka ee Rabbiga.

Mahapurush wuxuu ku yiri xerta Ramadas qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାବୁରାମ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ କହିବୁ ନାହିଁ,
ଘୋର କଳିକାଳ ଟାହି ଯେ ଟାପରା ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା ହେବ ତହିଁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shuṇa Bāburāma Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhanku kahibu nāhiñ,
Ghora Kaḷikāḷa ṭāhi je ṭāparā bānā nindā heba tahiñ.

Macne:
Xerta Ramadasow, ha u sheegin erayada qoraalkan ‘Chakada Madana’ nacasyada hortooda. Kaliyuga qoto dheer, nacasyadu way ku jeesjeesi doonaan, taas oo keeni doonta dhaleeceynta Rabbiga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇଟି ବିଷ ଜ୍ଞାନୀଙ୍କୁ ଅଟେ ପିୟୂଷ,
ଅଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲେ ମୋ ଅମୃତେ ହେବ ବିଷ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mūrkhanku e grantha aṭaiṭi biṣa jñānīnku aṭe piyūṣa,
Ajñānī āgare prakāsha karile mo amṛute heba biṣa.

Macne:
Nacasyada, erayadan Malika waxay la mid yihiin sun, halka kuwa caqliga leh ay u yihiin sida 'Piyush' ama malab. U sheegida erayadan kuwa jaahilka ah waxay la mid tahay in sun lagu daro malab.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କହୁଛି ଏହି ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଅଟଇ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଏହା ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ଖେଳ ହୋଇବ ତୋତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଗଣ୍ଠିଆ ଭକତ ଅଟଇ ଗଣ୍ଠି କଥାରେ ମନ,
ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ ପୁରାଣ।
ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ହୋଇବ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକତ,
ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଦାସରେ ମୋର ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra kahuchi ehi niṣṭhā bachana,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa aṭai bhaktaṅka gaṇṭhidhana.
Bāraṇa beḷaku rakhichi ehā bhaktaṅka pāiṃ,
Gupatare kheḷa hoiba tote deli chetei.
Gaṇṭhiā bhakata aṭai gaṇṭhi kathāre mana,
Sātasha bāstarī parārddha bhabishyata purāṇa.
Dharma saṃsthāpana hoiba bhakta hebe mukata,
Shuṇa Rāmachandra dāsare mora bachana satya.

Macne:
Dhagayso xerta Ramadas, erayadan Malika ee aniga waxay la mid yihiin 'Ganthidhana' ama hanti qaali ah oo nololeed u ah cibaadaysanayaasha. Rabbigu wuxuu aasaasi doonaa Dharma, cibaadaysanayaashana waa la xoreyn doonaa. Ficilladan rabbaaniga ah waxay u dhici doonaan si qarsoodi ah. Kaliya badbaadada cibaadaysanayaasha ayaan u curinayaa qoraalladan Malika. Waxay u noqon doonaan kuwo waxtar leh ‘Barana Bela’ ama dhammaadka Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sii qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗେଇ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଖେଦା ଲେଖଇ।
ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇ ଏହି,
ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ।
ଉତ୍ତମ ଶିଷ୍ୟରେ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Nemāḷa baṭaku laya lagei,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa khedā lekhai.
Sāraswata grantha aṭai ehi,
Beḷaku rakhichi bhaktaṅka pāiñ.
Uttama shiṣyare kara prakāsha,
Bānā nindā kahe Achyuta Dāsa.

Macne:
Hoosta geedka Banyan ee Nemalo, waxaan ku qorayaa qoraalkan Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’. Waxay waxtar u yeelan doontaa cibaadaysanayaasha dhammaadka Kaliyuga. Kaliya ku shaaci kuwa ugu wanaagsan cibaadaysanayaasha; haddii kale, waxaa jiri doona dhaleeceyn erayadan iyo Rabbiga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗି ଅଲଗାରେ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଭକ୍ତ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ ଜାଣ,
ଭକତ ଜାଣିବେ ଏହାର ଯେ ଭେଦ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଅଛି ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ପ୍ରକାଶ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟ ଯେ ସାଧୁ ପଦାଶ୍ରିତ ଭଣେ ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Lāgi alagāre Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa bhakta gaṇṭhidhana jāṇa,
bhakata jāṇibe ehāra je bheda shūnyare achi pramāṇa.
Bāraṇa beḷare e grantha prakāsha mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
Nemāḷa baṭa je sādhu padāshrita bhaṇe pāmara Achyuta.

Macne:
Chakada Madana waa hanti u ah cibaadaysanayaasha. Dhammaadka Kaliyuga, kaliya cibaadaysanayaasha ayaa awoodi doona inay fahmaan qaybaheeda iyo sirteeda. Ha u shaacin nacasyada hortooda.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚେତୁଆ ଭକତମାନଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ତିନି ଅଠର ଖଣ୍ଡ,
ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଲେଖିଲା ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଦୁରୁଦଣ୍ଡ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chetuā bhaktamānaṅka nimante tini aṭhara khaṇḍa,
Bhabishya lekhilā pāmara Achyuta na jāṇanti durudaṇḍa.

Macne:
Mahapurush wuxuu curiyay 318 mug oo saadaalo Malika ah oo kaliya loogu talagalay danta cibaadaysanayaasha baraarugsan. Nacasyada iyo dembiilayaashu ma awoodi doonaan inay ogaadaan sirta qoraalladan.

Wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ବିନାଶି ପ୍ରଭୁ ମେଦିନୀ ରଖିବେ,
ସନାତନ ଧର୍ମ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପ୍ରଚାର କରିବେ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନରହରି,
ନାମ ଭଜଏ ଘୋର କଳିରେ ଯିବ ତରି।
ବିପଦ କାଳରେ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ରକ୍ଷା ପାଇଁ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ରଚଇ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mlechha bināshi Prabhu medinī rakhibe,
Sanātana Dharma nishche prachāra karibe.
Kaḷiyuga sheshare prakāsha Narahari,
Nāma bhajae ghora kaḷire jiba tari.
Bipada kāḷare bhaktaṅka rakṣā pāiñ,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa grantha Achyuta rachai.

Macne:
Dhammaadka Kaliyuga, Rabbigu wuu soo degi doonaa. Wuxuu burburin doonaa kuwa xunxun iyo dembiilayaasha si uu u ilaaliyo Dhulka wuxuuna dib u aasaasi doonaa Sanatana Dharma. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in wakhtigaas, hagidda cibaadaysanayaasha iyo ilaalintooda, uu curinayo qoraallada Malika. Aayaddan, Mahapurush wuxuu si cad u qoray in ujeedada curinta Malika ay tahay in la ilaaliyo cibaadaysanayaasha wakhtiyada masiibada iyo in loo gogol xaaro badbaadadooda.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sii qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ପାପ ନାଶନ,
ଖେଦା ଚେତାବନୀ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଜ୍ଞାନ।
ସବୁ ଲେଖିଗଲି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଭକ୍ତ ମୋହର ଯେ ସୋଦର ଭାଇ।
ଭକତ ମୋ ପଞ୍ଚ ପ୍ରାଣଟି ଜାଣ,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ଖେଦା ଲିଖନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dharma saṃsthāpana pāpa nāshana,
khedā chetābanī bhabishya jñāna.
Sabu lekhigali bhaktanka pāiṃ,
bhakta mohara je sodara bhāī.
Bhakata mo pañcha prāṇaṭi jāṇa,
bhaktanka nimaṃte khedā likhana.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, saadaaladii aan ku qoray qoraalladayda Malika ee ku saabsan dhammaadka dembiyada iyo aasaaska Dharma dhammaadka Kali Yuga, dhammaantood waa cibaadaysanayaasha sababtoo ah cibaadaysanayaashu waa sida walaalahayga. Cibaadaysanayaashu waa shanta neef ee noloshayda (Pancha-prana), waxaanan qoray dhammaan qoraalladan Malika kaliya iyaga.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଅଟଇ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଜାଣ,
ଘୋର କଳିଯୁଗ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନ ଛନ୍ତି ଡର ଲାଗୁଅଛି ପୁଣ।
ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନ କର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି ମୁହିଁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Sātasha bāstarī parārddha aṭai Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa jāṇa,
ghora kaliyuga duṣhṭajana chaṃti ḍara lāguachi puṇa.
Mūrkhanka āgare prakāsha na kara niyama deuchi muṃhi.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, xerta Rama, waxaa jira 772 Parardhas ama cutubyo qoraalkan Malika ah ee lagu magacaabo 'Chakada Madana'. Ha u shaacin nacasyada hortooda. Waxaa jira dad badan oo xunxun Kali Yuga cabsi badan; ka fogee dhammaantood.

‘Chakada Madana’ lafteeda, Mahapurush wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନୋହିଲା ନୋହିଲା ପଡୁଥିବ ହୁରି ଭକ୍ତ ଜାଣିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shuṇa Rāmadāsa Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
nohilā nohilā paḍuthiba huri bhakta jāṇiba abashya.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, xerta Ramadasow, ha u shaacin saadaaladan Malika nacasyada hortooda. Waxay odhan doonaan erayadan waa been, in dhacdooyinkan aysan dhici doonin. Sidaa darteed, kaliya u sheeg cibaadaysanayaasha; kaliya iyaga ayaa awoodi doona inay rumaystaan tan.

3 Wax-sheegidda Malika Kuwa Rumeeyay

Bhavishya Malika waxay ka kooban tahay erayada Mahaprabhu laftiisa, sidaa darteedna ma aha wax la yaab leh in saadaalo aan tiro lahayn oo ku jira Malika ay rumoobeen ilaa hadda. Waxaan idin kala hadli doonaa qaar ka mid ah saadaaladan oo la xaqiijiyay inay 100% run yihiin.

3.1 Saadaal ku saabsan Atal Bihari Vajpayee Ji

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalladiisa Malika ee 'Kalikalpa Gita' iyo 'Achyuta Thara':

Odia Sloka:

ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ ଯେବେ, କଳ୍କୀଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା ଲାଗିବ ତେବେ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ରାଜା, ଫେରିବ କିଛିତାହାର ତରିଜା।
କିଛି ଦିନ ଭୋଗ କରିବ ମହୀ, ତେର ଦିନ ସେହି ଛତ୍ର ଉଡ଼ାଇ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇଲେ ଯେବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଯେ ରାଜା, ତାଙ୍କ ସମୟରେ କିଛି ହୋଇଲା ତରିଜା।
ତେରଦିନ ଛତ୍ର ସେହି ଉଡ଼େଇଣ ଦେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ମହୀ ଭୋଗ କଲେ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
ପ୍ରଥମେ ତେର ଦିନ ପ୍ରଧାନ ମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ହେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ଶାସନ କରିଲେ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ ଶକତି କାହାର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Tera dina pakṣa hoiba jebe, Kaḷkīṅka līḷā lāgiba tebe.
Khetratre hoiba Brāhmaṇa rājā, pheriba kichhitāhāra tarijā.
Kichhi dina bhoga kariba mahī, tera dina sehi chhatra uḍāi.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Khetratre hoile jebe Brāhmaṇa je rājā, tāṅka samayare kichhi hoilā tarijā.
Teradina chhatra sehi uḍeiṇa dele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa mahī bhoga kale.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Prathame tera dina pradhāna mantrī hele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa shāsana karile.
Achyuta bachana aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ shakati kāhāra.

Macne:
Qarniyo ka hor, Mahapurush wuxuu ka qoray siyaasi muhiim ah oo ka tirsan Hindiya madaxbannaan. Wuxuu sheegay in marka Brahmin uu xukumo Hindiya, isbeddello badan ay ka muuqan doonaan adduunka. Marka hore, wuxuu xukumi doonaa 13 maalmood, ka dibna wuxuu hayn doonaa awoodda shan sano. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in magaca qofkan ka talinaya Delhi uu noqon doono "Atal Bihari Vajpayee". Sida taariikhdu muujinayso, Shri Vajpayee wuxuu u shaqeeyay Ra'iisul Wasaare 13 maalmood 1996-kii ka dibna muddo shan sano ah oo buuxda laga bilaabo 1999 ilaa 2004.

3.2 Saadaal ku saabsan Mahatma Gandhi

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, 600 sano ka hor qoraalkiisa caanka ah ee 'Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta', wuxuu ka sameeyay saadaal ballaaran oo ku saabsan Mahatma Gandhi.

Odia Sloka:

ଜନମ ହୋଇ ଗୁଜରାଟରେ, ମୋହନ ମୋହିବ ରାମ ନାମରେ।
ଅହିଂସାକୁ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର କରିବ ଜାଣ, ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ ହଟିବେ ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣ।
ତା ଅନ୍ତେ ଧର୍ମ ହୋଇବ ହାଣି, ମୋହନ ରାଜୁତି ହୋଇବ ହାଣି।
ଗୁଳି ଚୋଟେ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଯିବ, ଗଡସେ କଳିମଞ୍ଜି ପୋତିବ।
ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବେ ସଂସାର ପ୍ରାଣୀ, ଟେକି କହୁଥିବେ କଥା ନ ଜାଣି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Janama hoi Gujarāṭare, Mohana mohiba Rāma nāmare.
Ahinsāku astra kariba jāṇa, phiriṅgī haṭibe se kāḷe jāṇa.
Tā ante dharma hoiba hāṇi, Mohana rājuti hoiba hāṇi.
Guḷi choṭe tāra prāṇa jiba, Gaḍse kaḷimañji potiba.
Jāṇi na pāribe saṃsāra prāṇī, ṭeki kahuthibe kathā na jāṇi.

Macne:
"Wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa gobolka Gujarat. Magaciisa wuxuu noqon doonaa 'Mohan' wuxuuna si joogto ah u xusi doonaa magaca Rama. 'Ahimsa' (Rabshad la'aan) ayaa noqon doonta hubkiisa, dadaalkiisa awgeedna, Ingiriiska (Firingis) waxaa lagu qasbi doonaa inay ka tagaan Hindiya."

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu intaa ku daray in Mohan uusan isagu xukunka qaban doonin, geeridiisa ka dibna ay hoos u dhac ku imaan doonto Dharma. Wuxuu si gaar ah u xusay in qof lagu magacaabo "Godse" uu ku dili doono "xabbad" (Guli), taasoo uu ku beeri doono abuurka Kali ee qaranka. Xitaa wuxuu saadaaliyay in dad badan ay ku guuldareysan doonaan inay Gandhi si sax ah u fahmaan oo ay ku caddeeyaan falka dilaha jaahilnimo awgeed. Sida aan ognahay, eray kasta oo saadaalintan ku saabsan Aabaha Qaranka wuu rumoobay.

3.3 Wax-sheegidda Duufaantii Weynayd ee 1999 iyo Burburkii Kalpavata

Qoraalkiisa 'Jai Birachit Chautisha', Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu saadaaliyay duufaantii 1999, 600 oo sano ka hor.

Odia Sloka:

ସମୁଦ୍ର ଉଠି ଆସିବ ବତାସ ବହିବ, ଶ୍ରୀ କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଭାଙ୍ଗି ପକାଇବ।
ସତ୍ୟଭାବ ହେ, ସାଲ ଅନେଶତ କର ଠାବ ହେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Samudra uṭhi āsiba batāsa bahiba, Shrī Kaḷpabaṭa mora bhāṅgi pakāiba.
Satyabhāba he, sāla aneshata kara ṭhāba he.

Macne:
"Badda ayaa kacaysa oo dabayl xooggan ayaa dhacaysa. Laanta Kalpavata-da quduuska ah (geedka Banyan-ka qadiimiga ah ee ku dhex yaal dhismaha Macbadka Jagannath) ayaa jabi doonta." Mahapurush-ku wuxuu sheegay in dhacdadan ay dhici doonto sanadka "Anesata" (99).

Oktoobar 29, 1999, Duufaan Weyn ayaa ku dhufatay Odisha iyadoo dabaylo gaaraya 250 km/h. Waxay sababtay burbur baaxad leh, iyadoo ay ku dhinteen in ka badan 10,000 oo qof. Sida saadaalinta ahayd, laan weyn oo ka mid ah geedka Kalpavata-da qadiimiga ah ee ku dhex yaal macbadka Puri ayaa jabtay intii lagu jiray duufaantan.

3.4 Wax-sheegidda Madaxbannaanida Hindiya (1947)

Qoraalka 'Garuda Kalpa', Aw Achyutananda wuxuu saadaaliyay in Hindiya ay ka xoroobi doonto xukunka Ingiriiska sanadka 1947 isagoo isticmaalaya koodh tirooyin ah.

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ଭକ୍ତି ବେଦ ବାରକୁ ଥୋଇ, ଏ ଠାରେ ବିଦେଶୀ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Brahma bhakti beda bāraku thoi, e ṭhāre bideshī jibe paḷāi.

Furfuridda Tirooyinka:
Mahapurush-ku wuxuu u isticmaalay ereyo calaamado ah tirooyinka:
  • Brahma: Waxaa lagu matalaa 1 (Sida "Ekam Brahma").
  • Bhakti: Waxaa lagu matalaa 9 (Sagaalka qaab ee Navadha Bhakti).
  • Veda: Waxaa lagu matalaa 4 (Afarta Vedas).
  • Baar: Waxaa lagu matalaa 7 (Toddobada maalmood ee toddobaadka).

Marka la isku daro (1-9-4-7), waxay samaysaa 1947. Saadaalinta ayaa sheegaysa in sanadkan, shisheeyaha (Ingiriiska) ay ka carari doonaan dhulkan.

3.5 Wax-sheegid ku saabsan Dilka Uurjiifka Dheddigga

Qoraalka 'Jaiphula Malika', Mahapurush-ku wuxuu saadaaliyay xumaanta bulshada ee dhammaadka Kaliyuga:

Odia Sloka:

କନ୍ୟା ରତ୍ନକୁ ଗର୍ଭର, ନଷ୍ଟ କରିବ ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ହୃଦୟେ ନ କରି ପାପ ବିଚାର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kanyā ratnaku garbhara, naṣṭa kariba tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
hṛudaye na kari pāpa bichāra.

Macne:
"Dadku waxay burburin doonaan gabadha yar (Kanya Ratna) uurka (Garbha) gudahiisa marar badan iyagoon wax dembi ah ka dareemayn qalbiyadooda." Tani waxay si sax ah u muujinaysaa xilligii mugdiga ahaa ee dilka uurjiifka dheddigga ee bulshada ku habsaday tobannaankii sano ee la soo dhaafay.

3.6 Wax-sheegidda Cudurka Faafa ee 2020 (COVID-19)

Mahapurush Hadidas, oo ah soo-bixid ka mid ah Panchasakhas oo dhashay qarnigii 18-aad, wuxuu ku qoray 'Agata Bhavishyat Chautisha':

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ବିଂଶ ଯେବେ ବର୍ଷ ବୋଲାଇବ, ବହୁ ବିତିପାତ ହେବ।
ଅଜଣା ବ୍ୟାଧିରେ ବହୁ ନାଶ ଯିବେ, ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dui biṃsha jebe barsha bolāiba, bahu bitipāta heba.
Ajaṇā byādhire bahu nāsha jibe, hāhākāra paḍijiba.

Macne:
"Marka uu yimaado sanadka 'Dui-Binsha' (Labaatan-Labaatan / 2020), waxaa jiri doona fawdo weyn. Dadku waxay u dhiman doonaan cudur aan la garanayn (Ajana Vyadhi) waxaana meel kasta ka jiri doona oohin quus ah (Haahakaar)." Tani waa sharaxaad si cajiib ah u sax ah oo ku saabsan cudurka faafa ee COVID-19 ee adduunka qabsaday sanadka 2020.

3.7 Wax-sheegidda Biya-xireenka Hirakud

Qoraallada 'Chakada Madana' iyo 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa', Aw Achyutananda iyo Arkhit Das waxay saadaaliyeen dhismaha biya-xireen weyn oo ku yaal Odisha.

Odia Sloka:

ଛବିଶ ଠାବରେ ହୀରାକୁଦ ଉଛୁଳିବ, ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଅର୍ଦ୍ଧଭାଗ ଜଳମଗ୍ନ ହେବ।
ଧନଜନ ନଷ୍ଟ ହେବ କହିଲି ବୁଝାଇ, ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ବେଶୀ ବେଳ ନାହିଁ।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲିଣଟି ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକାକାର ହେବ।
ସେହି ବେଳେ ଗଙ୍ଗା ପୁଣି ଆସିବେଟି ମାଡ଼ି, ବୈତରଣୀ, ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣୀ ଯେ ଦେବେ ଘୋର ରଡ଼ି।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ବଡ଼ ବନ୍ଧ ତିଆରି ଯେ ଥିବ, ସେଇବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁଦ୍ୱାରା ଭାଙ୍ଗିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ଛଅ ଜିଲ୍ଲା ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇ, ଯିବାପାଇଁ ବାଟ ନ ପାଇବେ ପୁଣି କେହି।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲି ଗ୍ରାମର ଯେ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chabiṣha ṭhābare Hīrākuda uchuḷiba, e Oḍiśhā arddhabhāga jaḷamagna heba.
Dhanajana naṣhṭa heba kahili bujhāi, dīna Achyuta kahai beśhī beḷa nāhiñ.
Hīrākuda boliṇaṭi grāma nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu ekākāra heba.
Sehi beḷe Gaṅgā puṇi āsibeṭi māḍi, Baitaraṇī, Brāhmaṇī je debe ghora raḍi.
Oḍiśhāre baḍa bandha tiāri je thiba, seibeḷe śhatrudwārā bhāṅgiṇa je jiba.
Oḍiśhāre chha-a jillā jaḷārṇnaba hoi, jibāpāiñ bāṭa na pāibe puṇi kehi.
Hīrākuda boli grāmara je nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu eka je hoiba.

Macne:
Achyutananda Das ji qoraalkiisa Malika ee 'Chakada Madana' iyo Aw Brahma Avadhut Arakhita Das, oo dhashay qeybtii dambe ee qarnigii siddeed iyo tobnaad oo ku sameeyay sadhana dhammaystiran godka Olashuni, qoraalkiisa Malika ee 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' wuxuu ku qoray in biya-xireen weyn laga dhisi doono gobolka Odisha oo magaciisu noqon doono "Hirakud". Wuxuu intaa ku daray in dhammaadka Kaliyuga, weerarka cadawga awgii, biya-xireenkaas uu burburi doono, taasoo lix degmo oo gobolka Odisha ah ay qarqoomi doonaan, gaadiidka ayaa istaagi doona, waxaana jiri doona khasaare weyn oo hanti iyo dad ah. Mahapurushas-ku waxay yiraahdeen isla waqtigaas Ganga waxay muujin doontaa qaabkeeda cabsi gelinta ah, webiyada Baitarani, Brahmani, iwm. iyaguna waxay abuuri doonaan burbur ba'an.

Sida la og yahay, sanadka 1948-kii gobolka Odisha ee Hindiya, shaqada dhismaha biya-xireen lagu magacaabo Hirakud ayaa bilowday, kaasoo la dhammaystiray sanadka 1953-kii. Bishii Janaayo ee sanadka 1957-kii, Ra'iisul Wasaarihii xilligaas Pandit Jawaharlal Nehru ayaa si rasmi ah u daah-furay biya-xireenkan. Dhererkiisu guud ahaan yahay 26 km, oo laga dhisay webiga Mahanadi ee degmada Sambalpur ee Odisha, biya-xireenkan maanta waa biya-xireenka ugu dheer ee adduunka ee uu dadku sameeyay. Sidan oo kale, saxnaanta qaybta koowaad ee saadaalintan ayaa la xaqiijiyay.

Dhawaan adduunka wuxuu arki doonaa qaybta labaad ee saadaalintaas oo iyaduna rumoobaysa marka, xilliga dagaalka Hindiya iyo Shiinaha, weerarka gantaalaha ee Shiinaha awgii, biya-xireenkan uu burburi doono oo gobolka Odisha uu la kulmi doono daad biyo ah oo ba'an.

3.8 Wax-sheegidda Dhalashada Pakistan Qarniyo ka hor

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das iyo kuwa kale waxay xuseen abuuritaanka iyo magaca Pakistan 600 oo sano ka hor qoraallada sida 'Gupta Kheda Malika' iyo 'Bhavishyat Malika'. Sidoo kale, aw Arkhit Das oo qarnigii 18-aad noolaa wuxuu ku xusay 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ପାକିସ୍ତାନର ଦଶା ଯାହା ହେବ ସହସା, ମନ କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦଇ ଥରେ ଶୁଣ ସେ ଭାଷା।
ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ତାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ, ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପ୍ରାଚ୍ୟ ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ଗଣ ଏହିପରି ମିଳିଣ, ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ଲାଗି ସର୍ବେ କରିବେ ରଣ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟଗୋଟି ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ହାହାକାର ଶୁଭୁଣ ଯେ ଥିବ।
ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ, ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟ ଗୋଟା ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ପଛ ବେଳକୁ ତ ପୁଣି ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଯେ ସ୍ଫୁରିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Pākistānara dashā jāhā heba sahasā, mana karṇṇa dei thare shuṇa se bhāṣā.
Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākistānaṭi raṅge, mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Prāchya pāshchātya gaṇa ehipari miḷiṇa, Pākistāna lāgi sarbe karibe raṇa.
Pākistāna rājyagoṭi chhārakhāra heba, chāriāḍe hāhākāra shubhuṇa je thiba.
Chīna Pākistāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa, Ruṣiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmana Jāpāna.
Pākistāna rājya goṭā chhārakhāra heba, pachha beḷaku ta puṇi buddhi je sphuriba.

Macne:
"Waddan lagu magacaabo Pakistan ayaa si lama filaan ah u soo bixi doona. Dhammaadka waqtiga, Hindiya waxay la kulmi doontaa dagaal ba'an oo ay la gasho Pakistan. Quwadaha Bariga Dhexe iyo Galbeedka ayaa isku xirmi doona; waddamada Muslimka ah, waddamada Galbeedka, Shiinaha, iyo Ameerika ayaa taageeri doona Pakistan, halka Ruushka, Jarmalka, iyo Jabaan ay caawin doonaan Hindiya. Pakistan si buuxda ayay u burburi doontaa (Chara-khara) dagaalkan, waxaana dhexda quus guud, ay ugu dambayn garan doontaa khaladaadkeeda."

Waxaa xusid mudan in ereyga 'Pakistan' la sameeyay oo kaliya 1933-kii, haddana awliyadan waxay magacaabeen qarniyo ka hor.

3.9 Wax-sheegid ku saabsan Magaalada Bhubaneswar

Aasaaska Bhubaneswar-ta casriga ah waxaa la dhigay 1948-kii Ra'iisul Wasaare Nehru. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, xusiddeeda caasimad ahaan waxay ka muuqataa qoraallada Malika sida 'Chakada Madana' iyo 'Kali Agata Bhabishyant'.

Odia Sloka:

ଭୈରବୀ ଡାକିବ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର, ଚୈତ୍ର ମାସ ଯେ ମଙ୍ଗଳବାର।
ପୃଥିବୀରେ ଗୋଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଆରମ୍ଭିବ ଯେବେ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସୈନ୍ୟ ମାଡ଼ି ଯେ ଆସିବେ।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ, ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ତ ପୁଣି ରାଜଧାନୀ ଜାଣ, ବିଦେଶୀ ଶତ୍ରୁଙ୍କ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି ପଡ଼ିବଟି ପୁଣ।
ଭୈରବ ଡାକ ହେବ ଜାଣ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରଟି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhairabī ḍākiba Bhubaneshwar, Chaitra māsa je Maṅgaḷabāra.
Pṛuthibīre goḷa yuddha ārambhiba jebe, Bhubaneshwaraku sainya māḍi je āsibe.
Bhubaneshwarare lāgiba goḷa, Ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Bhubaneshwar ta puṇi rājadhānī jāṇa, bideshī shatruṅka dṛuṣṭi paḍibaṭi puṇa.
Bhairaba ḍāka heba jāṇa, Bhubaneshwarati pramāṇa.

Macne:
Awliyadu waxay saadaaliyeen in Talaado bisha Chaitra ah, dhawaaq ba'an (guuxa Bhairavi) laga maqli doono Bhubaneswar. Intii lagu jiray Dagaalkii Saddexaad ee Adduunka, ciidamada cadawga waxay gaari doonaan Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Bana). Qoraallada waxay si cad u xuseen in Bhubaneswar ay noqon doonto caasimadda iyo bartilmaameed u ah cadawga shisheeye.

3.10 Wax-sheegid ku saabsan Macallinka Ruuxiga ah ee caanka ah Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, qoraalladiisa Malika ee la qoray ku dhawaad 600 oo sano ka hor, wuxuu saadaaliyay dhalashada "Yugapurusha" (Ninka Xilliga) kaasoo samayn doona ciyaaro rabbaani ah oo badan oo sumcaddiisu ay ku fidi doonto adduunka oo dhan.

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳାମାନ ହେବ କୀର୍ତ୍ତି ରହି ଯିବ, ଅନୁକୂଳ ନାମ ଧରି।
ଦେଶ ବିଦେଶରୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିବେ, ସତସଙ୍ଗ ଆଶ୍ରା କରି।
ସତନାମ ଗୋଟି ଉଚ୍ଚାରି ବୋଲିବେ, ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହେବ ମେଳ।
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ମାର୍ଗେ ରହି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦେଖିବ, ଯୁଗପୁରୁଷଙ୍କ ଖେଳ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Līḷāmāna heba kīrtti rahi jiba, Anukūḷa nāma dhari.
Desha bidesharu bhakata āsibe, sat-saṅga āshrā kari.
Satanāma goṭi uchchāri bolibe, bhaktaṅka heba meḷa.
Shūnya mārge rahi Achyuta dekhiba, Yugapuruṣaṅka kheḷa.

Macne:
Mahapurush-ku wuxuu qoray in aw la yiraahdo "Anukul" uu soo bixi doono, cibaadooleyaal ka kala yimid Hindiya iyo dibaddaba ay u iman doonaan inay magan doonan "Satsanga"-diisa. Magaca quduuska ah (Satnaam) ayaa la akhrin doono, waxaana jiri doona shir weyn oo cibaadooleyaal ah. Achyutananda Das wuxuu intaa ku daray in isagu, isagoo ku sii nagaanaya booska qarsoon/faaruqa ah (Shunya Maarga), uu markhaati ka noqon doono ciyaarta rabbaaniga ah ee Yugapurusha-kan.

Wax-sheegiddan oo rumowday, Anukulchandra Chakravarty wuxuu dhashay Sebtembar 14, 1888, degmada Pabna ee Bengal aan la qaybin (hadda Bangladesh). Caalamka oo dhan waxaa looga yaqaannay Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra, wuxuu aasaasay 'Satsang Ashram' ee Deoghar, Jharkhand, sanadkii 1946. Malaayiin cibaadaysato ah oo adduunka oo dhan ah ayaa ku biiray hawshiisa, iyagoo inta badan ugu yeedha 'Yuga Purushottam.'

3.11 Xusidda Bamka Atomikada ee Qoraallada Malika

Halka bamka atomikada lagu sameeyay bartamihii qarnigii 20aad, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das (ee 'Gupta Kheda Malika' iyo 'Bhavishyat Malika') iyo Balarama Das (ee 'Kali Tandaba Gita') waxay xuseen qarniyo ka hor.

Odia Sloka:

ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା କରିଦେବେଟି ଜମା, ପୋଡ଼ିଜାଳି ଦେବା ପାଇଁ ଭାରତ ସୀମା।
ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା ଯାହା ଲାଗି ଗାରିମା, ଦେଖାଇ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ଆଜି ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ସେନା।
ତାହା ଫୁଟିବ ନାହିଁ କେଣେ ଯିବ ମିଳାଇ, ଏହା ଦେଖି ବିଦେଶୀଏ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।
ଅଗ୍ନିର ଦାହିକା ଶକ୍ତି ଲୋ ବଉଳ ପ୍ରଭୁ କରିବେ ହରଣ, ପରମାଣୁ ବୋମା ଯାହାଲାଗି ଗାରିମା ଶୀତଳ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Paramāṇu je bomā karidebeṭi jamā, poḍijāḷi debā pāiñ Bhārata sīmā.
Paramāṇu je bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā, dekhāi huanti āji pāshchātya senā.
Tāhā phuṭiba nāhiñ keṇe jiba miḷāi, ehā dekhi bideshīe jibe paḷāi.
Agnira dāhikā shakti lo bauḷa Prabhu karibe haraṇa, paramāṇu bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā shītaḷa hoiba jāṇa.

Macne:
Awliyadu waxay qoreen in inta lagu guda jiro Dagaalkii Saddexaad ee Adduunka, cadowga Hindiya ay qorshayn doonaan inay burburiyaan xuduudaha Hindiya iyagoo isticmaalaya Bamyada Atomikada (Paramanu Bomba). Si kastaba ha ahaatee, qorshahoodu wuu fashilmi doonaa sababtoo ah Sayid Kalki wuxuu nuugi doonaa awoodda gubaysa/burburinaysa (Dahika Shakti) ee bambooyinkan. Hubka ay ciidamada reer Galbeedka iyo cadowgu ku faanaan ayaa noqon doona "qabow" (aan shaqaynayn) oo wax saameyn ah kuma yeelan doonaan dhulka Hindiya. Markay arkaan ficilkan aan suurtogalka ahayn, cadowga shisheeye ayay carari doonaan iyagoo cabsanaya. Qoraalladu waxay digniin ka bixinayaan in dagaal nukliyeer ah oo cabsi badan (Anu-Paramanu Yuddha) uu ugu dambayn dhici doono, ka dhigaya Dhulka mid ku dhow inaan la degganaan karin, kaas oo cibaadaysatadu ay goobjoog ka noqon doonaan.

3.12 Xusidda Madan Mohan Malaviya, Mahatma Gandhi, iyo Motilal Nehru

Wuxuu dhashay 1850, awliyada weyn Bhima Bhoi (raacsan dhaqanka Panchasakhas) wuxuu faahfaahin ka bixiyay hoggaamiyeyaasha dhaqdhaqaaqa xorriyadda Hindiya ee qoraalkiisa 'Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ମଦନମାଲବ୍ୟ ନାମ ବୋଲିକରି ଏକ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବ ଜନ୍ମି,
ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ସହିତରେ ଅହିଂସା ଧର୍ମରେ ନିରନ୍ତରେ ଥିବେ ପୁଣି।
ଚୁଡ଼ଙ୍ଗ ବଂଶରେ ଉତପତ୍ତି ହୋଇ ମୋତିଲାଲ ଯେ ନେହେରୁ,
ମହାପ୍ରତାପୀ ଅଟଇ ସେହୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିଛନ୍ତି ଶୂନ୍ୟପୁରୁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Madanamālabya nāma bolikari eka bhakta thiba janmi,
Gāndhī sahitare ahinsā dharmare nirantare thibe puṇi.
Chuḍaṅga baṃshare utpatti hoi Motilāl je Neheru,
mahāpratāpī aṭai sehu bhakta āsichanti Shūnyapuru.

Macne:
Qoraalku wuxuu saadaaliyay dhalashada cibaadaysato lagu magacaabo "Madan Mohan Malaviya" kaas oo, oo ay weheliyaan "Mahatma Gandhi," hoggaamin doona dhaqdhaqaaq ku salaysan diinta aan rabshadaha lahayn (Ahimsa). Intaa waxaa dheer, wuxuu xusay dhalashada cibaadaysato aad u saameyn badan oo awood leh "Motilal Nehru." Saddexdooduba waxay noqdeen hoggaamiyeyaal muhiim ah oo ka tirsan Shirweynaha Qaranka Hindiya iyo halganka xorriyadda.

3.13 Wax-sheegidda Dilka Mahatma Gandhi ee Nathuram Godse

Ee 'Jaiphula Malika', Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu saadaaliyay dhimashada Mahatma-ha.

Odia Sloka:

ମୋହନ ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ମରଣ, ନାଥୁରାମ ହସ୍ତେ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏ କଥା ସିଦ୍ଧ ଫଳ ରେ ନିପୁଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mohana Gāndhī maraṇa, Nāthurāma haste hoiba jāṇa lo jāiphula,
e kathā siddha phaḷa re nipuṇa.

Macne:
"Dhimashada Mohan Gandhi waxay ku dhici doontaa gacmaha Nathuram." Sida taariikhdu diiwaan gelisay, Janaayo 30, 1948, Nathuram Vinayak Godse wuxuu dilay Mahatma Gandhi.

3.14 Madaxbannaanida Hindiya Hoggaaminta Mahatma Gandhi hoosteeda

Ee 'Kali Bhagabata', Mahapurush Abhiram Paramahansa (awliyo qarnigii 20aad ee dhaqanka Malika) wuxuu sharaxay isbeddelka siyaasadeed.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟାଗ୍ରହୀ ହୋଇଲେ ମେଳି ।
ଅନ୍ତର ହୋଇଯିବ କଳି ।।
ଇଉରୋପିଆନ ଯିବେ ଛାଡ଼ି ।
ମୋହନ ସେନା ଦେବେ କାଢ଼ି ।।
ପ୍ରଥମ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ସେନାପତି ।
ହେବେ ମୋହନ ସୈନ୍ୟ ଘେତି ।।
ମୋହନ ବୋଲି ଯାଙ୍କୁ କହି ।
ମହତ ଆତ୍ମା ଅଟେ ସେହି ।।
ସେହି ନାରଦ ମହାମୁନ୍ୟ ।
ତାଙ୍କୁ କରନ୍ତି ସର୍ବେ ମାନ୍ୟ ।।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Satyāgrahī hoile meḷi,
antara hoijiba kaḷi.
Iuropiāna jibe chhāḍi,
Mohana senā debe kāḍhi.
Prathama yuddhe senāpati,
hebe Mohana sainya gheti.
Mohana boli jāṅku kahi,
mahata ātmā aṭe sehi.
Sehi Nārada mahāmunya,
tāṅku karanti sarbe mānya.

Macne:
Awliyadu wuxuu saadaaliyay in iyada oo loo marayo isu-ururinta Satyagrahis-ka, reer Yurub lagu qasbi doono inay baxaan. Wuxuu aqoonsaday "Mohan" (Gandhi) inuu yahay taliyaha/hoggaamiyaha halgankii ugu horreeyay ee weynaa ee madaxbannaanida. Wuxuu sidoo kale ku tilmaamay Mahatma Gandhi inuu yahay naf weyn (Mahatma) iyo muuqasho Narada ah, erayadiisana ay dhammaan xurmayn doonaan. Tani waxay rumowday maadaama hoggaaminta Gandhi ay noqotay xoogga qeexaya ee keentay in Ingiriisku ka baxo dhulka Hindiya.

4 Xilliga Kaliyuga

Sida ku cad qoraallada Sanatan, cimriga guud ee Kaliyuga waa 432,000 oo sano. Bhavishya Malika waxay sidoo kale xaqiijinaysaa muddadan guud, laakiin waxay ku daraysaa wax-sheegid muhiim ah: dembiyada xad-dhaafka ah ee aadanaha awgeed, Kaliyuga ma dhammaystiri doonto muddadeeda oo dhan.

4.1 Waajibaadka Qoraalka ee 432,000 oo Sannadood

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray 'Adi Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିରେ ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯାଏ, ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନିର୍ଣ୍ଣୟ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷire chāri lakṣa jāe, batiśa sahasra nirṇaya.

Macne:
"Kaliyuga dhexdeeda, afar boqol iyo soddon iyo laba kun (432,000) oo sano ayaa loo go'aamiyay cimrigeeda."

Kadibna Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa, 'Dashapatala Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଭୋଗ ପ୍ରମାଣ ସାର,
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ହଜାର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaliyuga bhoga pramāṇa sāra,
chārilakṣha je batisha hajāra.

Macne:
Taasi waa, cimriga loo qoray ee Kali Yuga waa afar boqol iyo soddon iyo laba kun oo sano.

Wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶି ସହସ୍ର କଳି,
କରିଲି ତିନି ଯୁଗଠାରୁ ବଳି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chārilakṣha batishi sahasra kali,
karili tini yugaṭhāru bali.

Macne:
Taasi waa, cimriga Kali Yuga waa afar boqol iyo soddon iyo laba kun oo sano.

Si kastaba ha ahaatee, Mahapurushas-ku kuma joogsan inay tan oo kaliya yiraahdaan; waxay sidoo kale ku sheegeen qoraalladooda Malika in dembiyada kala duwan ee ay aadanuhu galeen awgeed, Kali Yuga ma awoodi doonto inay dhammaystirto muddadeeda oo dhan.

4.2 Sababta Kaliyuga ay u dhammaan doonto goor hore

Panchasakhas-ku waxay caddaynayaan in halka cimriga aragtida ah uu yahay 432,000 oo sano, "Bhoga" (muddada dhabta ah ee la waayo-aragnimo) ay aad uga gaaban doonto culayska dembiyada awgeed. Ee 'Dashapatala Malika', waxaa ku qoran:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର, ଭୋଗ ନୋହିବ କହ ପୀତବାସ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra, bhoga nohiba kaha Pītabāsa.

Macne:
"Sayid Pitabasa (Jagannath)! Noo sheeg sida Kaliyuga ay u awoodi doonin inay dhammaystirto muddadeeda oo dhan ee 432,000 oo sano."

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Kalikalpa Gita':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ବର୍ଷ ଆଉ,
ଭୋଗ ନହେବ ପାପଭାରା ଯହୁଁ।
ଆପଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଉଦିତ,
ପାପୀମାନଙ୍କୁ କରିବେ ମୁକତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chāri lakṣha batisha sasra barṣha āu,
bhoga naheba pāpabhārā jahuṃ.
Āpaṇe prabhu hoibe udita,
pāpīmānaṃku karibe mukata.

Macne:
Taasi waa, culayska xad-dhaafka ah ee dembiyada ee Kali Yuga awgeed, Kali Yuga ma awoodi doonto inay dhammaystirto cimrigeeda 4,32,000 oo sano. Xitaa ka hor muddadan, Sayidku wuu soo muuqan doonaa, dembiilayaasha waa la burburin doonaa, Dharma-na waa la dhisi doonaa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ,
ପାପ ଯୋଗୁଁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kali chāri lakṣha batisha sasra nohiba bhoga,
pāpa joguṃ kṣhaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Kali Yuga ma awoodi doonto inay dhammaystirto muddadeeda loo qoray ee 4,32,000 oo sano. Dembiyada awgeed, cimrigeedu wuu burburi doonaa, macnaheedu waa, si buuxda looma waayo-aragnimo doono.

4.3 Haddii aanay ahayn 4,32,000 oo sano, markaa waa maxay cimriga la waayo-aragnimo ee Kali Yuga?

Maanta, dadka qaar waxay ku hawlan yihiin faafinta, iyagoo soo xiganaya Malika, in cimriga la waayo-aragnimo ee Kali Yuga uu yahay 10,000 oo sano. Ha la ogaado in tani ay gebi ahaanba khaldan tahay.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶ ସହସ୍ରରେ କଳିନାଶ ହେବ ଏ ଅଟେ ବିଷମ ବାଣୀ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dasha sahasrare kalināsha heba e aṭe biṣhama bāṇī.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Kali Yuga waxay ku dhammaan doontaa 10,000 oo sano – tani waa hadal aan isku waafaqsanayn ama been ah.

Panchasakha Mahapurushas-ku waxay ku qoreen meelo badan oo qoraalladooda Malika ah in cimriga la waayo-aragnimo ee Kali Yuga uu noqon doono 5,000 oo sano, ka dib markaas Sayid Kalki wuu soo muuqan doonaa oo Satya-Kali ama Ananta Yuga ama Adya Satya Yuga ayaa iman doona.

Kaalay, aan helno macluumaad dheeraad ah mawduucan mid mid iyada oo loo marayo slokas-ka Malika.

Ugu horrayn, aan ogaanno waxa Sayid Shri Krishna laftiisu yiri ku saabsan cimriga Kaliyuga.

4.4 Muddada Dhabta ah: 5,000 oo Sannadood

Si ka duwan macluumaadka khaldan ee ay faafiyeen qaar ka mid ah in Kaliyuga ay socoto 10,000 oo sano, qoraallada Malika waxay si isdaba joog ah u sheegaan in xukunka dhabta ah ee Kali uu dhammaanayo ka dib 5,000 oo sano, waxaana ku xiga xilli kala guur ah.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu soo xiganayaa ereyadii Sayid Krishna uu u sheegay Garuda ee 'Agata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ, ପାପ ଯୋଗୁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେ ପଚାଶ ଭୋଗ ହେବ ନିକର, ଏଥୁ କିଛି ଉଣା ହୋଇବ ଶୁଣ ଗରୁଡ଼ ବୀର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷi chāri lakṣa batiśa sahasra nohiba bhoga, pāpa jogu kṣaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.
Pāñcha sahasra je pachāsha bhoga heba nikara, ethu kichi uṇā hoiba shuṇa Garuḍa bīra.

Macne:
"Adoo Garuda geesi ah! Dhegeyso, Kaliyuga ma gaari doono 432,000 oo sano. Dembiga awgiis, muddadiisu way yaraan doontaa. Waxay ku dhammaan doontaa agagaarka 5,050 sano.

4.5 Tusaalaha Ravana

Odia Sloka:

ରାମଦାସ ବୋଲଇ ହୋ ଶୁଣ ଗୁରୁଦେବ, କଳିଯୁଗ କେତେ ପୁଣ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ଯେ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ, କେମନ୍ତେ ଯେ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ହୋଇବଟି ଶେଷ।
ଏଡ଼େ ବଡ଼ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ସଂଶୟ ଫେଡ଼ିଣ ମୋତେ କହ ଗୁରୁଦେବ।
ରାମଦାସ ବାଣୀ ଶୁଣି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହନ୍ତି, ତୋତେ କହୁଅଛି ରାମ ଶୁଣ ଦେଇ ମତି।
ରାବଣ ଯେ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ ତପ କଲା, ଛପନ ଗଣ୍ଡା ଯୁଗକୁ ବର ସେ ପାଇଲା।
ପାପେ ଗଲା ତାର ତିନି ଭାଗ ଯେ ବିନାଶ, ଏକଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସ୍ୱର୍ଗେ ପଶି ଅପସରୀ ମାନଙ୍କୁ ହରିଲା, ପର ଦ୍ରବ୍ୟ ହରି ଯେବେ ପାଦେ ଖଟାଇଲା।
ଆପେ ଜଗତମାତା ଯେ ଜନକ ନନ୍ଦିନୀ, ତାଙ୍କୁ ହରି ନେଲା ଗର୍ବେ ମୂଢ଼ ବିଂଶପାଣି।
ସୁରାପାନ କଲା ସେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିଙ୍କୁ ବଧିଲା, ବ୍ରହ୍ମହତ୍ୟା ଆଦିକରି କେତେ ପାପ କଲା।
ପାପରୁ ଯେ ତିନି ଭାଗ ହୋଇଲା ବିନାଶ, ଏକ ଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସେହିପରି କଳିଯୁଗ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ପାଞ୍ଚସସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅନ୍ୟ ପାପେ ଯିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Rāmadāsa bolai ho shuṇa gurudeba, kaliyuga kete puṇa bhoga je hoiba.
Chārilakṣha batisha je sahasra baraṣha, kemaṃte je yuga puṇa hoibaṭi sheṣha.
Eḍe baḍa yuga puṇa bināsha hoiba, saṃshaya pheriṇa mote kaha gurudeba.
Rāmadāsa bāṇī shuṇi Achyuta kahaṃti, tote kahuachi Rāma shuṇa dei mati.
Rābaṇa je tretayā yugare tapa kalā, chapana gaṇḍā yugaku bara se pāilā.
Pāpe galā tāra tini bhāga je vināsha, ekabhāga bhoga kalā Bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Swarge pashi apasarī mānaṃku harilā, para drabya hari jebe pāde khaṭāilā.
Āpe jagatamātā je Janaka naṃdinī, tāṃku hari nelā garve mūḍha biṃshapāṇi.
Surāpāna kalā se kṣhatriṃṃku badhilā, brahmahatyā ādi kari kete pāpa kalā.
Pāparu je tini bhāga hoilā bināsha, eka bhāga bhoga kalā bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Sehipari kaliyuga bināsha hoiba, pāṃchasasra bhoga heba anya pāpe jiba.

Gudaha 'Kalpatika', Achyutananda Das wuxuu sharxayaa ifafaalahan isagoo isticmaalaya tusaalaha Ravana. Ravana wuxuu sameeyay cibaado weyn wuxuuna kasbaday cimri dhan "56 Ganda" (muddo cosmic ah oo aad u weyn). Si kastaba ha ahaatee, sababtoo ah wuxuu afduubtay Hooyo Sita, wuxuu caayay nimfooyinka jannada, wuxuuna galay dembiyo xunxun sida Brahman-hatya, wuxuu kaliya ku raaxaystay hal qayb oo ka mid ah nimcadiisa, halka saddexda qaybood ee kale ay dembiyadiisu burburiyeen.

Sidoo kale, muddada Kaliyuga ee 432,000 oo sano waxaa "cunaya" kororka degdegga ah ee dembiga caalamiga ah, taasoo keeneysa inuu si degdeg ah u dhammaado ka dib 5,000 oo sano oo waayo-aragnimo ah, taasoo u gogol xaaraysa Ananta Yuga ama Satya Yuga.

4.6 Da'da Kaliyuga: Falanqayn Faahfaahsan

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗର ଅଟଇ ଆୟୁଷ।
ପାପଭାରରେ ଆୟୁ କଟିଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra,
Kaḷiyugara aṭai āyuṣa.
Pāpabhārare āyu kaṭijiba,
Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi bhoga hoiba.

Macne:
Micnaha, da'da Kaliyuga waa afar boqol iyo soddon iyo laba kun (432,000) oo sano, laakiin saamaynta dembiyada awgeed, da'dan waa la soo gaabin doonaa waxayna ku dhammaan doontaa shan kun (5,000) oo sano oo kaliya.

Muuqasho ka mid ah Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, oo dhashay qarnigii 18aad ee degmada Jajpur ee Odisha, Aw Mahapurush Hadi Das wuxuu ka hadlay da'da Kaliyuga qoraalkiisa Malika ee la yiraahdo ‘Kali Chautisha’. Wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ସଂବତ୍ସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗ ଆଦ୍ୟ ହୋଇବ ଶୁଭଯୋଗେ ପ୍ରବେଶ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Saṃbatsara pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hoiba sheṣa,
Satya yuga ādya hoiba shubhajoge prabesha.

Macne:
Micnaha, "Sambatsara Paancha Sahasra" (5,000 sano) gudaheeda, Kaliyuga wuu dhammaan doonaa, ka dibna, Adya Satya Yuga wuxuu bilaaban doonaa waqti barakeysan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraal kale oo Malika ah:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ଯେ ହଜାର ଥାରୁ ମହାଭୟ ହେବ,
ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସମ୍ରାଟକୁ ଆସି ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଗାନ୍ଧାର ସେନାଟି ଗୋଳ କରିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର ପଚାଶ କଳିର ଆୟୁଷ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Pāñcha je hajāra thāru mahābhaya heba,
Dillī samrāṭaku āsi bipada paḍiba.
Gāndhāra senāṭi goḷa kariba abashya,
Pāñcha hajāra pachāsha Kaḷira āyuṣa.

Macne:
Mahapurush halkan sidoo kale wuxuu ku qoray in ka dib 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah, waxaa jiri doona jawi cabsi weyn leh; masiibooyin ayaa ku dhici doona Boqorka Delhi ama Ra'iisul Wasaaraha dalka. Ciidanka Gandhara (Taliban) waxay hubaal ah inay dagaal ku qaadi doonaan Hindiya. Mahapurush wuxuu intaa ku daray inuu qoray in waxaas oo dhan ay dhici doonaan ka dib dhammaadka Kaliyuga, taasoo ah, ka dib marka ay dhaafaan 5,050 sano oo Kaliyuga ah.

Achyutananda Das ji sidoo kale wuxuu qoray aayad ku jirta qoraalkiisa Malika halkaas oo uusan si toos ah u sheegin da'da Kaliyuga inay tahay 5,000 ama 5,050, laakiin wuxuu si dadban ugu sheegay tilmaamo:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶେ ମସିହା ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କରିବେ କଳି ବିନାଶ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Biṃshe masiha hoiba sheṣa,
Ananta karibe Kaḷi bināsha.

Macne:
Micnaha, '20-aadkii'—laga bilaabo sanadka laba kun (2000) ilaa laba kun sagaashan iyo sagaal (2099)—mar uun inta lagu jiro wakhtigan, Kaliyuga wuu dhammaan doonaa. Sayid Ananta wuxuu burburin doonaa Kaliyuga dhulka.

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, wuxuu si faahfaahsan u sharxay da'da Kaliyuga qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Aagata Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବୋଲଇ ଶୁଣିମା ଗୋସାଇଁ କୁହ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବିଚାର,
କେତେବେଳେ କଳ୍କୀ ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ଶୁଣଇଁ ମୁଖୁ ତୁମ୍ଭର।
ଶିଶୁ ବୋଲନ୍ତି ହେ ଶୁଣିମା ବାରଙ୍ଗ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ତୁହି,
ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଯେବେ ଯିବ ଭୋଗ ହୋଇ।
ଜେସେନେକେ ନିଶି ପାହିଲେ ପ୍ରଭାତ ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ଏହା ଜାଣ,
ଯେମନ୍ତ ସମୟେ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ସେ ନାରାୟଣ।
ସମକ୍ଷର ବତା ଶୁଣି ଆଣିକରି ପ୍ରମାଣ ଏହାକୁ କର,
ସବୁ ଏକଠାବେ ମିଶାଇ କରିଣ କରିବୁ ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର।
ଏହି ସମୟକୁ ତଳେ କରିଥିବୁ କହଇ ହୋ ବାବୁ ତୋତେ,
ଠିକ ରେ ଏ କଥା ଦେଖାଇ କହିଲୁ ରଖିଥିବୁ ହୃଦଗତେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bāraṅga bolai shuṇimā gosāiṃ kuha bhabiṣya bichāra,
ketebeḷe Kalkī swarūpa hoibe shuṇaiṃ mukhu tumbhara.
Shishu bolanti he shuṇimā Bāraṅga Kaḷaṅki swarūpa tuhi,
yugashandhi pāñcha sahasra baraṣa jebe jiba bhoga hoi.
Jeseneke nishi pāhile prabhāta yugashandhi ehā jāṇa,
jemanta samaye Kaḷaṅki swarūpa hoibe se Nārāyaṇa.
Samakṣara batā shuṇi āṇikari pramāṇa ehāku kara,
sabu ekaṭhābe mishāi kariṇa karibu pāñcha hajāra.
Ehi samayaku taḷe karithibu kahai ho bābu tote,
Ṭhika re e kathā dekhāi kahilu rakhithibu hṛudagate.

Macne:
Xerta Baranga Das wuxuu ku yiri Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das—"Adoo Guru Dev, ii sheeg mustaqbalka. Waxaan rabaa inaan kaa maqlo goorta Sayidku uu ku muuqan doono qaabkiisa Kalki."

Markuu taas maqlay, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wuxuu u sheegay xertiisii ugu sarreysay Baranga in marka 'Yuga-Sandhi' (kala guurka da'daha) uu dhaco, markaas kaliya ayuu Sayidku qaadan doonaa muuqashadiisa Kalki. Isagoo sii caddeynaya, wuxuu yiri in Yuga-Sandhi-gan uu dhici doono marka Kaliyuga la soo maray 5,000 oo sano. Sida subaxdu u timaado habeen ka dib, da' kale ayaa timaada da' ka dib. Qaybta dhexe ee u dhaxaysa laba da'ood waxaa la yiraahdaa Yuga-Sandhi. Oo Yuga-Sandhi-gan dhexdiisa, Narayana wuxuu qaadan doonaa qaabkiisa Kalki. Yuga-Sandhi-gan wuxuu imaan doonaa ka dib marka ay dhaafaan 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah.

Achyutananda ji, isagoo sheegaya da'da Kaliyuga qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kaliyuga Malika’, wuxuu yiri:

Odia Sloka:

ଚହଟିବ ଲୀଳା ତୁ ଚାରିରେ ମିଶା ଏକ,
ଚଢ଼ା ତିନି ଶୂନ ତହିଁ ଯେତେ ହେଲା ଠିକ।
ଚଳିଯିବ ଘୋର କଳି ଦଳି ଦେବେ ମିଳି,
ଚେତାଇ ଗୀତ ଛନ୍ଦଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ସେ ଭାଳି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

chahaṭiba līḷā tu chārire mishā eka,
chaḍhā tini shūna tahiñ jete helā ṭhika.
Chalijiba ghora Kaḷi daḷi debe miḷi,
chetāi gīta chhandai Achyuta se bhāḷi.

Macne:
Micnaha, ku dar mid lambarka afar (4+1=5) oo saddex eber dhig wadarta agteeda; lambarka la helay wuxuu matalayaa wakhtiga ka dib marka ay dhammaan doonto muddada Kaliyuga ee cabsida leh. Aayadda kore, Mahapurush wuxuu si astaan ah u sheegayaa da'da Kaliyuga. Wuxuu yiri 4+1=5, ka dibna saddex eber oo la dhigo 5 agteeda waxay la macno tahay in ka dib marka ay dhaafaan 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah, da'daas ay dhammaan doonto.

Qoraal kale oo Malika ah ‘Bhavishya Chautisha’, wuxuu ku yiri xertiisii Ramachandra:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅମରପୁର,
ଠାକୁର ତହିଁରୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ, ଠାରି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଧର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ṭhikaṇā Amarapura,
Ṭhākura tahiñru hebe bāhāra, Rāmachandra he,
ṭhāri pāñcha sahasra dhara.

Macne:
Micnaha, Adoo xerta Ramachandra, muuqashada Sayid Kalki waxay ka dhici doontaa Amarapur (gobolka aan dhimanayn—oo jiray xitaa ka hor Satya Yuga, taasoo ah, Jajpur), tanina waxay dhici doontaa ka dib marka ay dhaafaan 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah.

Kadibna, qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kali Bayalisha’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ka sheekeeyaa da'da Kaliyuga calaamado. Mahapurush wuxuu yiri:
Micnaha, qor lambarka 3 korka tirada wiilasha Boqor Dasharatha uu lahaa (taasoo ah, 4^3 = 64). Mahapurush wuxuu markaa yiri in la rogo oo laga jaro 11 lambarkaas (Rogidda 64 waxay bixisaa 46, iyo 46 - 11 = 35). Mahapurush wuxuu yiri in la isku dhufto lambarka dhinaca midig ee lambarkan la helay 'Gandiva' (taasoo ah, 1000) wuxuuna qoray in Kaliyuga uu ku dhammaan doono lambarka la helay ama 5,000 oo sano.
Fiiro gaar ah: Halkan, 'Gandiva', Mahapurush wuxuu ula jeedaa masaafada ay gaarto, taasoo ahayd 1,000 Yojanas.

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶରଥ ସୁତ ମୁଣ୍ଡରେ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କ ଚଢ଼ିବ,
ବଜ୍ର ଅଙ୍କ ଦେଇ ଏଗାର ଅଙ୍କ ତହୁଁ ଫେରିବ।
ଶେଷ ଡାହାଣ ଭାଗ ଯାହା ହେଲା ଗାଣ୍ଡିବ ଗୁଣ,
କଳି ଶେଷ ଭୋଗ ଏହିଟି କହିଲି ଅରଜୁନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dasharatha suta muṇḍare tini aṅka chaḍhiba,
bajra aṅka dei egāra aṅka tahuṃ pheriba.
Sheṣa ḍāhāṇa bhāga jāhā helā gāṇḍiba guṇa,
kaḷi sheṣa bhoga ehiṭi kahili Arajuna.

Macne:
Macnaha, qor lambarka 3 korkiisa tirada wiilasha uu lahaa Boqor Dasharatha (taas oo ah, 4^3 = 64). Mahapurush wuxuu markaa leeyahay in la rogo oo laga jaro 11 lambarkaas (Rogidda 64 waxay bixisaa 46, iyo 46 - 11 = 35). Mahapurush wuxuu leeyahay in la isku dhufto nambarka dhinaca midig ee lambarkan la helay 'Gandiva' (taas oo ah, 1000) oo wuxuu qorayaa in Kaliyuga ay ku dhammaan doonto lambarka la helay ama 5,000 oo sano.
Fiiro gaar ah: Halkan, 'Gandiva' ahaan, Mahapurush wuxuu ula jeedaa masaafada ay gaarto, taas oo ahayd 1,000 Yojanas.

Achyutananda Das ji wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Adi Sanhita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ ଯେ ସୁବାହୁ ଯୁଗ କଳି,
କ୍ଷୀଣ ଆୟୁଷ ମହାବଳି।
ପାପେ ସକଳ କ୍ଷୟ ଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

E je subāhu yuga kaḷi,
kṣīṇa āyuṣa mahābaḷi.
pāpe sakaḷa kṣaya jiba,
pāñcha sahasra bhoga heba.

Macne:
Micnaha, da'da 432,000 oo sano ee Kaliyuga waxay ku soo koobmi doontaa 5,000 oo sano oo kaliya dembiyada awgeed.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଭୋଗ ହେବ,
ସେହି ଦିନୁ ସତ୍ୟକଳି ପ୍ରକାଶ ପାଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷi pāñchasahasra baraṣa bhoga heba,
sehi dinu Satyakali prakāsha pāiba.

Macne:
Micnaha, waayo-aragnimada Kaliyuga waxay noqon doontaa 5,000 oo sano. Ka dib, 'Satyakali'—iftiinka Adya Satya Yuga ee Kaliyuga dhexdiisa—wuu muuqan doonaa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାରରେ ଗଙ୍ଗାଦେବୀ ମେଳ କଟକ ଠାରେ,
ଭକ୍ତ ଦେଖିବେ ନେତ୍ରରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷi pāñchahajārare Gaṅgādebī meḷa Kaṭaka ṭhāre,
bhakta dekhibe netrare.

Macne:
Micnaha, ka dib marka ay dhaafaan 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah, Ilaahadda Ganga waxay mari doontaa Cuttack; taasoo la macno ah, wax kasta oo u dhexeeya Kolkata iyo Cuttack way quusi doonaan; waxaa jiri doona daad cabsi leh.

Mahapurush wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହଟେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ,
ମୋର ତଥ୍ୟମାନ ସେତେବେଳେଟି ହୋଇବ।
ପୂର୍ବ ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଆବର ଦକ୍ଷିଣ,
ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେବେ କଳଙ୍କି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hāte satya upujiba,
mora tathyamāna setebeḷeṭi hoiba.
pūrba pashchima uttara ābara dakṣiṇa,
prakāsha hebe Kaḷaṅki Achyuta bachana.

Macne:
Micnaha, ka dib marka ay dhaafaan 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah, Kaliyuga wuu dhammaan doonaa oo Runta (Satya) ayaa imaan doonta. Kadibna, iftiinka Sayid Kalki wuxuu ka muuqan doonaa dhammaan jihooyinka—Bari, Galbeed, Waqooyi, iyo Koonfur.

Aw Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ସପ୍ତ ବିଂଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଆୟୁଷ ହେବ ବିନାଶ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରଟି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ,
ସତ୍ୟଯୁଗ ପୁଣି ଉଦୟା ହେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chāri lakṣa sapta biṃsha sahasra,
Kaḷiyuga āyuṣa heba bināsha.
pāñcha sahasraṭi bhoga hoiba,
Satyayuga puṇi udayā heba.

Macne:
Micnaha, 427,000 oo sano ayaa laga burburin doonaa da'da 432,000 oo sano ee Kaliyuga; kaliya 5,000 oo sano ayaa la soo mari doonaa, ka dibna Satya Yuga ayaa soo bixi doona.

Ma aha oo kaliya Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, laakiin mid kale oo ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, sidoo kale wuxuu ka hadlay da'da Kaliyuga qoraalladiisa kala duwan ee Malika. Isagoo u sharxaya xertiisii Baranga qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chumbaka Malika’, wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଏବେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ଠିକ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବିଚାର ଚିତ୍ତରେ ଘେନ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ebe pāñcha ṭhika kahibā shuṇa,
Bāraṅga bichāra chittare ghena.
pāñcha sahasra jetebeḷe heba,
sampūrṇa līḷā prakāsha hoiba.

Macne:
Micnaha, si taxaddar leh u dhegeyso xerta Baranga, qabo lambarka 5. Marka ay dhaafaan 5,000 oo sano, markaas kaliya ayaa muuqan doona falalka rabbaaniga ah (Leelas) ee Sayidka.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chumbaka Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଗଲେ,
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଜନମ ଯେ ହୋଇବେ ଭଲେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷiyuga pāñcha sahasra gale,
Viṣṇu janama je hoibe bhale.

Macne:
Micnaha, ka dib marka ay dhaafaan 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah, muuqashada Sayid Vishnu ayaa dhici doonta.

Isagoo sharxaya in Kaliyuga uu dhammaan doono ka dib marka ay dhaafaan shan kun oo sano iyo in ciyaaraha Sayidka ay dhici doonaan, Mahapurush Balarama Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’:

Odia Sloka:

ହସ୍ତିନା କଟକ ଯେବଣ ଭୂଇଁ,
ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସହର ସେହି।
ପୂର୍ବର ଚରିତ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ଦେବାପି ଅଛନ୍ତି ତପସ୍ୟାରେଣ।
ଉତ୍ତର ଖଣ୍ଡେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରନ୍ତି,
ତପସ୍ୟା ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ହେ ହୋଇ ନାହାନ୍ତି।
ପଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରେ ବରଷ ପୁଣ,
ତପସ୍ୟା ତାଙ୍କର ହୋଇବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ।
ବାରୁଣା କୁଳେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଭେଟିବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଶ୍ରୀହସ୍ତେ ସିରୀପା ବାନ୍ଧିବେ।
ଶ୍ରୀପଦ୍ମ ହସ୍ତ ତା ମୁଣ୍ଡେ ଲଦିବେ,
ହସ୍ତିନା ରାଜ୍ୟ ନଗ୍ରରେ ସ୍ଥାପିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Hastinā kaṭaka jebaṇa bhūiṃ,
bartamāna Dillī sahara sehi.
Pūrbara charita kahibā shuṇa,
Debāpi achhanti tapasyāreṇa.
Uttara khaṇḍe tapasyā karanti,
tapasyā pūrṇa he hoi nāhānti.
Pañcha sahasre baraṣa puṇa,
tapasyā tāṅkara hoiba pūrṇa.
Bāruṇā kuḷe prabhuṅku bheṭibe,
Prabhu shrīhaste sirīpā bāndhibe.
Shrīpadma hasta tā muṇḍe ladibe,
Hastinā rājya nagrare sthāpibe.

Macne:
Micnaha, dhulkii ahaa Hastinapur Cuttack waa magaalada Delhi ee maanta. Dhegeyso sheekadii hore—Devaapi wuxuu ku mashquulsan yahay cibaado.
