Wuxuu cibaado ku samaynayaa tuulada Kalapa ee gobolka Uttarakhand ee Hindiya, taasoo la dhammaystiri doono ka dib marka ay dhaafaan 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah. Ka dib marka cibaadadu dhammaato, wuxuu la kulmi doonaa Sayidka. Sayidku wuxuu taaj saari doonaa isaga gacmihiisa ubaxa ah wuxuuna ka dhigi doonaa Boqorka Hastinapur ama Delhi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray buuggiisa Malika ee ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦାରୁ ଚଢ଼ି ଦାରୁରୂପ ତେଜିକରି କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହୋଇବ,
ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ ମୋହର ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳିରେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଘଟିବ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dāru chaḍhi dārurūpa tejikari Kalkī rūpa hoiba,
niṣṭhā bachana mohara Rāmachandra Kaḷire nishche ghaṭiba.
āhuri kathāe shuṇa Rāmadāsa Kaḷi pāñcha sahasrare.

Macne:
Macnuhu wuxuu yahay, ka dib markii ay ka soo wareegtay 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah, Sayid Jagannatha wuxuu ka tagi doonaa qaabkiisa 'Daaru-rupa' ama qaabka Jagannatha wuxuuna qaadan doonaa qaabka Kalki.

Dhammaan aayadaha kor ku xusan, waxaa cad in da'da la soo maray ee Kaliyuga ay tahay kaliya 5,000 oo sano. Ma aha 432,000 oo sano mana aha 10,000 oo sano.

Laakiin su'aal muhiim ah ayaa halkan ka soo baxaysa— Sida aan ognahay, 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah ayaa horey u dhaafay waxaana hadda socda sanadkii 5,127-aad. Xisaabtan marka loo eego, Kaliyuga waa inuu dhammaaday hadda waxaana socda 'Adya Satya Yuga'! Laakiin saamaynta Kaliyuga ayaa weli ka muuqata meel walba. Dembi iyo xaqdarro ayaa meel walba ka jira. Maxay tahay iska hor imaadkan?

Jawaabtu waa sida soo socota— Marka hore, 5,000 oo sano waa hadal guud, ma aha mid si adag u sax ah. 5,000 oo sano macnaheedu waa qiyaastii 5,000 oo sano. Tani waa sababta meelo badan, 5,050 oo sano ayaa sidoo kale lagu qoray.

Marka labaad, sida ku cad Malika, muddada Kaliyuga ayaa asal ahaan dhammaatay waxaana hadda socda 'Sandhikala' (xilliga kala guurka) ee Kaliyuga iyo Satya Yuga. Sida ku cad Malika, waa inta lagu jiro Sandhikala in ay dhici doonaan dhacdooyinka dhalashada Sayid Kalki iyo aasaaska Dharma ee Isaga—taas oo ah, burburinta kuwa xun iyo badbaadinta cibaadaysatada. Tani waa sababta dembi iyo xaqdarro ay meesha ugu sarreysa marayaan inkasta oo muddada Kaliyuga ay dhammaatay. Waa in la ogaadaa in marka aasaaska Dharma la dhammeeyo, saamaynta Kaliyuga ay si buuxda u dhammaan doonto waxaana dib loo soo celin doonaa Runta, Dharma, iyo Caddaaladda.

Achyutananda Das wuxuu sidoo kale ku qoray buuggiisa Malika ee ku saabsan goorta aasaaskan Dharma uu dhici doono hoggaanka Sayid Kalki hoostiisa.

4.7 Isku xirnaanta Meena-Shani

Mahapurush wuxuu ku qoray buuggiisa ‘Aagata Bhavishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲେ,
ଠ’ ତିନି ବାମେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ରଖିଲେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ,
ଠକିଯିବ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭଲେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ṭhikaṇā Achyuta bole,
ṭha’ tini bāme pāñcha rakhile Rāmachandra he,
ṭhakijiba mīna shani bhale.

Macne:
Macnuhu wuxuu yahay, haddii aad dhigto saddex eber iyo lambarka 5 oo ka horreeya (5,000), waxay tilmaamaysaa in ka dib markii ay ka soo wareegtay 5,000 oo sano oo Kaliyuga ah, isku xirnaanta Meena-Shani (Saturn ee Pisces) ayaa dhici doonta, taas oo noqon doonta mid aad u muhiim ah.

5 Meena-Shani Yoga; Sida ku cad Malika

5.1 Waa maxay Meena-Shani?

Xiddigiska, Saturn (Shani) waxaa loo tixgeliyaa meeraha ugu gaabiska badan ee dhaqaaqa, isagoo ku sugnaanaya hal calaamad xiddigiska qiyaastii 2.5 sano. Waxaa jira wadar ahaan 12 calaamadood oo xiddigiska ah wareegga xiddigiska, Saturn-na wuxuu ku sugnaanayaa mid kasta qiyaastii 2.5 sano. Tani waxay ka dhigan tahay in Saturn ay qaadato qiyaastii 12 × 2.5 = 30 sano si ay u gudubto dhammaan wareegga xiddigiska.

Ka soo qaad in Saturn uu galo calaamad xiddigiska maanta; wuxuu ku soo laabanayaa calaamaddaas oo kale ka dib qiyaastii 30 sano ka dib markii uu dhammaystiray gudbinta buuxda. Meena-Shani macnaheedu waa gudbinta Saturn ee calaamadda xiddigiska ee Pisces (Meena).

Markii ugu dambaysay ee Saturn uu ka gudbay Pisces waxay ahayd 1995. Maadaama ay qaadato qiyaastii 30 sano in Saturn uu ku soo laabto calaamaddaas oo kale, 1995 + 30 = 2025. Sidaa darteed, sanadka 2025, Saturn wuxuu mar kale u gudbayaa Pisces. Ka dib 2025, gudbinta xigta waxay noqon doontaa 2025 + 30, taas oo ah 2054–2055.

Panchasakha Mahapurushas waxay si cad ugu sharaxeen buugagtooda Malika in burburinta kuwa xun, ilaalinta cibaadaysatada, iyo aasaaska Dharma ee Sayid Kalki ay ugu horrayn dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro xilliga Meena-Shani.

Si tan si fiican loo fahmo, aan eegno qaar ka mid ah aayadaha ku xusan Malika iyo sharraxaadooda.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray buuggiisa Malika ee ‘Prachi Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀମଣ୍ଡଳରେ ଚମକ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ନ ଜାଣିବେ ହୀନ ଜନ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀରେ ସଂହାର ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ର ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mahīmaṇḍaḷare chamaka paḍiba,
na jāṇibe hīna jana.
Mlechcha māruṇīre saṃhāra hoiba,
mīna shani ra pramāṇa.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, waxaa dhulka ka dhici doonta oohin; waxaa meel walba ka dhici doona fowdo. Qofna marka laga reebo cibaadaysatada ma awoodi doono inuu fahmo kuwan. Dhacdooyinkan, dad xun iyo dembiilayaal aan la tirin karin ayaa la burburin doonaa. Mahapurush wuxuu yiri in dhacdooyinkan waxay dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro muddada 2.5 sano ee Meena-Shani Yoga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray buuggiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦା ପ୍ରମାଣ, ଗୁପ୍ତରେ ଲାଗିବ ସେ ମାୟା ରଣ।
ଥୋକେ ଯିବେ କାମଚଣ୍ଡୀ ମୁଖରେ, ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଗୁପ୍ତରେ।
ତରକ ତେରୁଆ ଯେତେ ଭକତ, ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦାରେ ନାଶ।
ଛିନ୍ନମସ୍ତା ଦେବୀ ଖର୍ପର ପାତି, କାଠମାଳିଆଙ୍କୁ ଖାଇବ ବାଛି।
ଘୋର ଅନ୍ଧକାର ସେବେଳେ ହେବ, ସବୁ ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଜ୍ଞାନ ହଜିଣ ଯିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ନ ମିଳିବ ଶସ୍ୟ ନିଅଣ୍ଟି, ମୀନ ଶନିଆସି ଦେବଟି ଘାଣ୍ଟି।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ଚୁଲି ସେହିଦିନ ଫିଟିବ, ହାଡ଼ିଆ କମାର ପରଣୁ ଥିବ।
ସେଦିନ ଯେ ଗୁରୁବାରଟି ଜାଣ, କାଳିଆ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଅଟେ ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Jobarā Kāḷiābodā pramāṇa,
guptare lāgiba se māyā raṇa.
Thoke jibe Kāmachaṇḍī mukhare, Mlechcha māruṇī je heba guptare.
Taraka teruā jete bhakata, Jobarā Kāḷiābodāre nāsha.
Chhinnamastā debī kharpara pāti, kāṭhamāḷiāṅku khāiba bāchi.
Ghora andhakāra sebeḷe heba, sabu buddhi jñāna hajiṇa jiba.
Anna na miḷiba shasya niaṇṭi, mīna shaniāsi debaṭi ghāṇṭi.
Lakṣe chuli sehidina phiṭiba, hāḍiā kamāra paraṇu thiba.
Sedina je gurubāraṭi jāṇa, Kāḷiā parīkṣā aṭe pramāṇa.

Macne:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu qoray in marka Saturn uu ku jiro Pisces, inta lagu jiro xilligaas, imtixaanno dhammaan munaafiqiinta iyo kuwa aan cibaadaysan ayaa ka dhici doona Jobra iyo Kaliaboda ee Cuttack. Hooyooyinka Rabbaaniga ah (Ilaahyada) ayaa burburin doona dhammaan kuwa aan cibaadaysan. Xilligaas, waxaa sidoo kale jiri doona mugdi toddoba maalmood iyo toddoba habeen ah. Caqliga qof walba wuu joogsan doonaa shaqada. Inta lagu jiro xilligaas, dembiilayaashu xitaa ma awoodi doonaan inay cunto helaan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray buuggiisa Malika ee ‘Kalikalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଅଙ୍କ,
ଭାରତଶ୍ୱରୀ ହେବ ଉତପାତ।
ହେତୁ ରଖିଥାଅ ବ୍ରଜକୁମର,
ସେକାଳେ ତେଜିବି ମୁଁ କଳେବର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna shani heba Mukunda aṅka,
Bhāratashwarī heba utapāta.
Hetu rakhithāa Brajakumara,
sekāḷe tejibi muñ kaḷebara.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, marka Saturn uu ku jiro Pisces, waxaa fowdo ka dhici doonta Hindiya. Xilligaas, Sayidka Sare wuxuu ka tagi doonaa qaabkiisa bani'aadamka caadiga ah wuxuuna qaadan doonaa qaabka Kalki ee aadka u xooggan.

Buug kale oo Malika ah, Mahapurush wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନିର ସଂଯୋଗ ହେବାର ସମୟ,
ପୃଥବୀରେ ହେବ ପୁଣି ଘୋର ଯେ ପ୍ରଳୟ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna shanira saṃyoga hebāra samaya,
pṛthabīre heba puṇi ghora je pralaya.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, inta lagu jiro isku xirnaanta Meena-Shani, waxaa mar kale dhulka ka dhici doona burbur xun (Pralaya).

Achyutananda Das ji wuxuu ku qoray buuggiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ୋଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧୃବ।
ଅଢ଼ୋଇ ବରଷ ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ ଛବିଶ ଅଙ୍କ କୁଅନା,
ଶ୍ମଶାନ ପରାୟ ଏ ସାରା ସଂସାର ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ସିନା।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna shani āsi deuthiba ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhōi heba,
dasha aṇā jiba chha' aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.
aḍhāi baraṣa ghora utpāta chhabbisha aṅka kuanā,
shmashāna parāya e sārā saṃsāra uchhanna hoiba sinā.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, xilliga uu Saturn ka gudbayo Pisces, Dagaalkii Saddexaad ee Adduunka ayaa ka bilaaban doona Dhulka, oo ay ku jirto Hindiya. Laba sano iyo bar, burbur cabsi leh ayaa lagu arki doonaa Dhulka. Adduunka oo dhan waa la burburin doonaa wuxuuna qaadan doonaa qaabka goob gubasho. Inta lagu jiro xilligaas, dad aan la tirin karin ayaa dhiman doona.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray buug kale oo Malika ah ‘Bhabishyata Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ସାବଧାନ ହୁଅ ତୁହି,
ଠା ଠିକଣା କିଛି ରହିବ ନାହିଁ।
ହେ ଭକ୍ତଗଣ! ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସିଗଲେ ଧାଇଁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Sābadhāna hua tuhi,
ṭhā ṭhikaṇā kichhi rahiba nāhiñ.
He bhaktagaṇa! Mīna shani āsigale dhāiñ.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, Dad yahow, ka digtoonow. Meena-Shani Yoga ayaa soo socda. Xilligaas, musiibooyin badan ayaa imaan doona, burburku wuxuuna saameyn doonaa ku dhawaad qof walba; qofna lama badbaadin doono. Caqliga dadka wuu joogsan doonaa shaqada. Fowdo ayaa meel walba ka jiri doonta.

Wuxuu dhashay qarnigii 19-aad, abwaan-wali Bhima Bhoi wuxuu ka qoray mawduucan buuggiisa Malika ee ‘Padmakalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଖିବ ଜଳ ଅବିଳମ୍ବେ,
ମୀନ ଶନିର ମଧ୍ୟଭାଗେ।
ମୋଗଲ ପଠାଣର ଧାଡ଼ି,
ମଧ୍ୟ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଥିବ ମାଡ଼ି।
ସିଂହ ମଙ୍ଗଳ ଗ୍ରହ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି,
କିଛି ମେଦିନୀ ଯିବ ଫାଟି।
ଗିରି କନ୍ଦର ଗୁମ୍ଫାମାନ,
ମୋ ଭକ୍ତେ ରହିବେଟି ପୁଣ।
ଆସିବେ ଚଣ୍ଡୀକାଇଗଣ,
ଭକ୍ଷିବେ ନଗ୍ରରେ ପଶିଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shukhiba jaḷa abiḷambe,
mīna shanira madhyabhāge.
Mogala paṭhāṇara dhāḍi,
madhya Oḍishā thiba māḍi.
Siṃha maṅgaḷa graha dṛṣṭi,
kichhi medinī jiba phāṭi.
Giri kandara gumphāmāna,
mo bhakte rahibeṭi puṇa.
Āsibe chaṇḍīkāigaṇa,
bhakṣibe nagrare pashiṇa.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, ka dib qaybta dhexe ee Meena-Shani Yoga, webiyada iyo durdurrada ayaa bilaabi doona inay isla markiiba qallalaan. Ciidamada dalalka cadowga ah ayaa soo geli doona Odisha oo dagaallami doona. Dhexda Meena-Shani Yogaas oo kale, marka Mars uu ku jiro Leo, dhul gariir ayaa sidoo kale dhici doona. Sayidku wuxuu cibaadaysatadiisa ku ilaalin doonaa si taxaddar leh godadka buuraha. Chandi iyo Yoginis ayaa burburin doona kuwa xun iyo dembiilayaasha.

Abwaan-cibaadaysato Narada Das wuxuu sidoo kale xusay fowdada dhacaysa inta lagu jiro Meena-Shani Yoga buuggiisa Malika. Wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀଏ ନାଶ ଯିବ ରଙ୍କରେ।
ବତିଶ ତେତିଶ ପୁଣି ଚଉତିରିଶ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ।
ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ ଉଲ୍ଲୋଳ,
ଯୋଗିନୀ ହେବେ ପ୍ରବଳ।
ପଡ଼ି ହାଡ଼ କଙ୍କାଳ,
ଗଡ଼ିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ ମାଳ।
କମ୍ପିବ ଭୈରବ ଡାକରେ,
ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare,
prāṇīe nāsha jiba raṅkare.
Batisha tetisha puṇi chautirisha,
mīna shani bhoga heba aḍhāi baraṣa.
Pṛthabī heba ulloḷa,
yoginī hebe prabaḷa.
Paḍi hāḍa kaṅkāḷa,
gaḍiba muṇḍa māḷa.
Kampiba bhairaba ḍākare,
mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, Meena-Shani Yoga wuxuu socon doonaa wadar ahaan laba sano iyo bar inta lagu jiro xilligan, dad aan la tirin karin ayaa dhiman doona. Waxaa dhulka ka dhici doonta oohin. Yoginis waxay fulin doonaan falka rabbaaniga ah ee burburinta. Xilligan, maydad ayaa dhulka ku firdhi doona. Dhulka wuxuu la gariiri doonaa guuxa Bhairavi.

Isagoo soo xiganaya wada hadalkii u dhexeeyay Sayid Krishna iyo Hooyo Rukmini inta lagu jiray Dvapara Yuga, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray buuggiisa Malika ee ‘Rukmini Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ମେଳ ଗୋ ହୋଇବ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ସେବେଳେ ସମର ହେବ ଭାରତ ମଣ୍ଡଳେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna shani meḷa go hoiba jetebeḷe,
sebeḷe samara heba Bhārata maṇḍaḷe.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, xilliga uu Saturn ku gudbayo Pisces, dagaal ba'an ayaa ka dhici doona Hindiya.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ପଡ଼ିଲେଟି ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ମନେ ରଖିଥିବୁ ରାମ ତୁହି ବ୍ରଜସୁତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna shani paḍileṭi ghora utpāta,
mane rakhithibu Rāma tuhi Brajasuta.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, marka Meena-Shani Yoga ay dhacdo, fowdo cabsi leh ayaa lagu arki doonaa Dhulkan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray buugga Malika ee loo yaqaan ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ତେରଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ ରାଜନ,
ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଗଗନ।
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ କଷଣ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ଚଉଦ୍ୱାରେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ।
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନିକ ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଚଳ,
ନିକଟରେ ଗୋଳ ଚହଳ।
ତପ ସଙ୍ଗେ ତୋପ ପରୀକ୍ଷା,
ସତ୍ୟ ଅନନ୍ତଟି ଦେଖା।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ହୋଇବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Teradina pakṣa hoiba,
mīna shani yoga paḍiba.
Aḍhāi baraṣa rājana,
dibasare tārā gagana.
Bhaktaṅku kaṣaṇa paḍiba,
Chaudwāre yuddha hoiba.
Baijñānika yantra achaḷa,
nikaṭare goḷa chahaḷa.
Tapa saṅge topa parīkṣā,
satya Anantaṭi dekhā.
Ananta yugaṭi hoiba,
Achyutī kāmanā pūriba.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, dhammaadka Kaliyuga, inta lagu jiro xilliga aasaaska Dharma, laba toddobaad (Paksha) oo saddex iyo toban maalmood ah ayaa dhici doona. Waxaa jiri doona Meena-Shani Yoga oo socon doona laba sano iyo bar. Inta lagu jiro xilligaas, xiddigaha ayaa lagu arki doonaa maalintii. Xilligaas, cibaadaysatada waxay la kulmi doonaan dhibaatooyin badan. Dagaal ayaa ka dhici doona Choudwar ee Cuttack; qalabka sayniska iyo korontada wuu joogsan doonaa shaqada. Dagaal ba'an ayaa dhici doona. Gantaalada casriga ah iyo madaafiicda dalalka cadowga ah waxaa looga hortagi doonaa awoodda cibaadada (Tapa) ee awliyada iyo xikmadleyda Hindiya ee dhammaystiran. Sayid Kalki ayaa soo bixi doona; cibaadaysatada waxay heli doonaan aragtida (Darshan) Sayidka. Da'da Weligeed ah (Ananta Yuga) ayaa imaan doonta. Rabitaanka Achyutananda ayaa rumoobi doona.

Wuxuu mar kale ku qoray buuggiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଆକାଶୁ ଖସିବ ମୀନ ଶନିର ରାଜତ୍ୱ,
ବାଡ଼ି ବେମାରିରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dibasare tārā ākāśu khasiba mīna shanira rājatva,
bāḍi bemārire aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, xiddiguhu waxay ka soo dhici doonaan cirka maalintii; dad aan la tirin karin ayaa u dhiman doona saamaynta cudurrada iyo faafitaanka. Wakhtigaas, Meena-Shani Yoga ayaa socon doonta.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି କରିବ ରାଜତ୍ୱ ମାନବ ମାରୁଣୀ ହେବ,
ପରାଧୀନ ହେବ ଏ ଭାରତବର୍ଷ ଶାସନ କଳ ଲାଗିବ।
ଅରାଜକ ହେବ ଏହି ଜମ୍ବୁଦ୍ବୀପ ଭକ୍ତ ହୋଇବେ ଛାନିଆଁ,
ଅନହୁତି ଅଗ୍ନି ପ୍ରଜ୍ୱଳିତ ହେବ ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ସାରା ଦୁନିଆ।
ଛିନିଛତ୍ର ହେବ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳ ଥୟ ନ ପଡ଼ିବେ କେହି,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କୁହଇ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna shani āsi kariba rājatwa mānaba māruṇī heba,
parādhīna heba e Bhāratabarṣa shāsana kaḷa lāgiba.
Arājaka heba ehi Jambudwīpa bhakta hoibe chhāniāñ,
anahuti agni prajwaḷita heba uchhanna sārā duniā.
Chhinichhatra heba Oḍishā maṇḍaḷa thaya na paḍibe kehi,
Chakaḍā maḍāṇa prakāsha kariṇa dīna Achyuta kuhai.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, marka Saturn uu galo Pisces, wakhtiga burburka aadanaha ayaa bilaabmi doona. Nidaamka dimoqraadiga ah ayaa burburi doona, Hindiya-na waxay lumin doontaa madaxbannaanideeda—waxay xitaa hoos imaan kartaa xukunka qof kale. Fowdo ayaa qabsan doonta nidaamka maamulka, dalkuna wuxuu ku dhex milmi doonaa fowdo. Cibaadaysatadu waxay ka argagaxayaan markay arkaan dhacdooyinkan. Dabka burburku ma qabsan doono oo kaliya Odisha iyo Hindiya, laakiin adduunka oo dhan.

Achyutananda ji wuxuu ku qoray Kalikalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକୁ ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଗମନ ହେଲେ ଏମନ୍ତ ଭିଆଣ,
ଭାରା ନିବାରଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଦେବରାୟ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେ ଉହ୍ଲେଇବେ ଜାଣ।
କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna rāshiku Shanishchara gamana hele emanta bhiāṇa,
bhārā nibāraṇe prabhu debarāya martye uhleibe jāṇa.
Kalkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣatrīmānaṅku nikṣatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, si Dhulka looga xoreeyo dembiga, Sayid Mahavishnu wuxuu ku soo degi doonaa Dhulka. Inta lagu jiro xilliga Meena-Shani Yoga, isagoo qaadanaya qaabka Kalki, Sayidka Sare wuxuu dalka ku dhex mari doonaa isagoo burburinaya kuwa xunxun iyo dembiilayaasha, wuxuuna badbaadin doonaa Hooyo Dhulka.

Mahapurush wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଭାବ ବିନୋଦିଆ ମୂର୍ତ୍ତିଙ୍କୁ ଭାବ,
ଭାବରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ ଚୁମ୍ବିବ।
ବାଇଆ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଗୀର,
ବାରହାତ ଖଣ୍ଡା ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବିରାଟ ଗାଦୀ ସଂହାର।
ଭକତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ,
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହୋଇ ପୃଥବୀ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ ବିଶେଷ।
ମାୟାରେ ବୁଲେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଣାକାର ହୃଦରେ ଘୋଷ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ଦୃଶ୍ୟ।
ଯୋବରା ଘାଟର ଲୀଳା,
ଜଗିଛି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ କରି ହେଳା,
କଟିବ ପାପୀଙ୍କ ଗଳା।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
bhāba binodiā mūrttiṅku bhāba,
bhābare Achyutī dāsa chumbiba.
Bāiā Achyutī gīra,
bārahāta khaṇḍā heba bāhara,
birāṭa gādī saṃhāra.
Bhakata Achyutī dāsa,
bhūmikampa hoi pṛthabī ushwāsa,
bhakta rahibe bisheṣa.
Māyāre bule Achyuta,
mantra aṇākāra hṛdare ghoṣa,
mīna shani heba dṛshya.
Jobarā ghāṭara līḷā,
jagichhi Achyuta na kari heḷā,
kaṭiba pāpīṅka gaḷā.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, dagaalkii Mahabharata ee hadhay ayaa dhici doona. Dagaal ba'an ayaa ka dhici doona Virat Gadi ama Choudwar ee Cuttack, seef laba iyo toban gacmood lehna waxay dili doontaa dembiilayaasha. Dhul gariir ba'an ayaa dhici doona kaas oo burburin doona kuwa xunxun. Imtixaanno dhammaan munaafiqiinta ah ayaa ka dhici doona Jobra Ghat ee Cuttack, Hooyada Rabbaaniga ahna waxay cabbi doontaa dhiiggooda. Inta lagu jiro tan, cibaadaysatada ayaa la ilaalin doonaa. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in dhacdooyinkan ay ka dhici doonaan Meena-Shani Yoga.

Achyutananda Das ji wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Tattvabodhini’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକି ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଯିବ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ନଷ୍ଟ ଭ୍ରଷ୍ଟ କରିଦେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna rāshiki Shanishchara jiba,
Oḍishā naṣṭa bhraṣṭa karideba.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, ka dib marka Saturn uu galo Pisces, gobolka Odisha ayaa waxyeello gaari doonta.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ଦେଶକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହେବ,
ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ସେଠାବରେ ହେବ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ମନେ ଭାବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna shani yoga deshaku paḍiba tera dina pakṣa heba,
gupata māruṇī seṭhābare heba Rāmachandra mane bhāba.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, Meena-Shani Yoga ayaa dhici doonta, waxaana jiri doona afar iyo toban maalmood oo saddex iyo toban ah. Isla wakhtigaas, dadku waxay u dhiman doonaan si qarsoodi ah, Dharma-na waa la aasaasi doonaa.

Intaa waxaa dheer ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, wuxuu ku qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଚଳିଯିବ କଳି ଗୋଳ ଉପୁଜିବ ବାବୁ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହୋଇଥିବ ଗୁପତ ରଖିବୁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chaḷi jiba kaḷi goḷa upujiba bābu,
mīna shani hoithiba gupata rakhibu.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, inta lagu jiro xilliga Meena-Shani Yoga, Kaliyuga wuu dhammaan doonaa, Dharma-na waa la aasaasi doonaa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଛାରଖାର ହେବ ଏ ସୁନା ସଂସାର ଭଇରବୀ ଦେବ ଡାକ,
ଅକାଳ ମରଣ ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ମୀନ ଶନିଯୋଗ ହେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chārakhāra heba e sunā saṃsāra bhairabī deba ḍāka,
akāḷa maraṇa gupata māruṇī mīna shaniyoga heba.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, adduunkan wuu "burburi doonaa" ama si buuxda ayuu u baaba'i doonaa. Hooyo Bhairavi waxay soo dayn doontaa guux ba'an; dembiilayaal badan ayaa la kulmi doona geeri aan wakhtigeedii ahayn, qaar badanina waxay u dhiman doonaan si qarsoodi ah. Dhammaan ficilladan burburka ah waxay dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro Meena-Shani Yoga, taas oo macnaheedu yahay marka Saturn uu ku jiro calaamadda zodiac ee Pisces.

Aayadaha Malika ee kor ku xusan, waxaa cad in inta lagu jiro xilliga Meena-Shani Yoga, ficillada burburka ee Sayid Kalki ay noqon doonaan kuwa ugu waxtarka badan. Inta lagu jiro xilligan, Dhulka oo dhan wuxuu markhaati ka noqon doonaa dhacdooyin masiibo ah oo baaxad leh oo aan hore loo arag. Kaliya qaylo, dagaal, rabshado, kacdoon, iyo masiibooyin dabiici ah ayaa meel kasta laga arki doonaa. Wakhtigan, kuwa xunxun iyo kuwa aan xaqa ahayn ayaa laga burburin doonaa Dhulka oo dhan.

5.2 Meena-Shani kee ayay dhici doonaan falalka rabbaaniga ah ee Sayid Kalki?

Ka dib markii laga ogaaday aayadaha kor ku xusan in falalka rabbaaniga ah ee burburka Kalki ay dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro xilliga Meena-Shani, su'aasha ugu weyn ayaa soo baxaysa: xilli Meena-Shani gaar ah ayay dhacdooyinkan ka dhici doonaan?

Meena-Shani Yoga waxay dhacdaa mar kasta 30 sano. Sidaa darteed, waxaa lagama maarmaan ah in la ogaado Meena-Shani-ga, sida ku cad Malika, kaas oo markhaati ka noqon doona dhacdooyinka aasaaska Dharma.

Marka si dhow loo fiiriyo, waxaan ogaanay in Saturn uu ku jiray Pisces sanadkii 1995; hadda, sanadka 2025, Saturn wuxuu mar kale u dhaqaaqay Pisces; ka dibna 2054, iyo qiyaastii 30 sano ka dib, 2084, wareegga Saturn ee Pisces ayaa mar kale dhici doona.

Meena-Shani kee?

Waxaa si cad loogu qoray Bhavishya Malika in marka dhacdooyinkan ay dhacaan labada sano iyo badh ee Meena-Shani gudaheeda, magaca Boqorka Puri, Odisha, wakhtigaas uu noqon doono Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chandra Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଥିବ ସେହି ବେଳର,
ପୃଥବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ଘନ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତର ଏ ପ୍ରଳୟ ରାଶି,
ଭୂଭାରାକୁ ରାମ ଯିବ ରେ ଗ୍ରାସୀ।
ସେକାଳେ ମୀନେ ଶନି ଥିବ ଚଲି,
ନାଶିବ କାଳ ହେବ ଜଳ ଖେଳି।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ଗୁରୁ ସ୍ମରିଣ,
ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳ୍ପରେ ମୁଁ କଲି ବଖାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dibyasiṃha thiba sehi beḷara,
pṛthabī hoijiba ghana andhāra.
pañchabhūtara e pralaya rāśi,
bhūbhārāku rāma jiba re grāsī.
sekāḷe mīne śani thiba chali,
nāśiba kāḷa heba jaḷa kheḷi.
Achyuta kahai guru smariṇa,
chandra kalpare muñ kali bakhāṇa.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, marka Saturn uu ku jiro Pisces, wakhtigaas, mugdi qoto dheer ayaa qabsan doona Dhulka 7 maalmood iyo 7 habeen. Burburka ay sababaan shanta curiye (Dhulka, Biyaha, Dabka, Hawada, iyo Ether) ayaa baabbi'in doona Dhulka oo dhan. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in xilligaas, magaca Boqorka Puri uu noqon doono ‘Divyasingha’.

Hadda qaar ka mid ah qodobbo ayaa mudan in halkan lagu xuso. Boqorka hadda jira ee Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva (IV), wuxuu hadda jiraa qiyaastii 72 sano. Meena-Shani-ga xiga—taas oo ah, 30 sano ka dib—da'diisu waxay noqon doontaa 100+, waxaana jirta fursad ku dhow eber inuu weli fadhiyo carshiga Puri wakhtigaas!

Hadda dadka qaar, oo sheegaya wakhtiga dhacdooyinka inay yihiin Meena-Shani-ga xiga (taas oo ah, sanadka 2054–2057), waxay soo jeedinayaan inay suurtogal tahay in dhacdooyinkan ay dhacaan inta lagu jiro xukunka boqor kale oo lagu magacaabo Divyasingha! Laakiin aan kuu sheego in tani aysan suurtogal ahayn. Boqorka hadda jira ee Puri waa Boqorka 19-aad, sida ku cad Malika, Boqorka 19-aad wuxuu noqon doonaa Boqorka ugu dambeeya ee Kaliyuga. Taas macnaheedu waa, sida ku cad Malika, ka dib Boqorka hadda jira Shri Divyasingha Deva, ma jiri doonaan Boqorro kale oo Puri ah.

Doodda kor ku xusan waxay caddaynaysaa in dhammaan dhacdooyinka masiibada ah ay dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro Meena-Shani Yoga ee u dhexeeya 2025–2028, inta lagu jiro xukunka Shri Divyasingha Deva IV.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ବହୁତ ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ପାପର ଭାରା,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ହୋଇବେ ଧରା।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରେ ପାଣି ପଶିବ,
ସିଂହ ଦ୍ୱାରେ ରକ୍ତଧାର ବହିବ।
ଟିକରା ଗୋହିରୀ ହୋଇବ ରଡ଼ି,
ଧାନ ଥିବ ଯିବ କଣ୍ଠା ଅଗାଡ଼ି।
ସେବେଳକୁ ଏକା ଅକ୍ଷର ସାର,
ସପତ ଦିନ ଯେ ହେବ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ଗାଲୁଆ ମାଲୁଆ ଭକତ ଛନ୍ତି,
ବଗୁଲିଆ କର୍ମେ ପେଟ ପୋଷନ୍ତି।
ଏମାନେ ଯେ ଦେବୀ ଖପରରେ ଯିବେ,
ଭକତମାନେ ଆନନ୍ଦରେ ରହିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bahuta anyāya pāpara bhārā,
Dibyasiṃha deba hoibe dharā.
Baḍa deuḷare pāṇi pashiba,
siṃhadvāre raktadhāra bahiba.
ṭikarā gohirī hoiba raḍi,
dhāna thiba jiba kaṇṭhā agāḍi.
sebeḷaku ekā akṣara sāra,
sapata dina je heba andhāra.
gāluā māluā bhakata chanti,
baguliā karme peṭa poṣanti.
emāne je debī khaparare jibe,
bhaktamāne ānandare rahibe.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, marxaladda ugu dambeysa ee Kaliyuga, waxaa jiri doona cadaalad darro badan iyo culeys xad dhaaf ah oo dembi ah. Wakhtigaas, biyaha badda ayaa soo geli doona Macbadka Jagannath. Dagaal ayaa ka dhex qarxi doona macbadka. Durdur dhiig ah ayaa ka soo qulquli doona Albaabka Libaaxa (Singhadwara). Wakhtigaas, mugdi ayaa qabsan doona Dhulka oo dhan toddoba maalmood iyo toddoba habeen. Dhammaan kuwa ah cibaadaysato munaafiqiin ah, oo nolol maalmeedkooda ka helaya falal khaldan, waxaa burburin doona Hooyooyinka Rabbaaniga ah. Cibaadaysatadu waxay wakhtigooda ku qaadan doonaan farxad. Inta lagu jiro wakhtigaas, Boqorka Puri wuxuu noqon doonaa Shri Divyasingha Deva, kaas oo ay qabsan doonaan ama afduuban doonaan kuwa dulmiya.

Waxaa si buuxda uga cad aayadda kor ku xusan in marka ay dhacaan dhacdooyinka aasaaska Dharma, iyo marka uu jiro mugdi toddoba maalmood iyo toddoba habeen ah Dhulka, Boqorka Puri wakhtigaas uu noqon doono Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush kale oo ka yimid Odisha oo dhashay qarnigii 20-aad ayaa ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa in ka dib sanadka 2033, iftiinka xilli cusub (Satyayuga) uu soo bixi doono; taas oo macnaheedu yahay, sida ku cad Mahapurush, dhammaan falalka rabbaaniga ah ee kala guurka xilliyada ayaa la dhammaystiri doonaa ka hor sanadka 2033. Waxaan idiin soo bandhigi doonaa aayadaha la xiriira tan Cutubka 12. Qodobku waa in dhammaan dhacdooyinka masiibada ah ay dhici doonaan ka hor 2033.

Mahapurush Bhima Bhoi (abwaan-cibaadaysato indho la' oo ku dhashay Sambalpur, Odisha qarnigii 19-aad) wuxuu mawduucan ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Padma Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ନିଶ୍ଚୟ କଳଙ୍କି ହୋଇବ, ଯେ ଦିନ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ।
ପଞ୍ଚଗ୍ରହ ଯେ ଯୁକ୍ତ ହେବେ, ଆଗାମୀ ଅଢ଼ାଇର ଯୋଗେ।
ତଦନ୍ତେ ଷଡ଼ ଗ୍ରହ ଆସି, ଶନି କୁମ୍ଭରୁ ଯିବେ ଖସି।
ପ୍ରବେଶ ମୀନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ, ଟାକିଥାଅ ହେ ସୁଜ୍ଞ ନରେ।
ହୋଇବ ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ ସମର, ଦମନ ହୋଇବ ବାହାର।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ନାଶିବ ଅବହେଳେ, ଜଗତେ ରଖି ବାହୁବଳେ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛଙ୍କୁ ଦମନ ନାଶିବ, ଦୁର୍ଗା ଖପର ପକାଇବ।
ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ କଳି ସେ ଦମନ, ଶୁଣ ହେ ସାଧୁ ସୁଜ୍ଞଜନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Nishchaya Kaḷaṅki hoiba, je dina mīna shani bhoga.
Pañchagraha je yukta hebe, āgāmī aḍhāira joge.
Tadante ṣaḍa graha āsi, shani kumbharu jibe khasi.
Prabesha mīna saṅgatare, ṭākithāa he sujña nare.
Hoiba asaṃkhya samara, damana hoiba bāhāra.
Biṣṇu nāshiba abaheḷe, jagate rakhi bāhubaḷe.
Mlechchaṅku damana nāshiba, Durgā khapara pakāiba.
Asaṃkhya kaḷi se damana, shuṇa he sādhu sujñajana.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, marka Saturn uu galo Pisces, falalka rabbaaniga ah ee burburka Sayid Kalki ayaa dhici doona. Xilligan wuxuu socon doonaa laba sano iyo badh. Inta lagu jiro wakhtigan, dagaallo aan la tirin karin ayaa ka dhex dhici doona waddamo kala duwan. Sayid Kalki wuxuu ilaalin doonaa cibaadaysatada, Hooyo Durga-na waxay burburin doontaa kuwa xunxun iyo dembiilayaasha.

Aayadda kor ku xusan, Mahapurush wuxuu si cad u sheegay Meena-Shani kee ay ka dhici doonaan falalka rabbaaniga ah ee burburka Kalki. Isagoo sharxaya aqoonsiga Meena-Shani-gaas, wuxuu yiri in maalinta Saturn uu galo Pisces, 5 meerayaal ah ay horeyba ugu jiri doonaan Pisces. Taas macnaheedu waa, maalintaas, wadar ahaan 6 meerayaal ah ayaa ku jiri doona Pisces—5 meerayaal ah oo horeyba u jiray iyo hal Saturn, oo wadartoodu tahay 6 meerayaal.

Haddii aan eegno Meena-Shani-ga 2025, waxaan ogaanay in hadalka Mahapurush uu noqonayo mid si fiican u sax ah. 29-ka Maarso 2025, maalinta Saturn uu u wareego Pisces, shan meerayaal ah (Rahu, Venus, Mercury, Sun, iyo Moon) ayaa horeyba ugu jiray Pisces.

Hadda, haddii aan eegno Meena-Shani-ga 2054, waxaan ogaanay in 14-ka Maajo 2054, maalinta Saturn uu u wareego Pisces, kaliya 2 meerayaal ah (Venus iyo Mars) ay horeyba ugu jiri doonaan Pisces, iyo Saturn-ka, waxay ka dhigaysaa 3 meerayaal. Halka sida ku cad Saint Bhima Bhoi, ay tahay in wadar ahaan 6 meerayaal ah ay ku jiraan Pisces, ma aha 3.

Haddii aan eegno Meena-Shani Yoga ee sanadka 2084, waxaan ogaanay in 20-ka Maarso 2084, maalinta Saturn uu u wareego Pisces, kaliya 2 meerayaal ah (Mercury iyo Sun) ay horeyba ugu jiri doonaan Pisces. Xitaa haddii Saturn lagu daro, waa 3 meerayaal. Tani waxay ka dhigan tahay in wareegga 6-meerayaal ah uusan halkan ka dhicin sidoo kale.

Waxaa cad in Mahapurush uusan ka hadlaynin Meena-Shani Yoga ee 2054 ama 2084. Meena-Shani-ga uu kaga hadlayo qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Padma Kalpa’ waa runtii Meena-Shani-ga 2025.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sidoo kale ku sheegay qoraalkiisa Malika cidda noqon doonta Mahant (Madaxa) Padmaban Peeth-kiisa oo ku yaal Nemalo, Cuttack, marka ay dhacaan falalkaan rabbaaniga ah ee burburka. Wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳରେ,
ନବ୍ୟକ୍ତ କଥା କହିଲି ରାମ ହେତୁ ଚିତ୍ତରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta kaḷi śeṣa beḷare,
Nabyakta kathā kahili rāma hetu chittare.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, O arday Ramadas, marka Kaliyuga uu dhammaado, wakhtigaas qof lagu magacaabo Gaganānanda ayaa noqon doona Madaxa Peeth-kayga.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu ku qoray qoraal kale oo Malika ah:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ ଗଗନେ ଉଡ଼ିବ ଧୂଳି,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶ ଯେ ଚମକି ପଡ଼ିବ ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ହାଲହୁଳି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta hoiba gagane uḍiba dhūḷi,
Oḍishā desha je chamaki paḍiba paḍijiba hālahuḷi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, falalka rabbaaniga ah waxay dhici doonaan xilliga Mahant Gaganānanda. Waqtigaas, waxaa jiri doona qaylo-dhaan ka dhacda gobolka Odisha.

5.3 Mahant Gaganānanda Goswami

Waxaa xusid mudan in jiilkii 14-aad ee Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, faraciisa iyo hadda 85–90 jirka ah Shri Gaganānanda Goswamiji uu yahay Madaxa kursiga ruuxiga ah ee Mahapurush, Padmaban, Nemalo. Tani waxay sidoo kale caddaynaysaa in Mahapurush-ku ka hadlayo xilligan xaadirka ah.

Afarta qodob ee kor ku xusan, waxaa caddaan ah in sida ku cad Malika, Meena-Shani-ga ay ka dhacaan ficillada rabbaaniga ah ee burburka Kalki uu imaan doono ka hor sanadka 2033, iyo inta lagu jiro xilligaas Meena-Shani, Shri Divyasingha Deva wuxuu noqon doonaa Boqorka Puri, Shri Gaganānanda-na wuxuu noqon doonaa Mahant-ka Padmaban Peeth, Nemalo. Intaas oo kaliya maaha, laakiin maalinta Saturn uu u gudbo Pisces, Shad-graha (6-meere) Yoga ayaa ka samaysmi doonta Pisces.

Waxaa caddaan ah in Mahapurush-ku tilmaamayo Meena-Shani-ga imaanaya inta u dhaxaysa 2025–2028 sababtoo ah afarta qodob ee kor ku xusan waxay khuseeyaan oo kaliya Meena-Shani Yoga-gan, mana khuseeyaan mid kale.

Waxaa iska cad in sida ku cad Malika, dhacdooyinka waaweyn ee musiibada ah ee uu fulin doono Sayid Kalki ay dhici doonaan inta u dhaxaysa 2025–2028.

Fiiro gaar ah: Maalmahaan dadka qaar waxay leeyihiin Meena-Shani-ga lagu sheegay Malika maaha Meena-Shani-ga hadda jira, laakiin waa kan xiga ee imaanaya 2054! Waxaa habboon in dhammaan cibaadaysatada aaminsan Malika aysan ku wareerin arrintan, sababtoo ah hadallada noocan oo kale ah waa kuwo gebi ahaanba khaldan.

6 Boqorka Puri dhamaadka Kaliyuga

Cutubkii hore, waxaan ku aragnay in dhammaan dhacdooyinka waaweyn ee aasaaska Dharma ay dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro xukunka Boqorka Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva. Cutubkan, waxaan ku fahmi doonaa arrintan iyada oo loo marayo aayado kale oo Malika ah.

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Jagannath Das, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kali Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ପୁରୁଷୋତ୍ତମ ଦେବ ରାଜା ଠାରୁ,
ଉନ୍ନବିଂଶ ରାଜା ହେବ ସେଠାରୁ।
ସେଠାରୁ ପୁଣ ରାଜା ନାହିଁ ଆଉ,
ଅକୁଳି ହୋଇବେ କୁଳକୁ ବହୁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Puruṣottama Deba rājā ṭhāru,
unnabiṃsha rājā heba seṭhāru.
Seṭhāru puṇa rājā nāhiṃ āu,
akuḷi hoibe kuḷaku bahu.

Macne:
Macnaha, laga bilaabo Boqor Purushottama Deva ka dib Boqor Indradyumna, wadar ahaan 19 boqor ayaa noqon doona Boqorrada Jagannath Puri waqtiyo kala duwan. Mahapurush-ku wuxuu qoray in Boqorka 19-aad uu noqon doono mid aan wiil lahayn oo uu noqon doono boqorkii ugu dambeeyay; macnaha, qof kale ma noqon doono boqor isaga ka dib.

Haddii aan eegno xaaladda hadda jirta, waxaan ogaanay in Boqorka hadda jira ee Puri uu yahay Boqorkii 19-aad oo aan sidoo kale wiil lahayn. Tilmaanta cad ee Mahapurush-ku waa in dhammaan ciyaaraha Kalki ee aasaaska Dharma ay dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro xukunka boqorka hadda jira.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Adi Samhita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନାମେ ରାୟେ,
ଏ ଯୁଗ ପ୍ରାନ୍ତେ ସେ ଉଦୟେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shri Dibyasiṃha nāme rāye,
e juga prānte se udaye.

Macne:
Macnaha, dhamaadka Kaliyuga, Shri Divyasingha wuxuu noqon doonaa Boqorka Puri.

Qoraal kale oo Malika ah, wuxuu ku qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଅଙ୍କେ ବାବୁ ସରବ ଦେଖିବୁ,
ଛାଡ଼ି ଚକା ଗଲୁ ବୋଲି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣିବୁ,
ନର ବାଳୁତ ରୂପରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମିବୁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dibyasiṃha aṅke bābu saraba dekhibu,
chhāḍi chakā galu boli nishchaya jānibu,
nara bāḷuta rūpare āmbhe janamibu.

Macne:
Macnaha, marka xukunka Boqorka lagu magacaabo Shri Divyasingha Deva uu ka socdo Puri, waqtigaas dhammaan ciyaaraha rabbaaniga ah ee Sayid Kalki ayaa dhici doona. Waqtigaas, Sayidku wuxuu ka tagi doonaa Macbadkiisa Jagannath wuxuuna ku dhalan doonaa jidh bini'aadan ah isagoo ah Kalki Avatar.

Kadibna qoraalka Malika ee ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, Achyutanandaji wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ବୃଦ୍ଧ ବାଳକ ରାଜା ହୋଇଥିବ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସଣ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବ।
ବାଳବୃଦ୍ଧ ଅଙ୍କେ ବତାସ ହେବ,
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ।
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୀନମାନ ବହୁ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bruddha bāḷaka rājā hoithiba,
gupatare saṇa sāla chāliba.
bāḷabruddha aṅke batāsha heba,
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba.
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba,
pṛthibī hīnamāna bahu hoiba.

Macne:
Macnaha, “Baalak-Briddha” wuxuu noqon doonaa Boqorka Puri; taasi waa, qof ayaa noqon doona boqor carruurnimadiisa ku fuula carshiga oo ku sii jira booskaas ilaa gabowga. Inta lagu jiro xukunkiisa, dhacdooyin sida musiibooyinka biyaha iyo musiibooyinka dabaysha ayaa dhici doona.

Waxaa la og yahay in boqorradii ilaa hadda la sameeyay, midna aan ahayn Shri Divyasingha Deva uusan boqor ka ahayn carruurnimadiisa ilaa gabowga. Boqorka hadda jira Shri Divyasingha Deva wuxuu carshiga fuulay July 7, 1970, isagoo da'diisu ahayd 17 jir oo kaliya. Isagoo booskan ku jira, wuxuu galay gabow, haddana wuxuu jiraa qiyaastii 72 sano. Aayaddan, luqad astaan ah, Mahapurush-ku wuxuu ku shaaciyay in Divyasingha Deva uu noqon doono boqorka dhamaadka Kaliyuga.

Achyutananda Dasji wuxuu sii qoray ‘Kalikalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ଅଙ୍କରେ ଜାଣ,
ଧୂମକେତୁ ହେବ ପୂର୍ବଭାଗେଣ।
ଉଲ୍‌କାପାତ ହେବ ହାଲହୁଳି,
ଶ୍ୱାନ ଶୃଗାଳ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ବୋବାଳି।
ମାତିବେ କାଳୀ ଯେ କରାଳୀଗଣ,
ଲୋକେ ମରିବେ ହୋଇ ରଣଭଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dibyasiṃha Deba aṅkare jāṇa,
dhūmaketu heba pūrbabhāgeṇa.
ulkāpāta heba hālahuḷi,
shwāna shṛgāḷa chhāḍibe bobāḷi.
mātibe kāḷī je karāḷīgaṇa,
loke maribe hoi raṇabhaṇa.

Macne:
Macnaha, marka Shri Divyasingha Deva uu yahay Boqorka Puri, inta lagu jiro xilligaas, xiddig dabayl leh ayaa ka muuqan doona dhanka bari. Waxaa jiri doona roobab meteoro ah oo dhulka oo dhan ah. Xayawaannada hilibka cuna sida eeyaha iyo dawacada ayaa ka tagi doona kaymaha oo u socon doona dhanka meelaha la deggan yahay. Hooyo Kali iyo Yoginis-keeda ayaa burburin doona dembiilayaasha dhulka, taasoo keeni doonta dhimashada dad aan la tirin karin.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu si kooban ugu wargeliyay aayadda kor ku xusan in dhacdooyinka aasaaska Dharma ay dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro xukunka Boqorka Puri ee lagu magacaabo Shri Divyasingha Deva dhamaadka Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu ku yiri ardaygiisa Ramadas qoraal kale oo Malika ah:

Odia Sloka:

କେଉଁ କାଳ ବୋଲି ବୋଇଲୁ ଶୁଣ ରାମ ରତନ,
ଥିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ରାଜନ କୋଳେ କନ୍ୟା ରତନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Keuñ kāḷa boli boilu śuṇa Rāma ratana,
thiba Dibyasiṃha rājana koḷe kanyā ratana.

Macne:
Macnaha, arday Ramadasow, dhegayso xilliga aan ka hadlayo—waqtiga ay dhacdooyinkan dhacaan, xukunka Boqor lagu magacaabo Divyasingha ayaa socon doona, kaasoo aan wiil lahayn; gabdho kaliya.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ କରିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପତି,
ତାଙ୍କ କୁଳେ ପୁତ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭଣେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bāra bāra beḷaku kariba Dibyasiṃha nṛpati,
tāṅka kuḷe putra nohiba bhaṇe dīna Achyutī.

Macne:
Macnaha, dhamaadka Kaliyuga, waxaa jiri doona xukunka Boqor lagu magacaabo Divyasingha oo aan wiil lahayn.

Basharadii Mahapurush-ku sidoo kale waxay si fiican ugu habboon tahay Boqorka hadda jira ee Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva. Boqorka hadda jira ma laha wiil; wuxuu leeyahay afar gabdhood oo kaliya.

Qoraal kale oo Malika ah, ‘Kaliyuga Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ereyadii uu Sayid Krishna ku yiri Arjuna ee Dwaparayuga, kaasoo Sayid Krishna yiri:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shrikshetra rājana mora na sebi payara.

Macne:
Macnaha, dhamaadka Kaliyuga, Boqorka Puri ma qaban doono adeegayga maalinlaha ah (qaabka Jagannath).

Achyutananda Dasji wuxuu markaas qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ନ ଥିବ ରାଜନ ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ମଉଡ଼ ମଣି,
ରାଜ୍ୟ ରାଜକୁଳ କନ୍ୟାକୁ ନ କରିବ ବର୍ଣ୍ଣି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Na thiba rājana Shrīkṣetre kṣetra mauḍa maṇi,
rājya rājakuḷa kanyāku na kariba barṇṇi.

Macne:
Macnaha, Boqorka Puri ma degganaan doono Shrikshetra ama Puri, laakiin wuxuu degganaan doonaa meel kale. Ma guursan doono amiirad ka timid qoyska boqortooyada Odisha laakiin wuxuu guursan doonaa amiirad ka timid gobol kale.

Awli weyn oo Odisha ah oo dhashay qarnigii 18-aad, Mahapurush Arakshita Das, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Dwadasa Chandrika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଆସିବେ ଫେରି,
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରକୁ ନ ଯାଇ ରହିବେ ଜାଣ ସେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି।
ଭେଦ ମୁଁ କାହୁଁ ଜାଣିଥାନ୍ତି ଆଦିମାତା କହିଲେ,
ଭଣେ ଅରକ୍ଷିତ ଅଧମ ତହୁଁ ଶୁଣିଲି ଭଲେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati nishchaya āsibe pheri,
kṣhetraku na jāi rahibe jāṇa se Khaṇḍagiri.
bheda muñ kāhuñ jāṇithānti Ādimātā kahile,
bhaṇe Arakṣhita adhama tahuñ śuṇili bhale.

Macne:
Mahapurush-ku wuxuu qoray in dhamaadka Kaliyuga, Divyasingha Deva uu noqon doono Boqorka Puri oo uusan degganaan doonin Shrikshetra ama Puri, laakiin wuxuu degganaan doonaa aagga Khandagiri ama Bhubaneswar. Mahapurush-ku wuxuu sii qoray sida uu isagu u ogaan karay sirta arrimahan; Hooyada Asalka ah (Adi Mata) ayaa u sheegtay arrimahan.

Aan u sheego akhristayaasha in dhammaan boqorradii ka horreeyay Boqorka hadda jira ay degganaan jireen Puri, Odisha, oo ay qaban jireen adeegga maalinlaha ah ee Sayid Jagannath, laakiin Boqorka hadda jira Shri Divyasingha Deva wuxuu adeegga Sayid Jagannath qabtaa oo kaliya munaasabadaha gaarka ah. Ma deggana Puri laakiin wuxuu ku nool yahay Shahid Nagar, Bhubaneswar. Intaa waxaa dheer, Shri Divyasingha Deva ma guursan amiirad ka timid Odisha laakiin wuxuu guursaday gabadha Lieutenant Colonel Anant Singh Charak ee Jammu.

Saddexda basharadood ee kor ku xusan waxay si fiican ugu habboon yihiin Boqorka hadda jira ee Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva. Tani sidoo kale waa caddayn ah in Kaliyuga uu dhammaan doono inta lagu jiro xukunka Boqor Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraal kale oo Malika ah ‘Tera Janma Sharana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Māgha Saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājattwa karibe ehi Ekāmra Nagara.

Macne:
Macnaha, marka Divyasingha Deva uu yahay Boqorka Puri, markaas xukunka Ananta Keshari, taasoo ah, Sayid Kalki, ayaa dhici doona. Waxay noqon doontaa maalinta Magha Saptami; Bhubaneswar (Ekambra Nagar) ayaa noqon doonta meesha.

Aayaddan sidoo kale waxay caddaynaysaa in Boqorka Puri ee xilliga ciyaaraha rabbaaniga ah ee Kalki uusan ahayn mid kale oo aan ahayn Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Mohakalpa’, Achyutanandaji wuxuu bixiyay tilmaan kale oo caddaynaysa in Shri Divyasingha Deva uu noqon doono boqorka ugu dambeeya ee Puri. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଧନୁଶର ଧରି ଆଗେ ସେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ଯେ ଜାଣ ବୈଷ୍ଣବ ଧର୍ମ ଛାଡିବେ।
କ୍ଷତ୍ରି ବୃତ୍ତି ଧରି ପଦାତିଙ୍କ ସହ କରିବେ ବହୁ ସମର,
ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କ ଦେଖି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଡିବେ ପଦ୍ମପାଦର।
ନବଲକ୍ଷ ଧର୍ମ-ହୀନ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ତହିଁ ତୁରିତେ ହେବେ ଛେଦନ,
ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଘାତେ ତହିଁ ମେଦିନୀ ଯେ କମ୍ପମାନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଠାରୁ ଆଦି ଓଡିଶା ରାଜ୍ୟରେ ଯେତେକ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଥିବେ,
ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେଇ ସମସ୍ତେ ତହିଁ ମରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ର ପୁଛିବେ,
ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ହତ୍ୟା ବୋଲି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ କହି ବଧ୍ୟ ନ କରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପରୀକ୍ଷା କରିବେ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ଜାଣ,
ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣଟି ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେବ ନାଶିବ ଯେ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ ଚକ୍ର ଶୂନ୍ୟ ଗଗନେ ରଖିବେ,
ଚଉଷଠି ଅକ୍ଷର ପ୍ରମାଣ ନ ପାଇ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଥାଇ ଛେଦିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Lalāṭa Kesharī dhanushara dhari āge se juddha karibe,
Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati je jāṇa baiṣṇaba dharma chhāḍibe.
Kṣatri bṛutti dhari padātiṅka saha karibe bahu samara,
Prabhuṅka dekhi Dibyasiṃha Deba paḍibe padmapādara.
Nabalakṣa dharma-hīna byakti tahiṃ turite hebe chhedana,
Lalāṭa Kesharī juddha ghāte tahiṃ medinī je kampamāna.
Kṣetra ṭhāru ādi Oḍishā rājyare jeteka brāhmaṇa thibe,
Gāyatrī mantraku parīkṣā na dei samaste tahiṃ maribe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba brāhmaṇamānaṅku Gāyatrī mantra puchhibe,
Brāhmaṇa hatyā boli brāhmaṇamānaṅku kahi badhya na karibe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba parīkṣā karibe Gāyatrī mantraku jāṇa,
Jeuṃ brāhmaṇaṭi parīkṣā na deba nāshiba je Sudarshana.
Ananta Kesharī Sudarshana chakra shūnya gagane rakhibe,
Chauṣaṭhi akṣara pramāṇa na pāi shūnyare thāi chhedibe.

Macne:
Macnaha, Boqorka Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, wuxuu ka tagi doonaa Vaishnava Dharma-kiisa wuxuuna qaadan doonaa xirfadda Kshatriya wuxuuna la dagaallami doonaa dagaallo badan. Markuu arko Sayid Kalki, wuxuu ku dhici doonaa cagaha Sayidka, wuxuu qaadan doonaa barakooyinka Sayidka, wuxuuna burburin doonaa sagaal lakh oo gaalo ah. Deegaanka Jagannath, Puri, dhammaan Brahmins-ka jira waxaa lagu tijaabin doonaa Gayatri Mantra. Isagoo ka cabsanaya dembiga dilka Brahmin-ka, Divyasingha Deva ma dili doono Brahmins-kaas oo ku guuldareysta tijaabada. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in dhammaantood ay burburin doonto Sudarshana Chakra ee Sayidka lafteeda. Sayidku wuxuu Sudarshana Chakra-kiisa dhigi doonaa cirka bannaan wuxuuna madaxa ka gooyo doonaa Brahmins-kaas oo aan akhrin Gayatri Mantra isla chakra-daas.

Halkan, Mahapurush wuxuu bixiyaa caddayn ah in Boqorka Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, uu la kulmi doono Sayid Kalki wuxuuna gacan ka geysan doonaa aasaaska Dharma. Waxaa cad in ficillada burburinta ee Sayid Kalki ay dhici doonaan inta uu xukunka hayo Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Kadib Mahapurush wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kalpa Tika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିୟ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ କରିଥିବେ ସେ ବନ୍ଧନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ନୃପତି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଯେ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଯେ ରଖିଥିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଗୋପନ।
ବନ୍ଧନର ଦୁଇ ଦିନରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି,
ରାତ୍ରିକାଳେ ରାଜା ପାଶେ ବିଜେ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ରାଜାର ବନ୍ଧନ ଫେଇ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବାସୁ ଦେବେ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ତଳ ପୁରେ ଗୋପନେ ରଖିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dibyasiṃha Deba se je Kṣhatriya rājana,
tāhānku Phiringī karithibe se bandhana.
Kṣhetrara nṛupati Dibyasiṃha je rājana,
tāhānku je rakhithibe Shrī Hari gopana.
Bandhanara dui dinare Prabhu je Shrī Hari,
rātrikāḷe rājā pāśe bije je Shrī Hari.
rājāra bandhana phei Prabhu bāśu debe,
Khaṇḍagiri taḷa pure gopane rakhibe.

Macne:
Macnaha, marka Boqorka Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, ay qabsadaan ciidamada shisheeye, laba maalmood ka dib, Sayid Kalki wuxuu gaari doonaa boqorka habeenkii, wuxuuna ka xoreyn doonaa addoonsiga, wuxuuna ku qarin doonaa buuraha hoose ee Khandagiri.

Intaa waxaa dheer, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Padmakalpa Tika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ମୋ ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ,
ସେହିବେଳେ ସରକାର ଖାଦ୍ୟ ପକାଇବେ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଆଦିକରି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ananta gumphā madhyare mo bhakta rahibe,
sehibeḷe sarakāra khādya pakāibe.
Ananta Keshari swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha Lalāṭa Keshari ādikari.

Macne:
Macnaha, marka masiibada biyo ee daran ay dhacdo, cibaadada Sayid Kalki ee ku tiirsan waxay ku nagaan doonaan nabadgelyo godka Ananta ee ku yaal buurta Khandagiri. Waqtigaas, dawladda waxay siin doontaa cunto iyo agab gargaar dhibbanayaasha. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in waqtigaas, Ananta Madhava, taas oo ah, Sayid Kalki, uu joogi doono Khandagiri, Odisha. Boqor Divyasingha Deva isna isaga la joogi doonaa.

Mahapurush mar kale wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kalpa Tika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାବରେ ସେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଦେଖିବେ,
ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହକୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Anaṃta kesharī swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha deba se je lalāṭakesharī.
Khaṇḍagiri ṭhābare se prabhuṃku dekhibe,
Lalāṭakesharī Dibyasiṃhaku chihnibe.

Macne:
Macnaha, marka Sayid Ananta Keshari, taas oo ah, Sayid Kalki, uu joogo Khandagiri, markaas waqtigaas Divyasingha Deva isna wuxuu joogi doonaa Khandagiri. Khandagiri, Divyasingha Deva wuxuu heli doonaa aragtida (Darshan) Sayidka.

Saddexda aayadood ee kor ku xusan sidoo kale, waxaa laga ogaanayaa in waqtiga ficillada rabbaaniga ah ee Sayid Kalki, Boqorka Puri uu noqon doono Divyasingha Deva.

6.1 Kulanka Sayid Kalki iyo Baladeva oo la kulmaya Divyasingha Deva iyo Boqorro Badan

Bhavishya Malika waxaa ka buuxa dhacdooyin qarsoon iyo wax-sheegyo badan. Wax-sheegyadan waxaa ka mid ah saadaasha boqorro badan oo la kulmaya Sayidka.

Mahapurush wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika in mustaqbalka, uu imaan doono waqti ay askarta shisheeye qabsan doonaan Chhatia, Jajpur ilaa Nilagiri ama Nilachal, Puri. Xitaa ciidanka weyn ee cadowga ayaa soo geli doona gudaha Macbadka Jagannath. Isagoo arkayo waxaas oo dhan oo dhibaato daran ah, Sayidka, oo ku dhex milmay cibaadada, wuxuu xasuusan doonaa Sahadeva, Nakula, Ashwatthama, iyo Shalya. Isla markii Sayidku xasuusto, fikirka iyo cibaadada afartan dagaalyahan oo geesinimo leh, oo khabiir ku ah qaansada, ayaa jabi doona, dhammaantoodna waxay gaari doonaan Chhatia waxayna ku dhici doonaan cagaha Sayidka, iyagoo weydiisanaya amarkiisa. Sayidku wuxuu amri doonaa afartaas inay burburiyaan ciidanka weyn ee Mughal oo ay ilaaliyaan cibaadada. Sida amarka Sayidka, afartaas waxay burburin doonaan askarta Mughal ka dibna waxay ku soo laaban doonaan Sayidka. Waqtigaas, cibaado aan la tirin karin ayaa Sayidka la joogi doona Chhatia, 27 maalmoodna, Chhatia waxay noqon doontaa meesha ay ku kulmaan cibaadada. Waqtigan, boqorrada Ruushka, Nepal, Manipur, Ali, Kujang, Kanika, iwm., waxay la kulmi doonaan Sayidka, waxay heli doonaan Darshankiisa, waxayna isku dhiibi doonaan cagahiisa. Waqtigaas, Sayidku wuxuu dhammaantood u kaxayn doonaa Sealdah si uu uga codsado Sayid Baladeva burburinta ciidanka shisheeye ee hadhay. Sidan, dhammaan boqorrada Sayidka la socda waxay aadi doonaan Sealdah waxayna la kulmi doonaan Baladeva, halkaas oo Arjuna iyo Bhima ay horey u joogi doonaan.

Halkan Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in waqtigan, boqorrada la kulmi doona Sayid Ananta Keshari iyo Sayid Baladeva, Boqorka Puri, Shrikshetra, isna uu joogi doono. Isagoo shaaca ka qaadaya cidda uu noqon doono, Mahapurush wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଯେ ରାଜା ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ତାହାଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗତେ ସ୍ଥିତି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kṣhetre je rājā Dibyasiṃha Deba tāhāṅka saṅgate sthiti.

Macne:
Macnaha, boqorrada helaya Darshanka Sayidka waxaa ka mid noqon doona Boqorka Shrikshetra Puri, Divyasingha Deva.

Doodda kor ku xusan, waxaa sidoo kale cad in Boqorka Puri waqtiga ficillada rabbaaniga ah ee Kalki uu dhab ahaantii noqon doono Shri Divyasingha Deva.

17ka aayadood ee kor ku xusan, waxaa si cad loo caddeeyay in dhammaadka Kaliyuga, marka Sayid Kalki uu dhashay iyo marka ficilladiisa burburinta ay ka dhacaan adduunka oo dhan, marka burburinta kuwa xun, badbaadinta cibaadada, iyo shaqada aasaaska Dharma ay dhacaan, waqtigaas boqor lagu magacaabo Divyasingha Deva ayaa ku fadhiisan doona carshiga Jagannath Puri, wuxuuna sidoo kale noqon doonaa boqorka ugu dambeeya ee booskan ku sii jira.

Shri Divyasingha Deva wuxuu noqon doonaa boqorka ugu dambeeya—waxaa qoray Mahapurush Jagannath Das, oo ka mid ah Panchasakhas, qoraalkiisa Malika, kaas oo aan ku soo sheegnay aayadda koowaad ee cutubkan.

Laakiin waxa xusid mudan ayaa ah in aan kaliya Jagannath Das, laakiin Mahapurush kale oo ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Shishu Ananta Das, isna uu ka qoray mawduucan qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Patta Madana’. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଆଉ ଶାସନ ନ ଥିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dibyasiṃha nṛupa rājattwa karibe āu śāsana na thiba.

Macne:
Macnaha, Boqor Divyasingha Deva wuu xukumi doonaa ka dibna, ma jiri doono xukun boqor kale. Divyasingha Deva wuxuu noqon doonaa boqorka ugu dambeeya. Si kale haddii loo dhigo, Kaliyuga wuu dhammaan doonaa waqtigiisa, ficillada burburinta ee Sayid Kalki-na way dhici doonaan.

6.2 Shri Divyasingha Deva

Xaqiiqda xusid mudan halkan ayaa ah in Mahapurush uusan ku joogsan kaliya inuu yiraahdo magaca Boqorka Puri dhammaadka Kaliyuga wuxuu noqon doonaa Divyasingha Deva; balse, wuxuu ka sheegay waxyaabo badan oo ku saabsan, sida inuu noqon doono boqorka 19aad, inuu noqon doono Divyasingha Deva afraad, inuu wiil la'aan noqon doono, inuu gabdho kaliya yeelan doono, inuusan ku noolaan doonin Puri balse Bhubaneswar, inuusan awoodi doonin inuu guto adeegyada maalinlaha ah ee Sayid Jagannath, inuu xukunka qaadan doono carruurnimadiisa oo uu boqor ahaan doono ilaa da'da weyn, iwm.

Waxyaalahan oo dhan waxay si fiican ugu habboon yihiin Boqorka hadda jira Shri Divyasingha Deva.

7 Calaamadood oo ku saabsan Dhammaadka Kaliyuga ee Macbadka Jagannath

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das iyo Panchasakhas-ka kale waxay ku qoreen meelo badan oo qoraalladooda Malika ah in cibaadada ay ka heli doonaan calaamado badan Macbadka Jagannath kuwaas oo ay ku ogaan doonaan in Kali Yuga uu dhammaaday iyo in Sayid Kalki uu soo degay. Calaamadahan waxaa laga arki doonaa ma aha oo kaliya Macbadka Jagannath laakiin sidoo kale kaniisadaha iyo macbadyada kale ee Hindiya, laakiin ugu horrayn waxay ka muuqan doonaan Macbadka Jagannath ee ku yaal Puri.

Odia Sloka:

ବାରମ୍ବାର ଶ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ଦିରରେ ପୁଣି,
ନାନା ଅଘଟଣ ସତ୍ୟ ମୋ ବାଣୀ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bārambāra shrīmandirare puṇi,
nānā aghaṭana satya mo bāṇī.

Macne:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika in dhammaadka Kali Yuga, noocyo kala duwan oo masiibooyin ah (calaamado xunxun) lagu arki doono marar badan Shrimandir (Macbadka Jagannath) ee ku yaal Puri. Wuxuu intaa ku daray in ereygan uu noqon doono mid run buuxda ah.

7.1 Taariikhda calaamadaha xunxun ee Macbadka Jagannath

Maanta waxaan ogaanay in 15-20kii sano ee la soo dhaafay, calaamado xunxun oo badan lagu arkay Macbadka Jagannath, sida dhagax weyn oo ka soo dhacay "Amla Bedha" ee macbadka sanadkii 1990; dhagxaan dhulka ku dhacaya oo ka yimid dhismayaasha sare ee macbadka, oo ay ku jiraan meesha ugu quduusan, marar badan sanadihii dhowaa; shimbiraha xunxun (kuwa hilibka cuna) oo ku fadhiya calanka macbadka iyo Aruna Stambha ee hortaagan Singhadwara (Albaabka Libaaxa); gorgorrada aan la tirin karin oo macbadka dul wareegaya; saqafka dhagax-gashan (Ratnachandua) oo la dul dhigay ilaahyada oo dab qabsaday; 'Patitapabana Bana' (calanka) oo macbadka korkiisa laga taagay oo dab qabsaday; Neelachakra oo miisaankeedu yahay 2.2 tan, oo laga sameeyay Ashtadhatu (sideed bir), oo ay dabaylo xooggan leexiyeen; 'Patitapabana Bana' oo duulay oo badda ku dhacay intii lagu jiray duufaan daran; laan ka mid ah Kalpavata weyn (geedka banyan) oo ku yaal goobta macbadka oo jabtay; Patitapabana Bana oo gubtay sababtoo ah hillaac; qiyaastii 40 foorno dhoobo ah oo ay burburiyeen dad xunxun oo ku jira jikada quduuska ah ee macbadka; dildilaacyo laga helay Aruna Stambha ee macbadka, alwaaxa gudaha Natamandapa, hoolka hortaagan meesha ugu quduusan (Jagamohana), iyo meel u dhow Singhadwara; dhagaxa 'Padma Pakhuda' (ubaxa lotus) oo ka maqan Ratnasinghasana (carshiga dhagax-gashan); hillaac ku dhacay jikada macbadka; tuugo ka dhacday macbadka habeenkii; korodhka jiirka ee macbadka sababtoo ah tirada cibaadada oo yaraatay intii lagu jiray faafitaanka Corona, taas oo keentay dhibaato iyo carqaladaynta cibaadada macbadka; dhiig daadsan oo laga helay meelo badan oo ay ku jiraan Ratnasinghasana ee macbadka, oo ay ku xigtay qubayska weyn ee cibaadada (Mahasnana) ee Chaturdha Murti (afar ilaah); cirka oo casaan noqday agagaarka macbadka; baalashka oo si lama filaan ah u waayay oo horey loo arki jiray tiro badan oo macbadka ah; isticmaalka taleefannada gacanta inkastoo la mamnuucay macbadka; duubista fiidiyowga khiyaanada leh ee goobta macbadka iyadoo la isticmaalayo kamaradaha basaaska ah ee muraayadaha lagu qariyay; gelitaanka kuwa aan Hinduuga ahayn iyo kuwa aan Sanatanis ahayn ee macbadka inkastoo la mamnuucay; is-dilid ama dhimasho sababo kale awgood oo ka dhacday gudaha macbadka; dhimashooyinka Baisi Pahacha, taas oo ah, 22ka tallaabo ee macbadka; dildilaacyo laga helay Gumuta (qubbada) ee Hasti Dwara (Albaabka Waqooyi) ee macbadka, iwm.

Wax-sheegyo gaar ah oo ku saabsan qaar ka mid ah dhacdooyinkan ayaa laga helaa qoraallada kala duwan ee Malika, halkaas oo si cad loo sheegay in dhacdooyinkan ay dhici doonaan kaliya dhammaadka Kali Yuga waxayna siin doonaan cibaadada calaamadda Sayid Kalki oo dhulka soo degaya. Sidaa darteed, kuwa maanta muujiya shaki iyo rumaysad la'aan ku saabsan dhammaadka Kali Yuga iyo soo degitaanka Sayid Kalki waa inay dib u eegaan fikradahooda.




Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray 'Jaiphula Malika'kiisa:

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁଁ ପଥର,
ଖସୁଥିବ ପୁଣି ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ତୁହି ଦେଖିବୁ ବେନି ନେତ୍ରର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Baḍa deuluṃ pathara,
khasuthiba puṇi tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
tuhi dekhibu beni netrara.

Macne:
Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in waqtigaas la sheegay, dhacdooyinka dhagxaan soo dhacaya marar badan oo ka dhacaya Macbadka Jagannath ee ku yaal Puri ay soo bixi doonaan, kuwaas oo cibaadada ay si cad indhahooda ku arki doonaan.

Haddii aan tixgelinno runta wax-sheeggan, waxaan ogaan doonaa in ka dib markii dhagax miisaankiisu yahay 5-6 tan uu ka soo dhacay "Amla Bedha" ee macbadka Juun 14, 1990, dhacdooyinka noocan oo kale ah ay dhaceen marar badan. Agoosto 13, 1992, laba dhagax oo waaweyn ayaa si toos ah ugu dhacay meesha ugu quduusan ee macbadka. Sidoo kale, Oktoobar 26, 1998, dhagax 15 kg ah ayaa ka soo rogmaday macbadka weyn wuxuuna ku dhacay meel u dhow macbadka Bimala ee gudaha xuduudaha goobta macbadka weyn. Nofeembar 1, 2011, dhagax miisaankiisu yahay qiyaastii 1 tan ayaa ka soo dhacay meel u dhow Dadhinauti ee albaabka galbeed ee Macbadka Jagannath.

Sidoo kale sannadkii 2015, bishii Febraayo 2, dhagax miisaankiisu yahay 30-40 kg ayaa dhulka ka soo dhacay Jalakrida Mandapa oo laga dhisay meel u dhow Macbadka Bimala ee dhinaca galbeed ee Macbadka Jagannath. Marka laga hadlayo waayadan dambe, bishii Ogosto 3, 2022, gabal malaas ah oo miisaankiisu yahay qiyaastii 1.5 kg ayaa ka soo dhacay saqafka meesha ugu quduusan ee macbadkan la dhisay qarnigii laba iyo tobnaad. Bishii Oktoobar 23, 2022, dhagax ayaa ka soo dhacay geeska koonfur-bari ee macbadka meel u dhow Majana Mandapa iyo Charcha Ghara, toddobaad gudahoodna, bishii Oktoobar 28, 2022, dhagax 2-3 kg ah ayaa ka soo dhacay lakabka saddexaad ee ka sarreeya taallada Sakhi oo ku dhow goobta ay ku midoobaan Natamandapa iyo Jagamohana. Xitaa sannadkii 2024, bishii Maarso 9, dhagax miisaankiisu yahay qiyaastii afar kiiloogaraam ayaa ka soo dhacay saqafka Macbadka Baraha oo ku yaal hortiisa Macbadka Bimala ee gudaha Macbadka Jagannath.

Sidan oo kale, wax-sheegiddii qarniyo ka hor uu sameeyay Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ee ku saabsan dhagaxyada si isdaba joog ah uga soo dhacaya Macbadka Jagannath ayaa sidoo kale la xaqiijiyay sannadihii la soo dhaafay.

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ସେହିଦିନଠାରୁ ସଖୀ ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ପଥର ଖସିବ ଯୁଗ ଶେଷ ହେବାପାଇଁ,
ଜାଣି ମୁଁ ଗରୁଡ଼ ଏ ଶକୁନମାନ ଗୁପତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ଏକ ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ତେଇଶି ବରଷେ କଳି ଦେଶ ଛାଡ଼ି ଯିବ।
ଚୁଳରୁ ପଥର ଯେବେ ଖସିବ ସୁତ,
ଖସିଲେ ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ହେବ ଏ କଳି ହତ।
ମୋର ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ତୋ ଦେଉଳ ଚୁଳେ,
ଖସିବ ପଥର ଖଣ୍ଡ ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ତଳେ।
ବତାସରେ ହେ। ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବକ୍ର ହେବ ବାରେ ହେ।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ଅରୁଣ ଖମ୍ବର।
ବତାସର ହେ। ବକ୍ର ହେବ ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ମୋର ହେ।
ତରକିବେ ତେରଜଣ ଜାଣିଥା ନିକର,
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁ ପଥର ଖସି ବାରମ୍ବାର।
ଗୃଧ୍ରପକ୍ଷୀ ମଥାପରେ ବସିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ,
ସେହିଦିନୁ ହତଶିରୀ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Baḍa deularu mora pathara khasiba,
sehidinaṭhāru sakhī bipada paḍiba.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru pathara khasiba yuga sheṣha hebāpāiṃ,
jāṇi mu Garuḍa e shakunamāna gupate deli chetei.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru eka pathara khasiba,
teishi baraṣhe kali desha chāḍi jiba.
Chularu pathara jebe khasiba suta,
khasile aaṃlā beḍhāru heba e kali hata.
Mora baḍa deularu khasiba pathara,
Shrīkṣhetra rājana mora na sebi payara.
Basiba je giddha pakṣhī to deula chule,
khasiba pathara khaṃḍa paḍiba je tale.
Batāsare he. Nīlachakra bakra heba bāre he.
Baḍa deularu mora khasiba pathara,
basiba je giddha pakṣhī Aruṇa khambara.
Batāsara he. Bakra heba Nīlachakra mora he.
Tarakibe terajaṇa jāṇithā nikara,
baḍa deulu pathara khasi bārambāra.
Giddhapakṣhī mathāpare basiṇa je jiba,
sehidinu hatashirī pṛithwī hoiba.

Macne:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu si faahfaahsan uga hadlay calaamado badan oo xunxun oo ku saabsan dhammaadka Kali Yuga oo laga helay Macbadka Jagannath ee Puri qoraallo badan oo Malika ah oo ay ka mid yihiin 'Rukmini Gita', 'Garuda Samvada', 'Hari Arjuna Chautisha', 'Kaliyuga Gita', 'Jai Birachita Chautisha', iyo 'Chakada Madana'. Xaaladdan, wuxuu qoray in marka dhagax ka soo dhaco "Amla Bedha" ee Macbadka Jagannath, cibaadaysatada ay tahay inay fahmaan in dhammaadka Kali Yuga uu aad u dhow yahay iyo in musiibooyin iyo masiibooyin badan ay dhulka ku dhici doonaan mustaqbalka dhow. Wuxuu sheegay in dhicitaanka dhagaxani uu noqon doono calaamad muhiim ah oo qarsoodi ah oo ku saabsan dhammaadka Kali Yuga, iyo 23 sano ka dib dhicitaankiisa, Kali Yuga wuu dhammaan doonaa. Wuxuu intaa ku daray in muddadaas la sheegay, shimbir calaamad xun wadata ay ku fadhiisan doonto dusha sare ee Macbadka Jagannath iyo Aruna Stambha oo ku yaal hortiisa Singhadwara ee macbadka. Ma aha oo kaliya tan, Neelachakra oo 2.2-tan ah oo laga sameeyay Ashtadhatu oo ku taal meesha ugu sarreysa macbadka ayaa qalloocan doonta sababtoo ah xawaaraha sare ee dabaysha.

Akhristayaasha waxaa la yaabi doona inay ogaadaan in dhammaan wax-sheegiddii kor ku xusan ee qarniyo ka hor la qoray ay hadda ka dhaboobeen Macbadka Jagannath. Roobab culus oo da'ayay, marka hore, bishii Juun 14, 1990, dhagax miisaankiisu yahay 5-6 tan ayaa ka soo dhacay "Amla Bedha" ee macbadka. Kadibna shimbir calaamad xun wadata ayaa sidoo kale lagu arkay iyadoo ku fadhida Aruna Stambha.

Waxaa xusid mudan in aan waligeed shimbir lagu arag iyadoo dul heehaabaysa macbadka Jagannath Puri, saynisyahannada iyo cilmi-baarayaashuna waxay ku guuldareysteen inay falanqeeyaan sababta ay tani u dhacday. Laakiin muddadan dhammaadka Kali Yuga, iyadoo la xaqiijinayo wax-sheegiddii Malika, shimbiraha calaamadaha xunxun wata sida gorgorrada/baqalyada ayaa lagu arkay iyagoo ku fadhiya Patitapabana Bana (tiirka calanka) ee dusha sare ee macbadka marar badan. Sannadihii dhowaa, waxa ay isku mid dhacday bishii Ogosto 11, 2018, Diseembar 26, 2018, iyo Luulyo 23, 2020, markii shimbiraha calaamadaha xunxun wata lagu arkay iyagoo ku fadhiya tiirka calanka macbadka. Bishii Diseembar 7, 2021, shimbiraha noocaan ah oo aan la tirin karin ayaa lagu arkay iyagoo koox-koox u dul heehaabaya Macbadka Jagannath. Dhacdo la mid ah ayaa mar kale la arkay bishii Ogosto 17, 2022. Taxanahan halkan kuma joogsan; bilaha May, Juun, iyo Sebtembar ee sannadkii 2023, dadka degan Puri ayaa mar kale markhaati ka ahaa dhacdadan aan wanaagsanayn markii shimbiraha la mid ah lagu arkay iyagoo ku fadhiya tiirka calanka ee dusha sare ee macbadka.

Dhawaan sannadkii 2025 sidoo kale, shimbir ayaa lagu arkay dusha sare ee Aruna Stambha bishii Sebtembar 4, halka gorgorrada lagu arkay iyagoo ku fadhiya dusha sare ee macbadka bishii Sebtembar 2, Sebtembar 16, iyo Oktoobar 14. Bishii Diseembar 9, shimbiraha aan la tirin karin ayaa lagu arkay iyagoo dul heehaabaya Shrimandir.

Intaa waxaa dheer, duufaantii aadka u daran ee 'Fani', oo ku dhufatay xeebta Puri bishii May 3, 2019, waxay qalloocisay Neelachakra oo ku taal dusha sare ee macbadka, taasoo sidaas ku buuxisay wax-sheegid kale oo qarniyo ka hor la qoray taxanahan.

Waxaa xusid mudan in wax-sheegiddan la qoray qarnigii 16-aad aysan ahayn calaamado kaliya oo ku saabsan dhammaadka Kali Yuga, laakiin sidoo kale ay yihiin calaamado ay bixiyeen Panchasakhas oo ku saabsan imaatinka Kalki Avatar, sababtoo ah Kalki Avatar wuxuu imaan doonaa oo kaliya dhammaadka Kali Yuga, sida ay sheegeen dhammaan qoraallada quduuska ah oo ay ku jiraan Bhavishya Malika.





Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।
ହର ଉଦ୍ୟାନରେ ମାୟା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛକୁ ଗ୍ରାସୁଥିବେ,
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବାନା ଝଡ଼ିବ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଲୋଡ଼ିଯିବେ।
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ପରୁ ପତାକା ଛିଡ଼ିଣ ପଡ଼ିବ ସମୁଦ୍ର କୂଳେ,
କଳକୀ ରୂପକୁ ଚିନ୍ତା ପୁଣ ହେବ ଭାବିବେ କ୍ଷୀରାଧି କୂଳେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Nīlachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṃgā adhara,
Jājanagre janma hoibe Biṣhṇu Sharmā gṛihara.
Hara udyānare māyā prakāshiba mlechchaku grāsuthibe,
Nīlachakra bānā jhaḍiba nishchaya bhakataṃku loḍijibe.
Nīlachakra paru patākā chiḍiṇa paḍiba samudra kūle,
Kalki rūpaku chiṃtā puṇa heba bhābibe kṣhīrādhi kūle.

Macne:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu xusay calaamad kale oo ku saabsan dhammaadka Kali Yuga iyo dhalashada Sayid Kalki qoraalladiisa Malika ee 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' iyo 'Kalikalpa Gita'. Wuxuu qoray in ay imaan doonto waqti Patitapabana Bana oo ku dheggan dusha sare ee Neelachakra ee Shrimandir ay ka duuli doonto meesheeda. Mahapurush wuxuu sidoo kale qoray in calanku uu duuli doono oo uu badda ku dhici doono.

Wax-sheegiddan uu sameeyay Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ayaa sidoo kale noqotay mid sax ah. Maalin ka hor inta aysan duufaantii aadka u daran ee 'Fani' ku dhufan xeebta Puri, bishii May 2, 2019, dabaylo xooggan awgood, Patitapabana Bana ayaa duushay oo badda ku dhacday. Dhacdo isku mid ah ayaa mar kale dhacday bishii May 18, 2020, laba maalmood ka hor inta uusan duufaanta 'Amphan' ku dhufan xeebta Odisha.



Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡ଼ି ଆମ୍ଭେ ଯିବୁ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ଲାଗିବ ରତ୍ନଚାନ୍ଦୁଆ ଅଗ୍ନି ସେତେବେଳେ।
ନିଶାକାଳେ ହେ। ନୀଳାଚଳୁ ଚୋରିଯିବ ହେଲେ ହେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Nīlāchala chāḍi āmbhe jibu jetebele,
lāgiba ratnachāṃduā agni setebele.
Nishākāle he. Nīlāchalu chorijiba hele he.

Macne:
Isagoo soo xiganaya erayada Sayidka, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee 'Jai Birachita Chautisha' in marka Sayid Jagannath uu ka tago Nilachala, taas oo ah, Shrimandir, si uu ugu dhasho jidh bini'aadan, waqtigaas daboolka dahabka leh ee ku yaal dusha sare ee saddexda ilaah ee quduuska ah ee Macbadka Jagannath uu dab qabsan doono.

Waxaa xusid mudan in dhacdadii Ratnachandua oo dab qabsaday ay dhacday sannadkii 2004-05, sidaas darteedna wax-sheegiddan Mahapurush ayaa sidoo kale la xaqiijiyay.

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍ପବଟ ଡ଼ାଳ ଛେଦନ ଭୋଇବ ଦେଖିବୁ ତୁ ନୟନରେ,
ଶିବକଳ୍ପ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ତୁରେ।
ଏକ ଦକ୍ଷିଣରେ ନବ ଯେ ଥିବ,
କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଛେଦନ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kalpabaṭa ḍāla chedana hoiba dekhibu tu nayanare,
Shibakalpa bāṇī anyathā nohiba shuṇa Rāmadāsa ture.
Eka dakṣhiṇare naba je thiba,
kalpabaṭa mora chedana hoiba.

Macne:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' 600 sano ka hor in ay imaan doonto waqti laanta Kalpavata oo ku taal gudaha Macbadka Jagannath ay jabi doonto. Wuxuu xitaa ku qoray qoraal kale oo ka mid ah in marka ay jirto 'sagaal' oo ku xigta 'hal', taas oo ah, lambarka 19, markaas oo kaliya ay dhacdadan dhici doonto.

Si sax ah sida ku cad hadalka Mahapurush, wax-sheegiddan ayaa rumowday sannadkii 2019 markii duufaantii aadka u daran ee 'Fani', oo ku dhufatay xeebta Puri bishii May 3, ay waxyeello u geysatay Kalpavata weyn, oo qarniyo jiray, oo ku yaal gudaha macbadka.

Odia Sloka:

ରକ୍ତଛିଟା ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ରତ୍ନସିଂହାସନେ,
ଏହି ଘଟଣା ଘଟିବ ମନେ ରଖ ଦିନେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Raktachīṃṭā paḍiba je ratnasiṃhāsane,
ehi ghaṭaṇā ghaṭiba mane rakha dine.

Macne:
Calaamadaha badan ee uu Mahapurush xusay in laga heli doono Macbadka Jagannath ee Puri dhammaadka Kali Yuga, mid ka mid ah wuxuu ahaa in lagu arko dhiig ku daatay Ratnasinghasana ee sanamyada quduuska ah.

Wax-sheegiddan uu qoray Mahapurush ayaa sidoo kale noqotay mid run ah. Sannadihii la soo dhaafay, dhiig ayaa ku daatay meelo badan oo macbadka ah, oo ay ku jirto Ratnasinghasana, ka dib markii, sida dhaqanka macbadka ah, Chaturdha Murti lagu daahiriyay iyada oo loo marayo cibaadada Mahasnana (qubayska weyn). Tusaale ahaan, bishii Maarso 6, 2021, dhiig ayaa lagu arkay meel u dhow 'Bhitar Katha' oo ku taal meel ka fog meesha ugu quduusan ee macbadka. Bishii Abriil 18, 2021, isku dhac dhex maray laba askari oo loo xilsaaray ilaalinta macbadka awgeed, dhiig ayaa ku daatay gudaha macbadka. Bishii Maarso 7, 2022, intii lagu jiray 'Abakasha Niti' (cibaadada), dhiig ayaa laga helay meel u dhow 'Bhitar Katha' gudaha Jagamohana ee macbadka (hoolka ku yaal meesha ugu quduusan hortiisa). Kadib bishii Abriil 11, 2022, dhiig ayaa lagu arkay meel u dhow Dhukudi Dwara ee macbadka. Bishii Nofeembar 3, 2022, ka dib cibaadooyinka 'Mangala Alati', 'Mailama', iyo 'Tadapa Lagi', dhiig ayaa lagu arkay meel u dhow 'Bhitar Katha'. Dhacdo noocaan ah ayaa mar kale la arkay toddobaadkii saddexaad ee Janaayo sannadkii 2023. Ka dib markii la soo gabagabeeyay cibaadooyinka 'Abakasha' iyo 'Besa Lagi' ee Sayid Jagannath, dhiig ayaa lagu arkay Shrimandir, taas oo keentay in allabariga (Bhoga) ee Gopalaballabha aan la dhammaystiri karin. Kadib bishii Juun 4, 2023, ka dib markii qubayska rabbaaniga ah (Devasnana) la soo gabagabeeyay, dhiig ayaa lagu arkay Snana Mandapa. Xitaa dhawaan, bishii Oktoobar 13, 2023, muran xooggan oo dhex maray laba adeegayaal ah oo ku saabsan qaybinta Prasad isla markiiba ka dib 'Madhyahna Dhupa' ee Shrimandir, dhiig ayaa ku daatay meesha ugu quduusan ee macbadka. Sannadkii 2024 sidoo kale, subaxnimadii hore ee Abriil 23, ka dib markii Singhadwara la furay, haweeney ka tirsan qoyska adeegayaasha ayaa gacanteeda saartay albaabka macbadka markii farta ay ku burburtay. Natiijadii, dhiig ayaa ka soo baxay farta iyada oo u dhow Dhukudi Dwara oo u dhow Bedha gudaha ah. Xaaladdan oo kale, Mahasnana ee Sayidka Sare ayaa la sameeyay xitaa ka hor Mangala Alati. Sidoo kale, dhacdooyinka dhiigbaxa ee gudaha macbadka ayaa sii socday sannadkii 2025 sidoo kale.

7.2 Dhicitaanka sanamka Sayid Balabhadra intii lagu jiray Rath Yatra 2024.

Intii lagu jiray 11-ka maalmood ee Rath Yatra ee Shri Jagannath Puri, cibaadooyinka 'Adapa Mandapa Bije' waxay bilaabmeen 7 PM fiidnimadii Luulyo 9, 2024. Intii lagu jiray tan, iyadoo sanamka Sayid Balabhadra laga qaadayay gaadhiga ilaa Adapa Mandapa ee Macbadka Gundicha, wuxuu ku dhacay wejigiisa Charamala, taas oo keentay in 12 adeegayaal ah ay sidoo kale dhaawacmaan. Dhacdo noocaan ah oo nasiib darro ah ayaa dhacday markii ugu horreysay taariikhda. Dhicitaanka Sayid Balabhadra ee sidan oo kale waa hubaal calaamad muujinaysa musiibooyin soo socda, kuwaas oo lagu xusay sida soo socota qoraalka 'Niladri' ee la xiriira xeerarka iyo siyaasadaha Shrikhetra, sida uu faray Sayid Brahma iyo Sayid Vishnu.

Odia Sloka:

ମୁରବୈରିଣ ଏତସ୍ୟ ଜଗଦୀଶସ୍ୟ ଭୂପତେହଃ।
ଭଦ୍ରାୟାଶ୍ଚ ବଳସ୍ୟାପି ପ୍ରମାଦାତ୍ ପତନଂ ଯଦି।।
ମହାଭୟଂ ନୃପସ୍ୟାପି ରାଜ୍ୟସ୍ୟାପି ଭବେଦ୍ଧ୍ରୁବମ୍।
ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷଂ ମରଣଂ ଚୈବ ଜାୟତେ ଚ ପୁନଃ ପୁନଃ।।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Murabairiṇa etasya jagadīshasya bhūpatehaḥ.
Bhadrāyāshcha balasyāpi pramādāt patanaṃ yadi.
Mahābhayaṃ nṛipasyāpi rājyasyāpi bhabeddhrubam.
Durbhikṣhaṃ maraṇaṃ chaiba jāyate cha punaḥ punaḥ.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, "Boqorow, haddii si kama' ah Jagannath, Balabhadra, ama Subhadra ay dhacaan, markaas nasiib darro weyn ayaa ku dhici doonta boqorka iyo gobolka. Abaaro ayaa dhici doona, cudurrada faafa ayaa marar badan soo noqnoqon doona."

Dab ka kacay meesha ugu quduusan ee Macbadka Mahakal, Ujjain sannadkii 2024.

Subaxnimadii Isniinta, Maarso 25, 2024, intii lagu jiray xafladda Holi, dab ayaa ka kacay meesha ugu quduusan ee Macbadka Mahakaleshwar ee Ujjain sababtoo ah tuurista 'gulal' (budada midabka leh) intii lagu jiray Bhasma Aarti. Qiyaastii 14 qof, oo ay ku jiraan wadaadka sare iyo adeegayaal kale, ayaa dhaawacyo gubasho ah ka soo gaaray dhacdadan.

7.3 Calaamadaha xunxun ee ugu muhiimsan ee laga helay Macbadka Jagannath sannadkii 2025.

Calaamadaha xunxun ee laga helay Macbadka Jagannath ayaa sii socday sannadkii 2025 sidoo kale. Calaamado badan oo kala duwan, Sayid Jagannath wuxuu cibaadaysatadiisa siiyay digniin ku saabsan musiibooyinka soo socda, qaar ka mid ah calaamadaha ugu muhiimsan ee aan halkan idiinku soo bandhigayno.

(a) Jabitaanka Akshaya Danda bishii Febraayo 14.

Sida maalin kasta, galabnimadii Febraayo 14, 2025 (Jimce), markii adeegaha Chunara uu gaaray Neelachakra oo ku taal dusha sare ee macbadka si uu u beddelo Patitapabana Bana ama calanka ugu muhiimsan, wuxuu arkay Akshaya Danda (ul bambo ah oo xambaarsan Patitapabana Bana) oo jabtay. Ka dib tan, adeegayaasha waxay si uun calanka ugu xireen Neelachakra lafteeda.

Jabitaanka Akshaya Danda ee sidan oo kale wuxuu dhaawacay dareenka cibaadaysatada aan la tirin karin, waxayna ka muujiyeen murugo weyn. Intaa waxaa dheer, cibaadaysato badan ayaa u arkay inay tahay calaamad muujinaysa musiibooyin soo socda mustaqbalka dhow.

(b) Dhiig ku daatay macbadka bishii Febraayo 16; Mahasnana ee ilaahyada oo la sameeyay.

16-ka Febraayo (Axad), ka dib markii la dhammaystiray cibaadooyinka subaxdii sida 'Dwara Phita Niti' iyo 'Mangala Alati', isla markii uu bilowday Darshan-ka Sayid Jagannath, wax yar ka dib, adeegayaashu waxay heleen dhiig dhibco ah oo u dhow aagga 'Bhitar Katha'. Isla markiiba dhacdada ka dib, waxaa loo sameeyay qabanqaabo 'Bada Mahasnana Niti' ee ilaahyada, Darshan-kana sidoo kale waa la joojiyay in muddo ah.

(c) Gorgor la duulaya Patitapabana Bana 13-ka Abriil

13-ka Abriil, 2025, cibaadaysatada Sayid Jagannath ee ku yaalla Shrimandir-ka Puri waxay marqaati ka noqdeen calaamad aan hore loo arag. Abbaaro 5-ta galabnimo, waxaa la arkay gorgor ku wareegaya macbadka isagoo afkeeda ku sidda Patitapabana Bana. Sida wararku sheegayaan, gorgorku wuxuu ku wareegay macbadka ilaa 4-5 jeer kadibna wuxuu u duulay dhanka badda iyadoo ka dulmaraysa iridda galbeed.

Fiidiyowga dhacdadan ayaa si weyn u faafay baraha bulshada. Cibaadaysato badan ayaa ka muujiyay murugo iyo walaac arrintan. Cibaadaysato badan ayaa la xiriiriyeen masiibooyin soo socda, qaar kalena waxay ku tilmaameen calaamad xun oo aan hore loo arag oo uu bixiyay Sayid Jagannath.

(d) Dhiig dhibco ah oo laga helay macbadka 21-ka Abriil iyo Mahasnana-da ilaahyada oo la sameeyay

21-ka Abriil (Isniin) sidoo kale, dhiig dhibco ah ayaa lagu arkay agagaarka Bhitar Katha ee Shrimandir-ka, kadibna Mahasnana-da sanamyada Sayidka ayaa si habboon loo sameeyay sida dhaqanku yahay. Dhanka kale, Darshan-ka Sayidka Sare ee cibaadaysatada ayaa la hakiyay ilaa 1 saac. Sida wararku sheegayaan, dhiig dhibco ah ayaa laga helay gudaha macbadka sababo la xiriira jajabyo muraayad ah oo lugta ka muday askari boolis ah.

(e) Gorgor ku fadhiya dusha Patitapabana Bana 2-da Sebtembar

Gorgor ku fadhiya Patitapabana Bana ee dusha Shrimandir-ka ayaa marar badan la arkay sannadihii la soo dhaafay. Mar kasta oo dhacdo noocan oo kale ah la arko, waxaa laga soo bandhigaa ku dhawaad dhammaan kanaalada wararka deegaanka ee Odisha. 2-da Sebtembar, si sax ah ayay mar kale u dhacday isla waxaas markii markii hore la arkay gorgor ku duulaya agagaarka Neelachakra kadibna in muddo ah ka dib waxaa la arkay isagoo ku fadhiya Patitapabana Bana-da ku dheggan dusha Neelachakra.

(f) Gorgor mar kale ku fadhiya Aruna Stambha 4-ta Sebtembar

Ma aha oo kaliya Macbadka Jagannath, Bhavishya Malika sidoo kale waxay qeexaysaa fadhiisashada gorgor ama shimbir kale oo xun Aruna Stambha oo ku yaalla hortiisa Singhadwara ee macbadka calaamad xun. 4-ta Sebtembar, taas oo ah, laba maalmood ka dib markii gorgorka lagu arkay dusha Shrimandir-ka 2-da Sebtembar, waxaa sidoo kale la arkay gorgor ku fadhiya Aruna Stambha. Dadku waxay tan sidoo kale u arkeen calaamad xun. Dhacdadan awgeed, qaar badan ayaa ku tilmaamay waxsii sheegidda Malika ee 600 oo sano ka hor la qoray inay rumowday.

(g) Gorgor mar kale ku fadhiya dusha macbadka 16-ka Sebtembar

Gudaha 15 maalmood oo kaliya laga bilaabo 2-da Sebtembar, subaxnimadii hore ee 16-ka Sebtembar, waxaa mar kale la arkay gorgor ku fadhiya Patitapabana Bana ee macbadka.

(h) Gorgor mar kale lagu arkay isagoo ku fadhiya Patitapabana Bana ee Shrimandir-ka 14-ka Oktoobar

Gorgor ayaa lagu arkay isagoo ku fadhiya dusha macbadka laba jeer bishii Sebtembar. Taxanuhu halkan kuma istaagin; dadka degan Puri waxay mar kale marqaati ka noqdeen ku celcelinta dhacdadan 14-ka Oktoobar. Fadhiisashada soo noqnoqota ee gorgorka ee dusha macbadka ayaa cibaadaysato badan ay ku tilmaameen calaamad masiibooyin soo socda.

(i) Gorgoro aan la tirin karin oo ku wareegaya dusha Shrimandir-ka 9-ka Diseembar

Sida 7-da Diseembar, 2021, iyo 17-ka Ogosto, 2022, calaamad aad u xun ayaa sidoo kale lagu arkay 9-ka Diseembar, 2025, markii gorgoro aan la tirin karin lagu arkay iyagoo ku wareegaya dusha Macbadka Jagannath. Dhacdadan ayaa soo jiidatay dareenka ma aha oo kaliya cibaadaysatada Puri ama Odisha, laakiin sidoo kale kuwa ku nool meel ka baxsan Odisha. Cibaadaysato badan ayaa dhacdadan ku tilmaamay calaamad muujinaysa dhacdo weyn oo xun oo soo socota mustaqbalka dhow.

7.4 Calaamado xunxun oo laga helay kloostaro iyo macbadyo kale oo muhiim ah sanadka 2025

Dab ayaa ka kacay goobta Macbadka Mahakaleshwar ee Ujjain

5-ta Maajo, 2025, 12-ka duhurnimo, dab ayaa ka kacay qolka xakamaynta ee guddiga xakamaynta wasakhda oo ku yaalla agagaarka Shankh Dwara ee goobta Macbadka caanka ah ee Baba Mahakaleshwar ee Ujjain. Dhacdadan ka dib, argagax ayaa ka dhashay goobta macbadka. Dabka ka dib, gelitaanka cibaadaysatada ee macbadka waa la joojiyay in muddo ah.

Dab ayaa ka kacay goobta Peetha ee Mahapurush Achyutananda Das

Maalintii Devasnana Purnima, 11-ka Juun, 2025, dab ka kacay goobta Nemal Padmabana Peetha ee Mahapurush Achyutananda Das oo ku yaalla Nischintakoili, Cuttack waxaa sidoo kale loo arkay calaamad aad u xun oo ay arkeen cibaadaysatada Malika. Jawiga argagax ayaa ka dhex abuurmay kumanaan cibaadaysato ah markii laambadda lagu hayay goobta ay si lama filaan ah dab u qabsatay, daqiiqado gudahoodna, olol xooggan ayaa bilaabay inuu ka soo baxo.

Geedka Nemal banyan oo maamulka uu jaray

Bilowgii Ogosto, intooda badan jirridaha geedka banyan-ka ee qadiimiga ah ee ku yaalla hortiisa macbadka ee Sadhana Peetha ee qoraaga asalka ah ee qoraallada Malika, awliyada weyn Shri Achyutananda Das, waa la jaray. Sida la sheegay, tallaabadan waxaa qaaday maamulka si loo horumariyo shaqada Achyutananda Das Peetha. Waxaa jiray mucaaradad ballaaran oo ay ka yimaadeen dadka deegaanka iyo wadaaddada, markii ay mudaaharaadeen, kaliya laamo yar oo ka mid ah geedka banyan-ka ayaa la dayay inaan la jarin. Waxaa xusid mudan in Mahapurush Achyutananda Das uu qoray qoraallada Malika isagoo fadhiya hoosta 'Nemal Banyan Tree' isagoo ku jira xaalad feker ah. Jarista geedka Nemal banyan waxaa loo arkay calaamad aad u xun oo ay arkeen cibaadaysatada Malika.

Calanka dahabka ah ee lagu rakibay dusha sare ee Macbadka Mahakaleshwar oo soo dhacay

1-da Juun, 2025, calaamad xun ayaa mar kale laga arkay Macbadka caanka ah ee Mahakaleshwar oo ku yaalla Ujjain. Dhab ahaantii, dabaylo xooggan awgood, calanka dahabka ah ee lagu rakibay dusha sare ee Macbadka Mahakaleshwar ayaa si lama filaan ah u soo dhacay. Wadaadka macbadka ayaa dhacdadan ku tilmaamay arrin walaac leh dhinaca diinta.

Maaskarada Sayid Shiva ee lagu sameeyay Shivalinga-da ee Macbadka Mahakaleshwar oo jabtay oo soo dhacday

Dhacdo gaar ah ayaa ka dhacday 18-ka Ogosto Macbadka Mahakal ee ku yaalla Ujjain. Halkan, sida maalin kasta 8:00 fiidnimo inta lagu guda jiro qurxinta (Shringara) ee Shivalinga-da, maaskaro Sayid Shiva ah ayaa lagu sameeyay Bhang (cajiinka xashiishka). Tiro badan oo Bhang ah ayaa la mariyay inta lagu guda jiray tan. Isla markiiba ka dib, Aarti-da ayaa ku dhowayd inay bilaabato markii Bhang Shringara-da ay jabtay oo soo dhacday. Sanka, dibnaha, iyo hal il ayaa ka jabay maaskarada Sayid Shiva oo soo dhacay.

Muuqaal ka baxsan dabiiciga oo lagu arkay Macbadka Maa Samaleswari

Macbadka caanka ah ee Maa Samaleswari oo ku yaalla Lakhanpur, Jharsuguda, Odisha, habeenkii Khamiista, 21-ka Ogosto, 2025, cibaadaysatada waxay heleen calaamad muujinaysa in Ilaahadda ay ka tagtay sanamkeeda oo ay soo baxday. Markii wadaadku uu furay albaabada macbadka subaxnimadii hore ee 'Mangala Alati', waxaa la arkay raadad cagood oo dibadda u socda, oo lagu rinjiyeeyay Alta (midab casaan ah), oo ay leedahay Ilaahadda, iyo sidoo kale, seeftii (Khanda) iyo saddex-geesoodkii (Trishul) ee ku dhegganaa sanamka Ilaahadda, ayaa laga helay iyagoo dhinac u dhacay.

Sida ku cad 'Bhavishya Malika', weerarkii ugu dambeeyay ee Kali Yuga, ka hor inta aan la aasaasin Dharma, dhammaan Ilaahaddu waxay ka tagi doonaan meelahooda oo ay soo bixi doonaan si ay u qabtaan hawsha baabi'inta kuwa xunxun iyo dembiilayaasha. Dhacdadan waa caddayn in wakhtigaas uu yimid iyo in waxsii sheegiddaas ay rumowday.

Dab ayaa ka kacay sanamyada quduuska ah ee macbadka Brahmagiri Jagannath

Bishii Agoosto 28, 2025, sanamyada Sayid Jagannath, walaalkii weynaa Balabhadra, iyo hooyo Subhadra ayaa dab qabsaday macbadka Brahmagiri Jagannath oo ku yaal tuulada Kandagoda ee hoos timaada degmada Puri ee Odisha. Dhab ahaantii, ka dib markii la dhameeyay cibaadadii fiidkii, wadaaddadii waxay aadeen guryahooda. 8:30 PM, qiiq ka soo baxayay macbadka awgeed, markii albaabka macbadka la jabiyay, waxaa la arkay in sanamyadii gudaha ay gubanayaan. Ka dib markii lala tashaday Muktimandapa, Shaligrama ayaa la caabuday laga bilaabo maalintaas. Ka dib markii la soo ururiyay Daru (alwaax quduus ah) Nabakalebara soo socota, sanamyo cusub ayaa la rakibay.

Calaamad muujinaysa burbur soo socda oo laga helay macbadka Lingaraj, tiirka calanka macbadka ayaa jabay

Isniintii, Sebtembar 15, 2025, sababtoo ah cadhada roobabka waaweyn iyo dabaysha, tiirka calanka macbadka Lingaraj ee qarnigii 11-aad ee ku yaal Bhubaneswar ayaa jabay. Talaadadii subaxdii, ka dib markii ay arkeen adeegayaashu, calan ayaa lagu taagay tiir cusub.

Calanka saaran dusha sare ee macbadka Lingaraj ee ku yaal Bhubaneswar ayaa jeexmay roobab culus iyo duufaan awgeed. Qaybta ugu quduusan ee macbadka waxaa saameeyay hillaac; mugdi ayaa qabsaday macbadka.

Roobab culus iyo duufaan daran oo dhacay Arbacadii, Oktoobar 8, 2025, macbadka Lingaraj ee ku yaal caasimadda Odisha, Bhubaneswar, ayaa noqday dhibane hillaac. Tani sidoo kale waxay ahayd calaamad muujinaysa dhacdo aan wanaagsanayn. Calanka macbadka ayaa jeexmay duufaanta awgeed, halka kamaradaha CCTV ee macbadka iyo nidaamka iftiinka ay waxyeelo ka soo gaartay hillaaca. Waxaa la soo sheegay in qaar ka mid ah cibaadeystayaasha iyo adeegayaasha macbadka joogay ay sidoo kale la kulmeen shoog koronto.

(j) Markii ugu horreysay taariikhdeeda 500-sano ah, Bal Bhog lama bixin macbadka Banke Bihari ee Vrindavan

Thakur Banke Bihari, oo soo muuqday qiyaastii shan boqol oo sano ka hor, waxaa loo bixiyaa Bhog (cunto quduus ah) afar jeer maalintii. Kahor inta aan la dhigin kursiga Darshan-ka subaxdii, Bal Bhog ayaa la bixiyaa. Kahor inta aan macbadka la xirin galabtii, Raj Bhog ayaa la bixiyaa, marka albaabada la furo fiidkii, Utthapan Bhog ayaa la bixiyaa. Marka albaabada la xiro, Shayan Bhog ayaa la bixiyaa. Qandaraaska diyaarinta Bhog waxaa la siiyaa macmacaanle (Halwai). Macmacaanlaha ayaa diyaariya Bhog-ga oo siiya adeegayaasha, iyaguna waxay bixiyaan Bhog-ga.

Laakiin Diseembar 15, 8:45 AM, albaabada macbadka ayaa furmay, laakiin macmacaanlaha ma iman Bal Bhog. Markii lala xiriiray, waxaa la ogaaday in macmacaanlaha uusan imaanin sababtoo ah lacagta bilaha ah oo aan la bixin. Markii ugu horreysay taariikhdeeda 500-sano ah, wax sidan oo kale ah ayaa dhacay markii Bal Bhog aan loo bixin karin Banke Bihari Ji maalintaas.

7.5 Abaabulka Rath Yatra waqti aan habboonayn

Sida aan dhammaanteen ognahay, Rath Yatra waxaa la abaabulaa sannad kasta Dwitiya Tithi ee Shukla Paksha ee bisha Ashadha. Dhaqankan waxaa had iyo jeer lagu raacay macbadyada kale ee Jagannath ee Hindiya iyo dibadda, oo ay ku jiraan macbadka Jagannath ee Puri. Laakiin sannadihii dhawaa, waxaa jiray marar badan oo ISKCON lagu arkay iyadoo ku xadgudbaysa dhaqankan.
