Arrinkan, ka dib markii ay diidmo ka yimaadeen Maamulaha Guud ee Maamulka Macbadka Shree Jagannath iyo Guddoomiyaha Guddiga Maamulka Macbadka Shree Jagannath, Gajapati Maharaj Dibyasingha Deva, iyo cabasho qoraal ah oo loo gudbiyay xarunta ISKCON ee ku taal Mayapur, horumarro qaar ayaa hubaal ah inay dhaceen, laakiin ISKCON wali waxaa lagu arkaa iyadoo abaabulaysa Rath Yatras aan waqtigeeda ahayn gudaha Hindiya iyo gaar ahaan dibadda.

Taxanahan, ISKCON waxay dhawaan abaabushay Rath Yatra Taiwan bishii Nofeembar 23, 2025, taas oo ay dhaleeceeyeen bulshada Odia ee adduunka oo dhan iyo cibaadeystayaasha Jagannath ee Odisha. Cibaadeystayaashu waxay u arkeen inaysan ahayn oo kaliya xadgudub ka dhan ah xeerarka dhaqanka laakiin sidoo kale waxay u arkeen inay tahay dayacaad amarka Sayid Jagannath laftiisa.


7.6 Khalad ku jira jadwalka Ingiriisiga ee 2026 oo ay daabacday Maamulka Shrimandir

Dhawaan, khalad weyn ayaa ka soo ifbaxay jadwalka Ingiriisiga ee 2026 oo ay soo saartay Maamulka Macbadka Jagannath. Dhab ahaantii, sawirka Ratnabedi ayaa lagu daabacay jadwalka kaas oo isku xigxiga Saddexmidnimada uu yahay mid khaldan. Marka laga eego dhinaca daawadayaasha, sawirka jadwalka, Sayid Jagannath waxaa la dhigay dhanka bidix ee fog, ka dibna Marwo Subhadra, ka dibna walaalkii weynaa Balabhadra, halka isku xigxiga saxda ah, Sayid Balabhadra uu ku jiro dhanka bidix ee fog, ka dibna Marwo Subhadra, ka dibna Shri Jagannath. Taasi waa, khaladku waa in jadwalka, sawirka walaalkii weynaa Balabhadra lagu daabacay meeshii Sayid Jagannath, iyo sawirka Sayid Jagannath oo ku yaal meeshii Sayid Balabhadra. Intaa waxaa dheer, Chakraraj Sudarshan lama siin meel sawirka Ratnasinghasana ee jadwalka. Ma aha oo kaliya, sawirka Rath Yatra ayaa sidoo kale lagu daabacay jadwalka, kaas oo muujinaya gaariga Hooyo Subhadra marka hore, ka dibna gaariga Sayidka Sare Jagannath, iyo ugu dambeyntii gaariga Sayid Balabhadra.

Khaladaadkan awgeed, waxaa jira qanacsanaan la'aan iyo xanaaq ku jira quluubta cibaadeystayaasha. Waxaa sidoo kale jira xanaaq ka dhex jira adeegayaasha Macbadka Jagannath ee ku saabsan dhacdadan. Sida ay qabaan qaar ka mid ah cibaadeystayaasha, tani waa calaamad uu Sayid Jagannath laftiisa bixiyay oo ku saabsan masiibooyinka soo socda iyo isbeddelka nidaamka adduunka ee sanadka cusub.

8 Dhalashada Sayid Kalki – Goorma iyo Xaggee?

Sida ku cad Bhavishya Malika, Sayid Kalki wuu dhashay. Ma aha oo kaliya Sayid Kalki, laakiin dhammaan dagaalyahannadii geesinimada lahaa ee xilligii Mahabharata sida Yudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babruvahana, Ekalavya, Ghatotkacha, Duryodhana, Shakuni, iwm., dhammaantood waxay dib u dhasheen dhulkan. Sida ku cad Malika, markan dhammaan dagaalyahannadan waxay si wadajir ah u dagaallami doonaan dhinaca Dharma oo ka dhan ah Adharma.

Si kastaba ha ahaatee, halkan kuma soo qaadan doono dagaalyahanno kale waxaanan hadalkayaga ku koobi doonaa oo kaliya Parahbrahma Narayana Mahavishnu Sayid Kalki Ram.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯୋଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Nīḷachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṅgā adhara,
yojanagre janama hoibe Biṣṇusharmā gṛuhara.

Macne:
Taasi waa, marka Patitapabana Bana (calanka) ee Macbadka Puri Jagannath uu ka duulo meeshiisa oo uu meel kale aado, isla waqtigaas, Mahaprabhu Kalki wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa Jajanagar ama Jajpur ee Odisha guriga Brahmin oo ammaanaya Sayid Vishnu.

Waa inaan u caddeeyaa akhristayaasha in dhacdadan lagu arkay marar badan Macbadka Jagannath, tusaale ahaan intii lagu jiray sanadihii 1990-91, intii lagu jiray sanadkii 2005, ka dibna intii lagu jiray Duufaanta Fani iyo Duufaanta Amphan ee sanadihii 2019 iyo 2020 siday u kala horreeyaan, iwm.

Mahapurush wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika in dhalashada Mahaprabhu Kalki ay dhici doonto oo kaliya ka dib dhacdadan. Maadaama ay dhacdadan dhacday, waxaa si hubaal ah loo dhihi karaa in Sayid Kalki uu dhashay. Laakiin maadaama ay dhacdadan dhacday dhowr sano oo kala duwan, uma muuqato mid suurtagal ah in la gaaro sanadka gaarka ah ee dhalashada Mahaprabhu iyadoo la adeegsanayo dhacdadan oo kaliya.

Si loo ogaado sanadka uu Mahaprabhu dhashay, waa inaan kaalmo ka helnaa aayado kale oo ka mid ah Malika.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।
କେଉଁ ଦିନ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ କହେ ରାମଦାସ ଯହିଁ,
ବିଶାଖାପାଟଣା ନୃପ ନାଶ ହେଲେ ମୁହିଁ ବିକାଶ ହୁଅଇ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Māgha saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājatwa karibe ehi Ekāmra nagara.
Keuñ dina tumbhe prakāsha hoiba kahe Rāmadāsa yahiñ,
Viśākhāpāṭaṇā nṛpa nāśa hele muhiñ bikāsha huai.

Macne:
Aayadaha kor ku xusan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu u sheegayaa ardaygiisa Ramadas in marka boqor lagu magacaabo Shri Dibyasingha Deb uu xukumo Puri, isla waqtigaas Ananta Keshari, yacni Mahaprabhu Kalki, wuxuu xukumi doonaa Ekamra Nagar ama Bhubaneswar. Waxay noqon doontaa maalinta toddobaad (Saptami Tithi) ee bisha Magha. Mahapurush wuxuu intaa ku daray in waqtigaas, isna uu la joogi doono Sayidka oo uu kori doono. Tani waxay dhici doontaa marka Boqorka Visakhapatnam (yacni, Wasiirka Koowaad ee Andhra Pradesh) uu dhinto.

Haddii aan si dhow u eegno, waxaan ogaan doonaa in calaamaddaas, Mahapurush uu ka hadlayo dhimashada Wasiirka Koowaad ee Andhra Pradesh, Y.S. Rajasekhara Reddy, taas oo ka dhacday shil helikobtar sanadkii 2009. Aayadda kor ku xusan, Mahapurush ma sheegayo sanadka dhalashada Sayidka, laakiin wuxuu si hubaal ah u sheegayaa in agagaarka sanadka 2009, ficillada rabbaaniga ah ee Kalki ay sii socon doonaan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Jaiphula Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଚୈତନ୍ୟ,
ଜନ୍ମିଛନ୍ତି ଆସି ଏହି ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସତଚାଳିଶକୁ ତୁହି ଘେନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Sehi Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Chaitanya,
janmichhanti āsi ehi martyeṇa lo jāiphula,
satachāḷishaku tuhi ghena.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Sayidkii hore u yimid isagoo ah Shri Krishna iyo Shri Chaitanya, wuxuu u iman doonaa adduunka dhimanaya qaabka Kalki lambarka 47.

Waxaa la wada ogsoon yahay in lambarkan 47 uu leeyahay Boqorka Puri, Shri Dibyasingha Deb, kaas oo ku dhaca inta u dhaxaysa sanadihii 2005 iyo 2008.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Kalpatika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଦ୍ରି ଛାଡି ପ୍ରଭୁ ପୀତବାସ,
ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟରେ ଜନ୍ମ ଲଭିବେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।
ଗୁଣରେ ଭେଦି ଭେଦ ଅଙ୍କେ ଜାଣ,
ଦେଲେ ଯେତେ ସାଲ ହେଲା ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ଏହି ସାଲରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିବେ,
ଅଙ୍କ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଯେ କହିଲୁ ଏବେ।
ବିଂଶ ରେବ ଅଙ୍କ ଯେବଣ ଦିନ,
ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଉଦେ ଗଗନ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ଲକ୍ଷଣ ଦେଖିବୁ ଯେବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇଥିବେ ତେବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Nīḷādri chhāḍi prabhu pītabāsa,
martyare janma labhibe abashya.
guṇare bhedi bheda aṅke jāṇa,
dele jete sāla helā pramāṇa.
ehi sālare prabhu janamibe,
aṅka pramāṇa je kahilu ebe.
biṃsha reba aṅka jebaṇa dina,
indu pare bindu ude gagana.
emanta lakṣaṇa dekhibu jebe,
prabhu janma hoithibe tebe.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Mahaprabhu wuxuu hubaal ah inuu dhulka ku soo degi doono ka dib markuu ka tago hoygiisa Jagannath. Isagoo sheegaya sanadka dhalashada Mahaprabhu, Mahapurush wuxuu leeyahay marka aad ku darto labaatanaad (20) lambarka Revati Nakshatra (27), markaa lambarka soo baxa waa in loo fahmo inuu yahay sanadka dhalashada Sayidka. Taasi waa, 20+27 = 47.

Waxaa la wada ogsoon yahay in lambarkan uu yahay sanadka xukunka (Anka) ee Boqorka Puri, Dibyasingha Deb, kaas oo tilmaamaya mid ka mid ah sanadihii u dhexeeyay 2005 iyo 2008.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଥାମାନ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଘଟିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ବାନା ଧରି ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଧରିଣ କଳକୀ ବେଶ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ehi kathāmāna nishchaya ghaṭiba Dibyasiṃha pañchachāḷisha,
shūnya bānā dhari āsibe Shrī Hari dhariṇa Kaḷakī besha.

Macne:
Taasi waa, marka ay tahay sanadka 45-aad ee Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb ee Puri, markaa Sayid Shri Hari wuxuu qaadan doonaa muuqaalka Kalki oo wuxuu ku soo degi doonaa dhulka.

Waxaa xusid mudan in sanadkii 45-aad ee Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb ee Puri uu tilmaamayo sanad u dhexeeya 2005 iyo 2008.

Achyutananda Das Ji wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତି ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ହୋଇବ ସଂସାର,
କାହାରି ନ ଥିବ କୁଳ ବିଚାର।
ବହୁତ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସଂସାରେ ହେବ,
ନିର୍ଭୟ ହୋଇ ଜଣେ କେ ନଥିବ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶେ,
ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କଳକୀ ବେଶେ।
ନାଶିବେ ଯାବତ୍ ଅବନୀଭାର,
ଏଇଠୁ ହୋଇବ ପୃଥବୀ ସଂହାର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ati anyāya hoiba saṃsāra,
kāhāri na thiba kuḷa bichāra.
bahuta utpāta saṃsāre heba,
nirbhaya hoi jaṇe ke nathiba.
Dibyasiṃha deba pañchachāliśe,
āsibe Śrī Hari Kaḷakī beśe.
nāśibe jābat abanībhāra,
eithu hoiba pṛuthabī saṃhāra.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dhammaadka Kaliyuga, waxaa adduunka ka jiri doona cadaalad darro badan. Qofna ma yeelan doono tixgelin qoys ama ma raaci doono Dharma. Meel kasta waxaa ka jiri doona buuq; qofna ma ku qaadan doono maalmihiisa nabad iyo farxad. Waqtigaas, sanadkii 45-aad ee boqorka la yiraahdo Dibyasingha Deb (u dhexeeya 2005-2008), Sayid Shri Hari wuxuu ku soo degi doonaa adduunkan dhimanaya isagoo ah Kalki. Wuxuu ka saari doonaa culayska dembiga dhulka, wuxuu burburin doonaa kuwa xun, wuxuuna baabbi'in doonaa 'Mlechhas'.

Mahapurushas waxay sheegeen dhalashada Sayid Kalki meelo badan oo qoraallo badan ah.

Mid kale oo ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika, ‘kalikalpa Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ବିରଜା ପୀଠ ବୈତରଣୀ ନଦୀ,
ଦଶାଶନୀ ଘାଟ ଉଦୟ ଭେଦି।
ସପତ ମାତୃକା ଟେକିଅଛି ଶିର,
ତାଙ୍କ ଅଧରୁ ବହିବ ରୁଧିର।
ସେହି ସଂକେତକୁ ଜାଣିଥାଅ ରାମ,
କଳଙ୍କି ଉଦୟ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Birajā pīṭha Baitaraṇī nadī,
Dashāshanī ghāṭa udaya bhedi.
Sapata mātṛkā ṭekiachhi shira,
tāṅka adharu bahiba rudhira.
Sehi saṅketaku jāṇithāa Rāma,
Kaḷaṅki udaya nishchaya jāṇa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Wabiga Baitarani wuxuu ku qulqulaa meel u dhow Macbadka Biraja ee ku yaal degmada Jajpur ee Odisha. Meel u dhow Dashashwamedha Ghat ee isla Wabiga Baitarani waxaa ku yaal macbadka Saptamatruka, halkaas oo ay joogaan Toddobada Hooyo Ilaahyo—kuwaas oo kala ah Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, iyo Narasimhi.

Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in marka dhiig ka soo qulqulo dibnaha, yacni afafka, Hooyooyinka Saptamatruka, ay taasi noqon doonto calaamad ay cibaadaysatadu ka fahmi doonaan in Sayid Kalki dhashay ama dhalashadiisu ay aad u dhowdahay.

Waxaa si fiican loo og yahay in dhacdadan ay horey uga dhacday Macbadka Saptamatruka, taasoo caddaynaysa in waxsii sheegidda Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ay run tahay.

Sida ay sheegeen wadaaddada Macbadka Biraja, Jajpur, iyo Macbadka Saptamatruka, iyo dadka deegaanka halkaas, dhiigga ka soo daatay afafka Hooyooyinka Saptamatruka ayaa horey u dhacay sanadkii 2005.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେ ଦିନ ବିରଜା ଆସ୍ଥାନ ଛୋଡ଼ିବ ଭୀମ ସିଂହନାଦ ହେବ,
ଗର୍ଭସ୍ଥିତ ଶିଶୁଭୂମିରେ ପଡ଼ିବେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ଯିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Je dina Birajā āsthāna choḍiba bhīma siṃhanāda heba,
garbhasthita śiśubhūmire paḍibe Bhubaneśwaraku jiba.

Macne:
Halkan, Mahapurush wuxuu ku qeexayaa wakhtiga dhalashada Mahaprabhu luqad astaan ah. Wuxuu qoray in maalinta ay Maa Biraja ka tagto kursigeeda, wakhtigaas ilmo (Sayid Kalki) uu geli doono uurka hooyadiis, kaasoo markii dambe aadi doona Bhubaneswar.

Waxaa si fiican loo og yahay in Maa Biraja ay ka tagtay kursigeeda sanadkii 2004. Bishii Sebtembar 2004, dad siyaasadeed ayaa shirqoolay oo xaday sanamka Maa Biraja (kaasoo lagu rakibay Macbadka Biraja, Jajpur). Qiyaastii 6 bilood ka dib, bishii Maarso 2005, sanamkii Hooyada ayaa ku soo laabtay macbadka.

Lixda aayadood ee kor ku xusan, waxaa si cad loo caddeeyey in Mahaprabhu Kalki dhashay, iyo in wakhtigiisu uu ku beegan yahay sanadaha 2005-2008. Sidaa darteed, ha u caddaato dhammaan akhristayaasha in Kalki avatar uu hadda joogo Dhulka.

8.1 Xaggee ayuu Mahaprabhu ku dhashay?

Sida ku qoran Puranas, meesha uu ku dhashay Sayid Kalki waa tuulada Sambhal. Bhavishya Malika sidoo kale waxay sheegaysaa in Sayid Kalki uu ku dhalan doono tuulada Sambhal. Qof kastaa wuu isku raacsan yahay qodobkan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ କଷଣ ଦେଖି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଭଗବାନ,
କଳିରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇଣ।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ପିତାଙ୍କର ନାମଟି ଅଟଇ,
ମାତା ନାମ ସୁମତି ଯେ କହିଲୁ ବୁଝାଇ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhaktaṅka kaṣaṇa dekhi prabhu bhagabāna,
Kaḷire Sambaḷa nagre janama hoiṇa.
Biṣṇusharmā pitāṅkara nāmaṭi aṭai,
mātā nāma Sumati je kahilu bujhāi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, isagoo arkaya dhibaatada cibaadaysatada xilliga ugu sarreeya ee Kali, Sayid Kalki wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa Sambal Nagar. Magaca aabaha Mahaprabhu wuxuu noqon doonaa Vishnusharma, magaca hooyadiisna wuxuu noqon doonaa Sumati.

Mahapurush wuxuu sii qoray ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ହେବେ ଜନମ,
ଛତିଆ ବଟରେ ଲାଗିବ ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି ସଂଗ୍ରାମ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Sambaḷa nagare prabhu se gupte hebe janama,
Chhatiā baṭare lāgiba dibārātri saṅgrāma.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Mahaprabhu Kalki wuxuu si qarsoodi ah ugu dhalan doonaa Sambal Nagar. Dagaal habeen iyo maalin ah ayaa ka dillaaci doona Chhatia Bata.

Achyutananda Das Ji wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାରୁ ମହରଗ ରାଜକର ଆସି ଘରେ ଘରେ ପଶିଯିବ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ କଳ୍କୀ ଉଦୟ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Māru maharaga rājakara āsi ghare ghare paśijiba,
bāraṇa beḷare Sambaḷanagre Kaḷkī udaya hoiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, marka dhimashada, gaajada, iyo canshuuruhu ay galaan guri kasta oo buuq uu meel kasta ku faafo, Sayid Kalki wuxuu ka soo bixi doonaa Sambal Nagar wakhtigaas ugu dambeeya ee Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush wuxuu sii qoray ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କହନ୍ତି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଭକତ ମୋ ଗଳା ମାଳ,
ଭକତ ନିମନ୍ତେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ ହେବି କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kahanti Achyuta śuṇa Rāmadāsa bhakata mo gaḷā māḷa,
bhakata nimante Sambaḷanagre hebi Kaḷkī abatāra.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳକୀର ସୀମା କାଳ ପୂରିଗଲେ କଳ୍କୀ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଯଶା ପୁରେ,
ସମ୍ବଳ ଗ୍ରାମରେ ଜାତ ହୋଇଥିବେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାର କାଳରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷakīra sīmā kāḷa pūrigale Kaḷkī Biṣṇujashā pure,
Sambaḷa grāmare jāta hoithibe mlechha saṃhāra kāḷare.

Macne:
"Marka wakhtiga Kaliyuga uu dhammaado, Kalki wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa guriga Vishnuyasha ee tuulada Sambal si uu u burburiyo Mlechhas."

Si kastaba ha ahaatee, meesha 'Sambal' ay ku taal waa mawduuc khilaaf ka taagan yahay maskaxda dad badan xitaa maanta. Maqaalkan, waxaan ku xallin doonaa arrintan.

8.2 Maxay Bhavishya Malika dhab ahaantii ka leedahay mawduucan?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Jaiphula Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ବେଳ କାଳ ଜାଣି,
ଓଡିଶାରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନ୍ମିବେ ପୁଣି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କେହି ତାଙ୍କ ମାୟାକୁ ନ ଚିହ୍ନି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Sehi beḷa kāḷa jāṇi,
Oḍishāre prabhu janmibe puṇi lo jāiphula,
kehi tāṅka māyāku na chinhi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, wakhtigaas marka dembigu uu ugu sarreeyo, Sayid Kalki wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa gobolka Odisha. Khiyaanada Sayidka ayaa noqon doonta mid aad u xooggan oo qofna ma awoodi doono inuu aqoonsado.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁରେ କଳ୍କୀର ଜନମ,
ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ରାଜହଂସ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଜନମି ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ଏହା କହିଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ୍ୟ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Utkala bhuiñre Kaḷkīra janama,
grāma nāma Rājahansa.
Kaḷkī janami līḷā prakāśiba,
ehā kahili prakāshya.

Macne:
"Dhalashada Kalki waxay ka dhici doontaa dhulka Utkala (Odisha), tuulo lagu magacaabo Rajahansa. Ka dib dhalashadiisa, Kalki wuxuu muujin doonaa ciyaarihiisa rabbaaniga ah."

Hadda, marka laga hadlayo meesha Odisha uu Sayid Kalki ku dhalan doono, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das qudhiisa ayaa ku qoray qoraal kale oo Malika ah, ‘Bhabishya Jatipataka Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ନଦୀ ତୀର,
ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁବିପ୍ର ଦ୍ୱିଜବର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Oḍiśā deśare gupta Gaṅgā nadī tīra,
Sambaḷa nagre Biṣṇubipra dwijabara.

Macne:
Taasi waa, meesha uu ku dhashay Mahaprabhu waxay ku taallaa meel u dhow bangiyada Gupta Ganga (Wabiga Baitarani) ee Sambal Nagar, Odisha. Isla meeshaas, guriga Brahmin wanaagsan oo ammaanaya Sayid Vishnu, Mahaprabhu Kalki ayaa ku dhalan doona.

Labadan aayadood ee kor ku xusan, waxaa si buuxda u cad in sida ku qoran Bhavishya Malika, Sayid Kalki uu ku dhalan doono Sambal, oo ku taal gobolka Hindiya ee Odisha.

8.3 Xaggee ayuu Sambal ku yaal Odisha?

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Jagannath Das, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalka Malika ‘Bhabishyat Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରରେ,
ଦୁଇ ଜଣ ଅଛନ୍ତି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଘରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Jājapura Sambaḷa nagrare,
dui jaṇa achhanti brāhmaṇa ghare.

Macne:
Aayadda kor ku xusan, Mahapurush wuxuu qoray – “Jajapura Sambala nagrare”. Taasi waa, “Gudaha Sambal Nagar ee Jajpur”. Waxaa cad in Sambal Nagar ay ku taal Jajpur.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷୀର ନଦୀ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଯେ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ଖ୍ୟାତ ଯେ କମଳପୁର ଗୁପ୍ତ ଯେ ସମ୍ବଳ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kṣhīra nadī dakṣhiṇa je Birajā maṇḍala,
khyāta je Kamalapura gupta je Sambaḷa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, koonfurta wabiga Khira iyo waqooyiga Mahanadi waxaa ku yaal aagga macbadka Biraja. Meel u dhow gobolkan Biraja waxaa ku yaal tuulo loo yaqaan Kamalapur. Tani waa waxa si qarsoodi ah loogu yeero tuulada Sambal.

Aayaddan, Mahapurush wuxuu ku shaaciyey in Sambal ay ku taal gobolka Biraja ama degmada Jajpur ee Odisha.

Aan fahanno meesha Sambal ku yaallo Odisha anagoo adeegsanayna aayado kale.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Gurubhakti Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘର,
ଜନମିଣ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଚକ୍ରଧର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Biṣṇusharmā ghara,
janamiṇa tapasyā karibe Chakradhara.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Chakradhara (Sayid Vishnu) wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa guriga cibaadaysanayaasha ku nool Sambal Nagar, Gaya, ka dibna wuxuu samayn doonaa cibaado.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das mar kale wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଜାତ,
ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Biṣṇusharmā gruhe prabhu hoibe jāta,
Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Harihara kṣhetra.

Macne:
"Sayidku wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa guriga Vishnusharma, ee gobolka Harihara ee Sambal Nagar, Gaya."

Sida ku cad labada aayadood ee kor ku xusan, dhalashada Sayidku waxay ka dhici doontaa Sambal Nagar ee Gaya. Hadda, Gaya-daasi waxay noqon doontaa midda ku taal Odisha, waxaana Sayid Krishna laftiisu u sheegay laba iyo tobankiisa Gopas, taas oo Mahapurush Achyutananda Das uu ku soo xigtay qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Biraja Mahatmya’. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାର ସୁତ ନିହାର ବଚନ ଏ ଅଟେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଠାର,
ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥେ ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଗ୍ରାମଟି ସମ୍ବଳ ପୁର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Śuṇa bāra suta nihāra bachana e aṭe Achyuta ṭhāra,
Nābhigayā tīrthe Harihara kṣhetra grāmaṭi Sambaḷa pura.

Macne:
"Dhageysta wiilal laba iyo toban ah, kani waa ereyga hubaal ah ee ka yimid Achyuta: tuulada Sambalpur waxay ku taal gobolka Harihara ee goobta cibaadada Nabhigaya."

Taasi waa, gobolka Harihara wuxuu ku yaal goobta cibaadada Nabhigaya, taas oo loo yaqaan Sambal.

Doodaha kor ku xusan, waxaan ku aragnay meelo badan in Mahapurush uu ku tilmaamay meesha uu Mahaprabhu ku dhashay inay tahay gobolka Biraja ama goobta cibaadada Gaya (Nabhigaya). Hadda waxaan si sax ah u ogaan doonnaa meesha ay gobolladani ku yaalliin, waxa ay yihiin caqiidooyinka khuraafaadka ah ee la xiriira, iyo waxa ay muhiimaddoodu tahay.

8.3.1 Gobolka Biraja

‘Macbadka Biraja’ waa macbad weyn oo ku yaal bangiyada Wabiga Baitarani ee Jajpur, Odisha, halkaas oo Adi Shakti lagu caabudo qaabka ‘Biraja’. Dhab ahaantii, waagii hore, Brahma Ji laftiisu wuxuu halkan ku sameeyay Yajna (allabari) Brahmakunda, ka dibna Hooyo Parvati waxay ka soo baxday dabka allabariga waxayna Brahma Ji ku amartay inuu ku aasaaso magaceeda ‘Biraja’. Gobolka waxaa sidoo kale loo yaqaan gobolka Biraja ka dib magaca Ilaahadda Biraja.

Waa meeshan ay ku dhacday xuddunta (Nabhi) Ilaahadda Sati, taas oo ka dhigaysa mid ka mid ah 51-ka Shakti Peethas. Kani waa macbadka kaliya ee Ilaahadda lagu caabudo qaabka Mahishasura Mardini oo leh laba gacmood.

Meeshan waxay sidoo kale caan ku tahay Nabhigaya oo loogu talagalay Pinda Daan (allabaryada awoowayaasha). Dhab ahaantii, waa Shakti Peetha-da kaliya ee Pinda Daan lagu sameeyo. Agagaarka Brahmakunda, waxaa jira ceel qoto dheer oo loo yaqaan Nabhikunda, kaas oo dadku biyahiisa ku sameeyaan Pinda Daan awoowayaashooda. Meeshan waxaa sidoo kale lagu xusay Skanda Purana.

8.3.2 Nabhigaya

Waxaa lagu xusay Puranas in Satya Yuga, uu jiray jinni la odhan jiray Gayasura. Inkasta oo uu ku dhashay qabiil jinni ah, haddana qalbigiisa waxaa dhiirigeliyay Dharma iyo cibaado. Wuxuu ahaa mid aad u diin badan, aad u xoog badan, oo leh jidh weyn. Had iyo jeer wuxuu u shaqayn jiray danta adduunka iyo faa'iidada dadka kale. Waxaa lagu xusay Puranas in jinnigu uu sameeyay cibaado adag, taas oo Sayid Vishnu uu ku farxay, Sayidkuna wuxuu ka codsaday inuu raadsado barako. Gayasura wuxuu codsaday barako gaar ah. Wuxuu yiri, “Ha gaaraan dadku jannada kaliya taabashadayda.” Sayid Vishnu wuxuu siiyay barakadan isagoo arkaya cibaadadiisa xooggan. Laakiin natiijadii, Yamaloka (adduunka dadka dhintay) wuxuu bilaabay inuu cidlo noqdo, sababtoo ah qof kasta oo Gayasura taabto wuxuu si toos ah u aadi jiray jannada. Tani waxay dhibaato weyn ku noqotay ilaahyada iyo Yamraj. Dhammaan ilaahyadu waxay u tageen Saddexmidnimada (Brahma, Vishnu, Mahesh) waxayna ka baryeen inay xal u helaan dhibaatadan. Ilaahyadu waxay ka codsadeen Saddexmidnimada inay joojiyaan Gayasura. Saddexmidnimadu waxay go'aansatay inay joojiyaan Gayasura, kaas oo sababay caqabado ku yimid sharciyada abuurista inkasta oo uu xaq ahaa. Saddexdooduba waxay qaateen qaabka saddex Brahmin ah, waxay u tageen Gayasura, waxayna yiraahdeen waxay rabaan inay sameeyaan Yajna si joogto ah toddoba maalmood, taasna waxay u baahnaayeen meel ballaaran oo quduus ah. Waxay ka codsadeen Gayasura inuu jidhkiisa siiyo Yajna, laakiin ma ahayn inuu dhaqaaqo toddobadan maalmood. Gayasura wuu ogolaaday tan. Gayasura wuxuu madaxiisa ku fidsaday Gaya (Bihar) halkaas oo Sayid Vishnu uu fadhiyay, wuxuu gobolkiisa xuddunta ku dhigay Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha) halkaas oo Brahma Ji uu fadhiyay, wuxuuna cagahiisa ku dhigay Pithapuram (Andhra Pradesh) halkaas oo Sayid Shiva uu fadhiyay, sidaas darteed Yajna wuu bilaabmay. Saddexmidnimadu waxay Yajna-da samaysay si joogto ah toddoba maalmood.

Gayasura wuxuu tirinayay maalmaha isagoo maqlaya diiqu markuu subaxdii ciyo. Isagoo taas og, maalintii ugu dambaysay, Sayid Shiva wuxuu qaatay qaab diiq ah wuxuuna ciyay ka hor intaan habeenku dhammaan. Markuu maqlay codka diiqa, Gayasura wuxuu u maleeyay inay subax tahay wuxuuna dhaqaajiyay jidhkiisa isagoo u malaynaya in toddobadii maalmood ay dhammaadeen. Saddexmidnimadu isla markiiba waxay u muuqatay isaga waxayna tiri in Gayasura uu jabiyay ballankiisii ahaa inuu jidhkiisa dhaqdhaqaaq la'aan ku hayo toddoba maalmood. Waxay yiraahdeen ciqaabta jebinta ballanka waxay ahayd dhimasho. Gayasura oo xaq ah wuxuu aqbalay tan isagoon wax cabasho ah qabin. Saddexmidnimadu waxay ku faraxday diinigiisa waxayna ka codsatay inuu raadsado barako. Kadibna Gayasura wuxuu ka codsaday inay magaciisa lagu xusuusto Saddexmidnimada. Wuxuu ka codsaday inay si joogto ah jidhkiisa ugu qurxiyaan dharka Brahmins. Wuxuu sidoo kale codsaday barako ah in haddii awoowayaasha qof kasta la siiyo allabaryo (Pinda Daan iyo Tarpan) saddexdan meelood, inaysan dib u dhalan.

Isagoo aqbalaya rabitaanka Gayasura, Sayid Vishnu wuxuu qaaday qaabka Gadadhar ee Gaya, Brahma Ji waxaa lagu caabudaa qaabka Girija Devi ee Nabhigaya, iyo Sayid Shiva waxaa lagu caabudaa Pithapuram sida Kukkuteshwar (Sayidka qaaday qaabka diiqa). Sidan oo kale, saddexdan meelood waxay caan ku noqdeen Pinda Daan iyo Tarpan:

  • Shirogaya (Gaya, Bihar)
  • Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha)
  • Padagaya (Pithapuram, Andhra Pradesh)

Caddaynta ah in goobta cibaadada Nabhigaya ay ku taal Jajpur waxaa sidoo kale laga helaa qoraallada Malika ee Panchasakha Mahapurushas.

Qoraalka Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁରଠାରେ ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥ ଏକଥା ଜାଣ ସତ୍ୱର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Jājapurathāre Nābhigayā tīrtha ekathā jāṇa satwara.

Macne:
"Tan ku ogaada hubaal buuxda, goobta cibaadada Nabhigaya waxay ku taal Jajpur."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, isagoo saadaalinaya dhalashadiisa tobnaad qiyaastii 600 oo sano ka hor, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalka Malika ee ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶମ ଜନମ ମୋହର ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଯାଜପୁର ନାଭିଗୟା ହରିହର ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Daśama janama mohara hoiba prakāśa,
Jājapura Nābhigayā Harihara khyāta.

Macne:
"Dhalashadayda tobnaad waxay ka muuqan doontaa gobolka caanka ah ee Harihara ee Nabhigaya, Jajpur."

Aayaddan sidoo kale, waxaa cad in goobta cibaadada Nabhigaya ay ku taal Jajpur.

Mahapurush wuxuu arrintan ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ପଞ୍ଚକୋଶ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ନାଭିଗୟା ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘରେ ହେବ ଖେଳ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Jājapura pañchakośa Birajā maṇḍala,
Nābhigayā Biṣṇusharmā ghare heba kheḷa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, gobolka Biraja wuxuu ku yaal gudaha shan-kosha oo u dhow Jajpur. Sayid Kalki wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa guriga cibaadaysanayaasha Vishnu ee ku nool gobolkaas Biraja, Nabhigaya.

Sidaa darteed, doodda kor ku xusan, waxaa si buuxda u cad in Sambal Nagar, goobta cibaadada Gaya, iyo gobolka Biraja ee Panchasakhas ay ku saadaaliyeen dhalashada Sayid Kalki, ay dhab ahaantii ku yaalliin degmada Jajpur ee Odisha.

Si aan u helno caddayn dheeraad ah oo ku saabsan meesha uu ku dhashay Sayid Kalki, waxaan qaadan doonnaa aayado kale oo lagu xusay Malika.

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଉଶ୍ୱାସୀ ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷkī abatāre Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśi bhaktaṅku uśwāsī dharmaku tumbhe pāḷiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Kalki Avatar wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa gobolka Odisha. Mahaprabhu Kalki wuxuu burburin doonaa kuwa xunxun, wuxuu badbaadin doonaa cibaadaysanayaasha, wuxuuna dib u soo celin doonaa Dharma Dhulka.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, isagoo soo xiganaya Sayidka, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଦେଖିବ ଜାଣ,
ଆମ୍ଭ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ ଯାଜପୁରେଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Indu pare bindu dekhiba jāṇa,
āmbha ābirbhāba Jājapureṇa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, marka dhibic ama xiddig lagu arko dayaxa korkiisa cirka, ogow in muujinta Sayidka Sare ay ka dhici doonto Jajpur.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Jai Birachita Chautisha’, wuxuu ku qoray isagoo si sax ah u soo celinaya ereyada Sayidka laftiisa –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେତେବେଳେ ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡିକରି ଯିବୁ,
ଯାଜପୁର ବିପ୍ର ଘରେ ଯାଇଁ ଜନମିବୁ।
ଜଗିଥିବୁ ହେ। ଯେ ଯେତେ କହିଲେ ନ ଶୁଣିବୁ ହେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Jetebeḷe Nīḷāchaḷa chhāḍikari jibu,
Jājapura bipra ghare jāiñ janamibu.
Jagithibu he. Je jete kahile na śuṇibu he.

Macne:
"Markaad ka tagto Nilachala (Puri), waxaad aadi doontaa oo aad ku dhalan doontaa guriga Brahmin ee Jajpur. Feejignow; ha dhageysan wax kasta oo qof kasta yiraahdo."

Qoraalka Malika ee ‘Tatwabodhini’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହେବେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଭାବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Jājanagre prabhu janama hebe,
janama hoibe brāhmaṇa bhābe.

Macne:
"Sayidku wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa Jajanagar; Wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa Brahmin."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee la yiraahdo ‘Bigyanakalpa’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ମହାପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ,
ଗରୁଡକୁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନି ଲୀଳା ଯେ କରିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Jājanagre Mahāprabhu janama hoibe,
Garuḍaku saṅge gheni līḷā je karibe.

Macne:
"Mahaprabhu wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa Jajanagar; isagoo Garuda la socda, wuxuu samayn doonaa ciyaartiisa rabbaaniga ah."

Aayadaha kor ku xusan, ma jiro shaki dambe oo ah in dhalashada Mahaprabhu Kalki ay hubaal ka dhici doonto Sambal Nagar, goobta xajka ee Nabhigaya, gobolka Biraja, degmada Jajpur, Odisha.

Haddii aan eegno Bhagavata Purana iyo Mahabharata oo la qoray ka hor Bhavishya Malika, waxay sidoo kale tilmaamayaan Jajpur, Odisha oo ku saabsan goobta dhalashada Sayid Kalki.

Waqtiyadii hore, Maharishi Vedavyas wuxuu ku sheegay goobta dhalashada Sayid Kalki inay tahay 'tuulada Sambhal' ee Shrimad Bhagavata Mahapurana. Wuxuu qoray –

Sanskrit Sloka:

संभलग्राममुख्यस्य ब्राह्मणस्य महात्मनः ।
भवने विष्णुयशसः कल्किः प्रादुर्भविष्यति ।।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Śambhalagrāmamukhyasya brāhmaṇasya mahātmanaḥ |
bhavane viṣṇuyaśasaḥ kalkiḥ prādurbhaviṣyati ||

Macne:
"Guriga Brahmin naf-weyn leh, oo ah madaxa tuulada Sambhal, oo lagu magacaabo Vishnuyasha, Sayid Kalki ayaa ka muuqan doona."

Dambe, 'Vanaparva' ee Mahabharata, Maharishi Vyas laftiisu wuxuu wax yar ka beddelay tan wuxuuna u bixiyay goobta dhalashada Sayid Kalki 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama'. Wuxuu qoray –

Sanskrit Sloka:

कल्कि विष्णुयशा नाम द्विजः कालप्रचोदितः ।
उत्पत्स्यते महावीर्यो महाबुद्धिपराक्रमः ।।
सम्भूतः सम्भलग्रामे ब्राह्मणावसथे शुभे ।
मनसा तस्य सर्वाणि वाहनान्यायुधानि च ।।
उपस्थास्यन्ति योधाश्च शस्त्राणि कवचानि च ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kalki Vishnuyashā nāma dwijaḥ kālaprachoditaḥ |
utpatsyate mahāvīryo mahābuddhiparākramaḥ ||
sambhūtaḥ sambhalagrāme brāhmaṇāvasathe shubhe |
manasā tasya sarvāṇi vāhanānyāyudhāni cha ||
upasthāsyanti yodhāshcha shastrāṇi kavachāni cha |

Macne:
Aayadaha kor ku xusan, Maharishi Vyas wuxuu sheegay in Sayid Kalki uu ku dhalan doono 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama', taas oo macnaheedu yahay tuulo Sambhal ah oo 'la aasaasay' ama 'la dhisay', tuulo Sambhal ah oo loo aasaasay sidii gumeysi Brahmins ah oo loogu talagalay samaynta Yajnas iyo cibaadada. Wuxuu qoray in Sayid Kalki uu ku dhalan doono guriga Brahmin wanaagsan oo ku noolaa tuuladan Sambhal oo ammaanay Sayid Vishnu.

Hadda, haddii aan baarno oo aan ka raadino bogagga taariikhda, waxaan ogaan doonaa in degsiimada Brahmins ee loo aasaasay Yajna iyo cibaadada ay ku taal degmada Jajpur ee Odisha.

Xaqiiqdii, magaca Jajpur waxaa la bixiyay qarnigii 10aad ka dib magaca boqorka Somavamsi Jajati (Yayati). Boqor Jajati wuxuu aasaasay magaaladan wuxuuna ka dhigay Abhinav Jajatinagar caasimadiisa. Ka dib, isagoo magaceeda beddelaya, waxaa marka hore laga dhigay Jajanagar ka dibna Jajpur. Boqor Jajati Keshari wuxuu keenay qiyaastii 10,000 oo Brahmin ah oo ka yimid Kannauj ee Uttar Pradesh wuxuuna dejiyay Jajpur, Odisha, si ay u qabtaan toban Ashwamedha Yajnas (Dashashwamedha). Xitaa maanta, Dashashwamedha Ghat oo ku taal bangiyada Webiga Baitarani ayaa naga xusuusinaysa dhacdadan. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, Maharishi Vyas wuxuu tilmaamay gumeysigan Brahmins ee deggan dhinaca bari ee macbudka Biraja ee Jajpur isagoo ugu yeeray 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama' ee 'Vanaparva' ee Mahabharata.

9 Khandagiri – Ma ku taal Bhubaneswar mise meel kale?

Qof kasta oo xiisaynaya Bhavishya Malika ma qabo shaki ku saabsan meesha Mahaprabhu Kalki uu ku dhalan doono. Waxay si fiican u ogyihiin in avatar Kalki uu ku dhalan doono tuulada Sambal ee ku taal Jajpur, Odisha.

Sidoo kale ma qabaan shaki ah in ka dib markuu ku dhasho Jajpur, Sayidku uu degganaan doono meel lagu magacaabo Khandagiri.

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଳିଯୁଗେ କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ହରି,
ଶ୍ରୀ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ehi kaliyuge Kalki abatare Hari,
Shri khaṇḍagirire thibe prabhu bije kari.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, Kaliyuga, Sayid Hari wuxuu degganaan doonaa Khandagiri isagoo ah Kalki incarnation.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୋପନେ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଯେ ଆସି,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ବସି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Gopane Mukunda je asi,
Khandagirire thibe basi.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, Sayidku wuxuu si qarsoodi ah ugu noolaan doonaa Khandagiri.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika, 'Patta Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକତ ସୁମେଳ ହୋଇ ଏକ ଠୁଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଶେ ଯିବେ,
ଅନନ୍ତ ମୂରତି ଦର୍ଶନ ସାରିଣ ସର୍ବେ ସର୍ବଙ୍କୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhakata sumeḷa hoi eka ṭhuḷa Khaṇḍagiri pāshe jibe,
ananta mūrati darshana sāriṇa sarbe sarbaṅku chinhibe.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, meel lagu magacaabo Khandagiri, waxaa jiri doona isu imaatin cibaadaysato ah. Halkaas, cibaadaysatadu waxay heli doonaan darshan (aragti) Sayid Kalki waxayna is aqoonsan doonaan.

Ilaa heerkan, qof kasta wuu isku raacsan yahay, laakiin shaki ayaa ka dhasha dadka ku saabsan meesha Khandagiri ay ku taal. Maanta, iyada oo loo marayo fiidiyowyo iyo PDF-yo qaar, waxaa la samaynayaa sheegashooyin kala duwan oo ku saabsan Khandagiri. Dadka qaar waxay leeyihiin, meeshan lagu magacaabo Khandagiri, oo Sayidku degganaan doono, waxay ku taal Jajpur, Odisha, halka qaar kalena ay leeyihiin Khandagiri waxay ku taal degmada Dhenkanal ee Odisha.

9.1 Xaggee ayay ku taal Khandagiri?

Maqaalkan, aan sheego in magacyadii kor ku xusnaa ee ku saabsan Khandagiri (Jajpur, Dhenkanal), ay labaduba gebi ahaanba khaldan yihiin.

Isagoo sharxaya meesha Khandagiri ay ku taal, mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Basanta Manjari’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମହିମା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଭକ୍ତ ତହିଁ ସେଠାରେ ଅଛଇ।
ଗୁପତ ଲୀଳା ହୋଇବ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ,
ତପସ୍ୟାରେ ରହିଥିବେ ଋଷିଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗରେ।
ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ସେକାଳରେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବ,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସେହି ଭସାଇଣ ଦେବ।
ତ୍ରିଭୁବନ ସ୍ୱର କଥା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ବାରଭୁଜା ଦୁର୍ଗାମାତା ଜଗି ରହିଛଇଁ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ବାବୁ ଶୁଣ ମନଦେଇ,
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଜଗିଅଛି ସେଠାବକୁ ତହିଁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Khaṇḍagiri mahimā kahita nuhañi,
lakṣha lakṣha bhakta tahin seṭhāre achhai.
Gupata līḷā hoiba Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre,
tapasyāre rahithibe ruṣhiṅka saṅgare.
Gupta Gaṅgā sekāḷare bāhara hoiba,
Bhubaneśwaraku sehi bhasāiṇa deba.
Tribhubana swara kathā kahita nuhañi,
bārabhuja Durgāmātā jagi rahichhaiṃ.
Āhuri kathāe bābu śuṇa manadei,
Bhūāsuṇī jagiachhi seṭhābaku tahiñ.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, sharafta Khandagiri waa mid aan la qiyaasi karin. Waxaa jira malaayiin cibaadaysato ah halkaas. Falal rabbaani ah oo qarsoodi ah (leelas) ayaa ka dhici doona Khandagiri. Culimo iyo suufiyo badan ayaa halkaas ku mashquulsan cibaado. Webiga qarsoon (Gupta Ganga) ayaa halkaas ka socda, kaas oo sababi doona burbur Bhubaneswar xilliga falka burburinta Kalki. Sharafta Bhubaneswaras sidoo kale waa mid aad u weyn. Halkaas, Hooyo Barabhuji iyo Hooyo Bhuasuni ayaa jooga, kuwaas oo qaban doona hawsha burburinta dhamaadka Kali Yuga.

Shlokada kor ku xusan, Mahapurush wuxuu caddeeyay in Khandagiri ay ku taal Bhubaneswar.

Isagoo sharxaya meesha Khandagiri ay ku taal, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sidoo kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ,
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପକୁ ଧ୍ୟାନ କରନ୍ତି।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହିଯାଏ ଗୁପ୍ତର,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ହୁଏ ସତ୍ୱର।
ଘଟଣା ସ୍ଥାନ ଯେ ଅଟଇ ସେହି,
କଳିର ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ସେଠାହିଁ।
ଉଦୟଗିରି ଯେ ଅପୋଡା ସ୍ଥାନ,
ସେଠାବରେ ବହୁ ସାଧୁ ଆଶ୍ରମ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhubaneśwarare lāgiba goḷa, ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sādhu achhanti, Kaḷkī rūpaku dhyāna karanti.
Kṣhīra dhāra bahijāe guptara, gumphāre bhajana hue satwara.
Ghaṭaṇā sthāna je aṭai sehi, Kaḷira bhārata yuddha seṭhāhiñ.
Udayagiri je apoḍā sthāna, seṭhābare bahu sādhu āśrama.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, waxaa jiri doona dagaal ka dhaca Bhubaneswar, iyo kaynta Ekamra ayaa halkaas ku taal. Kadib Mahapurush wuxuu sheegay in Khandagiri ay sidoo kale halkaas ku taal, halkaas oo culimo iyo awliyo badan oo dhammaystiran ay degganaan doonaan oo ay si joogto ah u xusi doonaan magaca Kalki oo ay ka fekeri doonaan qaabkiisa. Godadkaas Khandagiri, habeen iyo maalin bhajans iyo kirtans ayaa ka dhici doona. Halkaas, webi qarsoon (Kshira Nadi) ayaa sidoo kale socon doona. Khandagiri waxay noqon doontaa "Goobta Dhacdada" (Ghatana Sthana), halkaas oo dagaalka haray ee Mahabharata uu ka dhici doono. Meel u dhow waa Udayagiri, halkaas oo ay ku yaalaan ashramyada awliyo badan oo dhammaystiran.

Shlokada kor ku xusan, Mahapurush wuxuu si cad u sheegay in Khandagiri, halkaas oo awliyo aan la tirin karin ay degganaan doonaan, halkaas oo xuska qarsoon ee magaca Kalki uu ka socon doono godadka, iyo halkaas oo dagaalka haray ee Mahabharata uu ka dhici doono, ay ku taal Bhubaneswar, halkaas oo kaynta Ekamra ay sidoo kale ku taal.

Halkan waxaan jeclaan lahaa inaan dareenka akhristayaasha u jeediyo xaqiiqo muhiim ah. Qoraallada Malika, Bhubaneswar waxaa inta badan loogu yeeraa Ekamra Kshetra, Ekamra Van, ama Ekamra Kanan. Xaqiiqdii, sida ku cad Ekamra Purana ee qarnigii 13aad, waxaa jiray hal geed mangoo ah oo kaliya meel u dhow goobta ilaahadda Lingaraj. Sidaa darteed, aagga Lingaraj waxaa sidoo kale loogu yeeraa Ekamra Kshetra (aagga leh hal geed mangoo ah).

Tani waa sababta Mahapurushas ay marar badan ugu yeereen Bhubaneswar Lingaraj Kshetra (aagga macbudka Lingaraj) ama Ekamra Van Kshetra (aagga geedka mangoo ee keliya).

Kaliya maahan tan, sababtoo ah meesha Bindu Sarovar ay ku taal Bhubaneswar, Panchasakhas qoraalladooda Malika waxay sidoo kale ugu yeereen Bhubaneswar gobolka Bindu Sarovar.

Mahapurush wuxuu qoray isagoo caddeynaya 'Garuda Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ଏ ସବୁ ମୋ ଗୁପ୍ତ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଆଉ ପୃଥ୍ବୀରେ ଯେତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନ ପୁରାଣେ ଅଛି ବର୍ଣ୍ଣନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhubaneśwara je ekāmra kānana e sabu mo gupta sthāna,
āu pruthwīre jete siddha sthāna purāṇe achhi barṇana.

Macne:
Sayid Krishna wuxuu u sheegayaa cibaadaysatihiisa, Garuda, in Ekamra Kanan ee ku taal Bhubaneswar ay tahay meel qarsoon oo Siddha ah.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika 'Chhayalisha Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଏକାମ୍ରରେ,
ରହିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ତିନିଘରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhubaneśwara Ekāmrare, rahibe bhakta tini ghare.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, cibaadaysatada Sayidku waxay joogi doonaan gobolka Bhubaneswar Ekamra.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika 'Chumbaka Malika' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ସୀମା ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ,
ପଞ୍ଚବିଂଶ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବେ ତଟରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhubaneśwara sīmā Khaṇḍagirire, pañchabiṃśha bhakta thibe taṭare.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, Khandagiri waxay ku taal xadka Bhubaneswar halkaas oo 25 cibaadaysato ah ay ka qayb qaadan doonaan ciyaarta rabbaaniga ah.

Mahapurush wuxuu sii qoray 'Chakada Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଗୋଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାର,
ଭାବି କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ନିଜ ନାମ ସ୍ମର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhubaneśwarare goḷa Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāra,
bhābi kahai Achyutī nija nāma smara.

Macne:
Taas oo ah, waxaa jiri doona dagaal ka dhaca aagga Khandagiri ee Bhubaneswar.

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, ayaa ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଠାରେ ଜାଣ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସେ ସରୋବର ପୁଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhubaneśwara ṭhāre jāṇa,
Bindu se sarobara puṇa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, ogow in Bindu Sarovar uu ku yaalo Bhubaneswar.

Mahapurush Yasovanta Das ayaa ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜ ସ୍ଥଳୀ,
ଦୁଇ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକଜଣ ସ୍ତିରୀ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhubaneśwara Liṅgarāja sthaḷī,
dui puruṣha ekajaṇa stirī.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Bhubaneswar waa goobta Lingaraj, taasoo la macno ah in macbadka Lingaraj uu ku yaalo Bhubaneswar.

Haddii aan kaalmo ka helno Google, xitaa markaas waxaan si fudud u ogaan karnaa in haddii ay tahay Khandagiri ama Ekamra Kanan ama Bindu Sarovar ama macbadka Lingaraj, dhammaantood ay ku yaalaan Bhubaneswar lafteeda.

Khandagiri, oo noqon doonta hoyga Sayidka, halkaas oo awliyo badan ay ku xusi doonaan magaca Kalki—si loo sharaxo in Khandagiri ay ku taalo Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Kshetra), Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ayaa mar kale ku qoray ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଦଦେଶେ ଭରତ ପାଦୁକା,
ଛଦ୍ମବେଶୀ ସାଧୁ ପୂଜା କରୁଅଛି ଏକା।
ଗୁପ୍ତଗଙ୍ଗା ବହିଅଛି ଗୁପ୍ତ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଗୁପ୍ତ ବେଶେ ରହିଛନ୍ତି ପୁରି।
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଅଟଇ ଶିବ ଯୋଗ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶୁକପକ୍ଷୀ ପାଇଲା ଯେଉଁଠି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଜ୍ଞାନ।
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଦେବୀ ଅଛି ଜାଣିଥା ସତ୍ୱର,
ସେ ଦେବୀ ଘାତରେ ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ କମ୍ପମାନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Khaṇḍagiri pādadeśe Bharata pādukā, chhadmabeśī sādhu pūjā karuachhi ekā.
Guptagaṅgā bahiachhi gupta Khaṇḍagiri, siddha sādhu gupta beśe rahichhanti puri.
Ekāmra bana aṭai Śiba yoga sthāna, śukapakṣhī pāilā jeuṇṭhi brahmajñāna.
Bhūāsuṇୀ debī achhi jāṇithā satwara, se debī ghātare pruthibī heba kampamāna.

Macne:
Taasi waa, cagaha buurta Khandagiri waxaa ku yaal Bharata Paduka, oo uu cibaadaysanayo awliyo isqarinaya. Isla Khandagiri, webiga Gupta Ganga ayaa si qarsoodi ah u socda. Halkaas (Khandagiri), awliyo badan oo dhammaystiran ayaa ku nool iyagoo isqarinaya. Kaynta Ekamra ayaa halkaas ku taal, taasoo ah goobta Yoga ee Sayid Shiva. Halkaas, shimbirta Shuka ayaa mar heshay Brahma-knowledge. Meel u dhow waxaa ku taal Goddess Bhuasuni, oo qaban doonta shaqada burburinta Dhulka dhammaadka Kali Yuga.

Waxaan jeclaan lahaa inaan akhristayaasha halkan u sheego in mid ka mid ah aayadaha sare, Mahapurush uu yiri Khandagiri waxay ku taallaa aagga kaynta Ekamra oo awliyo badan ay ku samaynayaan cibaado adag Khandagiri. Webina wuxuu halkaas ku socdaa si qarsoodi ah. Aayaddan hadda jirta, Mahapurush wuxuu ku celcelinayaa isla waxaas.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ayaa mar kale ku qoray ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ଲୀଳାମୟଙ୍କର ସତ୍ୟ ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ,
ଲୀଳା କରୁଥିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମାଧବ ସର୍ବେ ଆନନ୍ଦ ହୋଇଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Līḷā prakāśiba līḷāmayankara satya je Ekāmra bana,
līḷā karuthibe Ananta Mādhaba sarbe ānanda hoiṇa.

Macne:
Halkan Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale sheegay in hoyga Sayidka uu yahay Bhubaneswar. Laakiin ma uusan qaadan magaca Bhubaneswar iyo Khandagiri midna. Mahapurush wuxuu isla goobtaas ugu yeeray ‘Ekamra Van’. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in falalka rabbaani ah ee Sayidka (Leelamaya) ay ka soo bixi doonaan aagga Ekamra Van. Isla aagga Ekamra Van, Sayid Ananta Madhava, yacni Kalki, wuxuu qaban doonaa falal rabbaani ah, kuwaas oo aragtidooda iyo maqalkooda ay farxadda cibaadaysanayaasha ka dhigi doonaan mid aan xad lahayn.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ayaa mar kale ku qoray ‘Aagata Bhavishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳା ଧଳା ଅଶ୍ୱ ଉପରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ରହିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନରେ ଯେତେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ଋଷି,
ସକଳେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ନାମକୁ ଘୋଷି।
ସକଳେ ସେବେଳେ ଠୁଳ ଯେ ହେବେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗର,
କପିଳେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଭେଟିଣ ହୋଇବେ ସୂପକାର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷā dhaḷā ashwa upare prabhu hebe bāhāra,
Bhubaneshwarare rahibe prabhu raṅgā adhara.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sthānare jete achhanti ruṣi,
sakaḷe bāhāra hoibe prabhu nāmaku ghoṣi.
Sakaḷe sebeḷe ṭhuḷa je hebe Bindu Sāgara,
Kapileshwarare bheṭiṇa hoibe sūpakāra.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Sayidku wuxuu u bixi doonaa burburin isagoo fuushan faras “Kala-Dhala” ama madow iyo caddaan ah. Intaa ka hor, Sayidku wuxuu joogi doonaa Bhubaneswar. Dhammaan awliyada iyo suufiyada ku samaynaya cibaado adag goobta dhammaystiran ee Khandagiri ayaa ka soo bixi doona godadka wakhtigaas, iyagoo ku dhawaaqaya magaca Sayidka, waxayna isugu imaan doonaan Bindu Sarovar oo ku yaala Bhubaneswar. Kadib, cunto kariyayaashu waxay dhammaantood kula kulmi doonaan macbadka Kapileshwar ee u dhow.

Aayadda sare sidoo kale, Khandagiri waxaa loogu yeeraa meel dhammaystiran oo noqon doonta hoyga awliyo iyo suufiyo badan. Waxaana sidoo kale la yiri haddii ay tahay Khandagiri ama Bindu Sarovar ama Macbadka Kapileshwar, dhammaantood waxay ku yaalaan Bhubaneswar waxayna isku dhow yihiin.

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, markii uu ardaygiisa Baranga Das weydiiyay halka Sayid Kalki uu ku dhalan doono iyo halka uu degganaan doono, wuxuu ku sharaxay qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ହେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ କହିବା ସେ ରଙ୍ଗ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଅବତାର ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିରଜା କ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ଜନମ ଲଭିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମିଶ୍ର ଗୃହେଣ।
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ଗୃହକୁ ଛାଡିବେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଯାଇଁ,
ସିଦ୍ଧଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ସିଦ୍ଧଙ୍କ ସଦନ ରହିବେ ସେ ଭାବଗ୍ରାହୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ସୁବର୍ଣ୍ଣଚ୍ୟୁତ ଗିରିରେ,
ସେହି ସ୍ଥାନେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରିଥିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ଜନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ଅନନ୍ତ ବାସୁଦେବ ବିପ୍ରଲିଙ୍ଗ ଭେଟ ସେ ଯହିଁ ଆଗରେ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ବିଧି ସାରିଣ ସିଦ୍ଧ କେଦାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ସାରିବେ ସମସ୍ତ ଭକତ ମିଳି,
ନିଜ କଳା ଭକ୍ତି କଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବେ ଛାଡିବେ କଳିର ମଳି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Shuṇa he bāraṅga kahibā se raṅga prabhu abatāra sthāna,
Shrī Birajā kṣetre janama labhibe Ananta Mishra gruheṇa.
Janama hoibe gruhaku chhāḍibe tapasyā karibe jāiñ,
Siddhagiri sthāna siddhaṅka sadana rahibe se bhābagrāhī.
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna Ekāmra kānana Subarṇachyuta girire,
sehi sthāne prabhu bije karithibe bhakta jana saṅgatare.
Shrī Ananta Bāsudeba bipraliṅga bheṭa se jahiñ āgare,
Bindusāgare snāna bidhi sāriṇa siddha Kedāra madhyare.
Shrī Bindu Sāgare snāna sāribe samasta bhakata miḷi,
nija kaḷā bhakti kaḷā prakāshibe chhāḍibe kaḷira maḷi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Sayidku wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa guriga cibaadaysane la yiraahdo Ananta Mishra ee Shri Viraja Kshetra (Jajpur). Dhalashada ka dib, Sayidku wuxuu ka tagi doonaa gurigiisa wuxuuna aadi doonaa meel ay ku nool yihiin awliyo dhammaystiran si uu u sameeyo cibaado adag. Mahapurush ma uusan ku joogsan inuu yiraahdo "meel dhammaystiran," wuxuu sidoo kale yiri magaca meeshaas wuxuu noqon doonaa Khandagiri, oo ku yaali doonta Ekamra Kanan. Isla aagga Khandagiri ee Ekamra Kanan, Sayidku wuxuu la degganaan doonaa cibaadaysanayaashiisa. Mahapurush wuxuu yiri macbadka Shri Ananta Vasudev wuxuu halkaas ku jiri doonaa, iyo Linga, yacni macbadka Lingaraj, sidoo kale wuu halkaas ku jiri doonaa. Sayidku wuxuu ku maydhan doonaa Bindu Sagar wuxuuna booqan doonaa macbadka Siddha Kedara (Kedareshwar).

Mahapurush wuxuu sidoo kale yiri dhammaan cibaadaysanayaashu waxay ku maydhan doonaan isla Bindu Sarovar, ka dibna iftiin rabbaani ah ayaa ka muuqan doona cibaadaysanayaasha, dhammaantoodna waxay ka takhalusi doonaan wasakhda Kali Yuga.

Aayadda sare, Sishu Ananta Dasji wuxuu caddeeyay in halka macbadka Shri Ananta Vasudev uu ku yaalo, halka macbadka Kedareshwar uu ku yaalo, halka Lingaraj uu joogo, isla Bhubaneswar (gobolka Ekamra Van), Khandagiri ay ku taalo. Khandagiri waa gobolka awliyada dhammaystiran, Sayidkuna wuxuu degganaan doonaa halkan.

Mid ka mid ah muuqashooyinka Achyutananda Dasji, Saint Hadi Das, ayaa ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦେଇ ଶୁଣ ସୁଜନେ କଳିଯୁଗ ବିଚାର,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ଜନମ ହେବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ଚକ୍ରଧର।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହି ଯାଉଛି ସ୍ଥାନ ଅମରାବତୀ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଗୁପତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି।
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ଲାଗିଛି ରାତ୍ର ଦିନ ମଧ୍ୟରେ,
ଗୁପତେ ଖେଳ ସେ ଖେଳିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେହି ଠାବରେ।
ଝାଡଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ସେ ଠାବ,
ଝଲମଲ ଜ୍ୟୋତି ବାହାରେ ଲୀଳା ଚହଟ ହେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Karṇa dei shuṇa sujane kaḷiyuga bichāra,
Kaḷkī rūpare janama hebe Shrī Chakradhara.
Kṣīra dhāra bahi jāuchhi sthāna Amarābatī,
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna gupate siddha sādhu achhanti.
Gumphāre bhajana lāgichhi rātra dina madhyare,
gupate kheḷa se kheḷibe prabhu sehi ṭhābare.
Jhāḍakhaṇḍagiri gumphā madhyare prabhuṅkara se ṭhāba,
jhalamala jyoti bāhāre līḷā chahaṭa heba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dhammaadka Kali Yuga, Sayid Maha Vishnu wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa Dhulka isagoo ah muuqashada Kalki. Ciyaarahiisa rabbaani ah waxay marka hore ka dhici doonaan aagga webiga Kshira (Vaitarani), Amaravati, yacni Jajpur. Kadib ciyaarahiisa waxay ka dhici doonaan Khandagiri halkaas oo awliyo dhammaystiran ay ku dhex milmi doonaan bhajan-kirtan habeen iyo maalin godadka si qarsoodi ah. Mahapurush wuxuu yiri Sayidku wuxuu degganaan doonaa wuxuuna ciyaarahiisa ku qaban doonaa isla aagga Khandagiri ama Jhadakhandagiri.

Saint Hadi Das ayaa ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଘଟଣା ପ୍ରତିମା ପୁରୁଷ ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜଙ୍କ ପୁର,
ଘୋର କଳି ମହାଭାରତ ହେବ ସେହି ଠାବର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ghaṭaṇā pratimā puruṣha Liṅgarājaṅka pura,
ghora kaḷi mahābhārata heba sehi ṭhābara.

Macne:
Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in "Goobta Dhacdada" ay noqon doonto gobolka Lingaraj ama Bhubaneswar, dagaalka Mahabharata ee soo harayna wuxuu ka dhici doonaa halkaas. Waxaa la og yahay in aan horey u aragnay aayad uu Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ku qoray – “Ghatana sthana je atai sehi, kalira bharata yuddha sethahin”. Achyutananda Dasji wuxuu aayaddan ku qoray Khandagiri qoraalkiisa Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ wuxuuna sheegay in Khandagiri ay tahay “Ghatana Sthana,” halkaas oo dagaalka Mahabharata ee soo haray uu ka dhici doono.

Kadib, Hadi Das wuxuu qoray – Bhubaneswar ama gobolka Lingaraj waa “Ghatana Sthana” dagaalka Kali Mahabharata wuxuu ka dhici doonaa halkaas.

Tani waxay la macno tahay in waxa Achyutananda Das uu ka sheegayo Khandagiri ee ‘Chakada Madana’ ay tahay waxa saxda ah ee muuqashadiisa, Hadi Das, uu ka sheegayo gobolka Lingaraj (Bhubaneswar) ee ‘Kali Chautisha’. Tani waxay caddaynaysaa in labada meelood, yacni Khandagiri iyo Bhubaneswar, ay tilmaamayaan isla meel. Si kooban, Khandagiri waxay ku taal Bhubaneswar.

Fiiro gaar ah – Ilaa hadda aayadaha badan ee sare, waxaan ku barannay in Khandagiri ee Bhubaneswar ay tahay gobolka awliyada dhammaystiran iyo kuwa quduuska ah. Awliyadaas waxay ku dhex milmeen bhajan-kirtan ee Sayid Kalki habeen iyo maalin godadka Khandagiri. Hadda waxaan baran doonaa awliyada iyo suufiyada ugu muhiimsan ee cibaado adag u samayn doona Sayid Kalki ee Khandagiri iyo inta awliyo guud ahaan ay ku samayn doonaan bhajan-kirtan godadka.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Aagata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିକି ଯେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର କେତେକ ଦୂର,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ସାଧୁସନ୍ଥ ନିକର।
ନାରଦ ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଶମ୍ଭୁ ଯେ ଅଗସ୍ତି ଉପଲସ୍ତି,
ଦୁର୍ବାସା କଶ୍ୟପ ଚ୍ୟବନ କୁମ୍ଭଋଷି ସନ୍ତତି।
ପରାଶର ବଶିଷ୍ଠାଦି ହୋ ବିଶ୍ୱାମିତ୍ରଙ୍କୁ ଘେନି,
ଷାଠିଏ ସହସ୍ର ଋଷି ହୋ ଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପୁଣି।
ଦେବଋଷି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଋଷି ଯେ ସର୍ବେ ମେଳି ହୋଇଣ,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ କରନ୍ତି କଳ୍କୀ ନାମ ପୁଣ।
ଦେବତାମାନେ ଯେ ସେଠାରେ ରାତ୍ରେ ଗୋଷ୍ଠୀ କରନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀର ଆଦ୍ୟ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ନିତ୍ୟେ ଜପୁଛନ୍ତି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Khaṇḍagiriki je Bhubaneshwara keteka dūra,
Khaṇḍagiri madhye achhanti sādhusantha nikara.
Nārada Mārkaṇḍa Shambhu je Agasti Upalasti,
Durbāsā Kashyapa Chyabana Kumbharuṣi santati.
Parāshara Bashiṣṭhādi ho Bishwāmitraṅku gheni,
ṣāṭhie sahasra ruṣi ho giri madhyare puṇi.
Debaruṣi Brahmaruṣi je sarbe meḷi hoiṇa,
gumphāre bhajana karanti Kaḷkī nāma puṇa.
Debatāmāne je seṭhāre rātre goṣṭhī karanti,
Kaḷkīra ādya mantraku nitye japuchhanti.

Macne:
Mahapurush wuxuu ku soo xigtay wadahadalka Krishna-Garuda qoraalkiisa Malika. Garuda wuxuu leeyahay gobolka Khandagiri kama fogeyn Bhubaneswar (aad ayuu ugu dhow yahay). Waxaa jira awliyo badan oo ku nool Khandagiri. Halkaas, Narada, Markandeya, Shambhu, Agastya, Upalasti, Durvasa, Kashyapa, Chyavana, “Kumbharushi Santati” oo macnaheedu yahay Dridhasyu, Parashara, Va-shish-tha, Vishwamitra, iwm., oo wadartoodu tahay laba iyo toban awliyo ah oo ay ku jiraan wadar ahaan 60,000 oo awliyo, Devarishis, iyo Brahmarishis ayaa ku dhex milmay magac-bhajan ee Sayid Kalki godadka. Ilaahyadu waxay kulamo ku qabtaan godadkaas habeenkii waxayna maalin walba ku dhawaaqaan mantra Sayid Kalki.

Aayaddan, Garudaji ma uusan ka sheegin oo kaliya awliyada cibaado adag ku samaynaya godadka Khandagiri laakiin wuxuu sidoo kale sheegay in godka Khandagiri uusan ka fogeyn Bhubaneswar, ama si ka sii saxan, uu ku yaali doono meel aad u dhow.

15-ka aayadood ee sare, waa inay u caddaatay akhristayaasha in sida ku cad Bhavishya Malika, Khandagiri—halka Kalki uu degganaan doono, halka awliyo dhammaystiran oo aan la tirin karin ay ku noolaan doonaan, halka bhajan-kirtan ee magaca Kalki uu si joogto ah uga socon doono godadka, halka webi uu si qarsoodi ah u socon doono, halka dagaalka Mahabharata ee soo haray uu ka dhici doono—ay ku taal oo kaliya Bhubaneswar ama gobolka Lingaraj ama gobolka Ekamra Van, meel kale maaha.

10 Ujeedada Kalki Incarnation

Sida aan dhammaanteen ognahay, Sayidku wuxuu Dhulka ku qaataa toban muuqasho si uu u dhiso xaqnimada (Dharma). Haddii ay tahay Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, ama Dvapara Yuga, ujeedada muuqashada Sayidka ee Dhulka waxay ahaanaysaa mid isku mid ah—dhisidda Dharma.

Shrimad Bhagavad Gita, Sayid Krishna wuxuu ku yiri Arjuna –

Sanskrit Sloka:

परित्राणाय साधूनां विनाशाय च दुष्कृताम् ।
धर्मसंस्थापनार्थाय संभवामि युगे युगे ।।

Macne:
Si loo ilaaliyo kuwa wanaagsan (cibaadaysanayaasha), si loo burburiyo kuwa xunxun, iyo si loo dhiso xaqnimada si adag, waxaan is muujiyaa qarni ka dib qarni.

Qoraallada Bhavishya Malika sidoo kale, waxaa lagu xusay in si loo burburiyo kuwa xunxun, loo ilaaliyo cibaadaysanayaasha iyo awliyada, iyo dib u dhiska Dharma, Sayid Maha Vishnu uu Dhulka ku soo degi doono isagoo ah muuqashadiisa tobnaad iyo tan ugu dambaysa, Kalki.

Wadahadalka u dhexeeyay Sayid Krishna iyo saaxiibkiis Arjuna ee Dvapara Yuga waxaa laga soo xigtay qiyaastii 600 oo sano ka hor Kali Yuga, waxaana qoray Mahapurush Achyutananda Das qoraalkiisa Malika 'Chausathi Patala'. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ପାପ ଭାରା ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ସହି ନପାରି,
ମୋ ଆଗରେ କଲା ବେଗେ ଗୁହାରି।
କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ମୁହିଁ ହୋଇବି,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ବିନାଶିବି।
ବସି ନରପତି ହୃଦ ଦେଶରେ
ପ୍ରଜା ପାଳିବି ଉତ୍ତମ ଭାବରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Pāpa bhārā pṛthwī sahi napāri,
mo āgare kalā bege guhāri.
Kaḷkī abatāra muhiñ hoibi,
duṣṭa prāṇīmānaṅku bināshibi.
Basi narapati hṛda deshare,
prajā pāḷibi uttama bhābare.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Dhulka, isagoo aan awoodin inuu qaado culeyska dembiyada dhammaadka Kali Yuga, wuxuu i baryi doonaa. Kadib waxaan qaadan doonaa muuqashada Kalki waxaanan burburin doonaa kuwa xunxun waxaanan badbaadin doonaa cibaadaysanayaasha. Anigoo jooga Hindiya, waxaan maamuli doonaa adduunka oo dhan waxaanan xukumi doonaa dadka si caddaalad ah oo habboon.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraal kale oo Malika ah –

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭୟଙ୍କର ଉତ୍ପାତ ହେବ ଧରିତ୍ରୀ ବକ୍ଷରେ ଜାଣ,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାରି ଭାରା ଉଶ୍ୱାସିବେ ପୁଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mahābhayaṅkara utpāta heba dharitrī bakṣhare jāṇa,
Kaḷkī rūpare mlechha saṃhāri bhārā uśwāsibe puṇa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, waxaa jiri doona kacdoon cabsi leh oo Dhulka ka dhaca. Kalki laftiisa ayaa burburin doona kuwa xunxun wuxuuna ka yareyn doonaa Dhulka culeyska dembiga.

Isagoo soo xiganaya wadahadalka Krishna-Arjuna, Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika –

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳେ ମୁହିଁ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହେବି,
ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିଣ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନମାନଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷi śeṣha beḷe muñhi Kaḷkī rūpa hebi,
santhaṅku rakhiṇa duṣhṭajanamānanku nāśhibi.

Macne:
Shri Krishna wuxuu leeyahay dhammaadka Kali Yuga, waxaan qaadan doonaa muuqashada Kalki. Anigoo ilaalinaya awliyada, dadka quduuska ah, iyo dadka qalbigoodu daahirka yahay, waxaan burburin doonaa dhammaan kuwa xunxun iyo dembiilayaasha.

Isagoo mar kale soo xiganaya ereyada Sayidka, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika 'Chausathi Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ବେଶୀ ପାପ ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପେ ମୁଁ ଉଦୟ ହେବି,
ଓଡିଶା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ମନ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଯାଇ କରିବି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Beśī pāpa jetebeḷe heba Kaḷkī rūpe mu udaya hebi,
Oḍiśā madhyare sthāne sthāne mana parīkṣhā jāi karibi.

Macne:
Sayidku wuxuu leeyahay in waqtiga Kali Yuga, marka dembigu gaaro meeshii ugu sarreysay, markaas waxaan soo bixi doonaa qaabkayga Kalki. Waxaan tijaabin doonaa cibaadaysanayaasha meelo kala duwan oo Odisha ah, waxaan kala saari doonaa xaqa iyo baadilka, oo aan u dhaqmi doonaa si waafaqsan.

Isagoo soo xiganaya ereyada Sayidka, mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Balaram Das, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee 'Kali Aagata Bhavishyanta' –

Odia Sloka:

ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଯେ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ମାତିବେ,
ଖପର କରେ ଘେନିଥିବେ।
ରୋଧିର ବୃଷ୍ଟି ଯେ ହୋଇବ,
କେ ଅବା କାହିଁ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ଯାହାକୁ ରଖିବିଟି ଟିକିଏ ମୁହିଁ,
ସେ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନେ ଥିବେ ରହି।
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଲୋକ ଯେ ନାଶ ହେବେ,
ସନ୍ଥ ଯେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ ରହିବେ।
ଅନେକ ଉତ୍ପାତ ଲାଗିବ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଚହଳ ପଡିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chaṇḍī je Chāmuṇḍā mātibe,
khapara kare ghenithibe.
Rodhira bṛṣṭi je hoiba,
ke abā kāhiñ nāsha jiba.
Jāhāku rakhibiṭi ṭikie muhiñ,
se prāṇīmāne thibe rahi.
Duṣṭa loka je nāsha hebe,
santha je abashya rahibe.
Aneka utpāta lāgiba,
shūnyare chahaḷa paḍiba.

Macne:
Sayidku wuxuu leeyahay in intii lagu jiray aasaaska Dharma dhammaadka Kali Yuga, Hooyo Ashta Chandis iyo Yoginis kale ay bilaabi doonaan burburin ba'an. Waxaa jiri doona roob dhiig ah oo ka imanaya cirka. Waxaa jiri doona khalkhal badan iyo masiibooyin ka iman doona meel bannaan, yacni, hawada sare. Kaliya kuwa aan ilaaliyo ayaa nabad gali doona. Kuwa xunxun waa la burburin doonaa, iyo awliyada iyo dadka quduuska ah waa la badbaadin doonaa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee 'Kali Kalpa Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣhatrīmānanku nikṣhatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dhammaadka Kali Yuga, Sayidku wuxuu qaadan doonaa qaabkiisa Kalki oo wuxuu u safri doonaa adduunka oo dhan oo wuxuu badbaadin doonaa Hooyo Dhulka isagoo burburinaya kuwa xunxun.

Gudaha 'Kali Kalpa Gita' lafteeda, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନିଧନ ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ସଂହାରଣ,
କରିବେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଦେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ମହୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
କଳିକଳ୍ପ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mlechha nidhana duṣhṭa saṃharaṇa,
karibe niśhche deba Kaḷkī Rāma.
Emanta mahī je heba uśwāsa,
Kaḷikalpa kahe Achyuta dāsa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Sayid Kalki wuxuu hubaal badbaadin doonaa Dhulka isagoo burburinaya Mlechhas (dadka aan ilbaxnayn/xunxun) oo baabi'inaya kuwa xunxun.

10.1 Imaanshaha Sayid Kalki

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Nafta Weyn Sishu Ananta Das, wuxuu ka qoray mawduucan qoraalkiisa Malika ee 'Chumbaka Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଆଶ୍ୱାସି ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷkī abatāra Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśhi bhaktaṅku āśwāsi dharmaku tumbhe pāliba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Sayidka Weyn Kalki wuxuu ku dhalan doonaa Odisha. Intaa ka dib, Sayidku wuxuu burburin doonaa kuwa xunxun oo wuxuu ilaalin doonaa cibaadaysanayaasha si uu dib u soo celiyo Dharma.

11 Ficilada Burburka: Bilow iyo Dhammaad Cayiman?

Sida aan ka ognahay tixraacyada Bhabishya Malika, xilligan kala guurka ah ee u dhexeeya Kali Yuga iyo Satya Yuga, waxaa adduunka oo dhan ka dhici doona dhacdooyin burburin ah oo aan hore loo arag hoggaanka Sayid Kalki hoostiisa, kuwaas oo lagu burburin doono dhammaan Mlecchas (dadka aan ilbaxnayn), dembiilayaasha, kuwa xunxun, iyo kuwa aan xaqa ahayn. Bhabishya Malika sidoo kale waxay bixisaa xaqiijin ah in dhammaan cibaadaysanayaasha ay nabad gali doonaan inta lagu jiro dhacdooyinkan oo aysan u baahnayn inay cabsi qabaan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chhayalisha Patala’, wuxuu soo xiganayaa ereyada Sayidka Weyn kaas oo Sayidku ku bixinayo faahfaahinta burburka qayb ahaan ee soo socda (Khanda Pralaya).

Mahapurushku wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

କାଳଭାବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟରେ,
ସୃଷ୍ଟି ନାଶ କରି ଲୀଳା କରେ ପାପ ଭାରେ।
ମାତ୍ର ମହାପ୍ରଳୟକୁ ରହିଥିବୁ ଟାକି,
ସେ କାଳରେ ଚରାଚର ନ ରହିବେ ବାକି।
ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ସହିତ ସଂହାରିଣୀ ଶକ୍ତି,
ଖଣ୍ଡା ଖପର ହାତରେ ବୁଲି ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି।
ରୋଗ ଶୋକ ଦୁଃଖ ଜାଲ ସଂସାରେ ପାତିବେ,
ପର ଆପଣାର ଭେଦ କିଛି ନ ରଖିବେ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ପବନ ଯେ ରୁଦ୍ର ମୂର୍ତ୍ତି ଧରି,
ଉପାଡ଼ିବ ସୁମେରୁ ସହିତ ଯେତେ ଗିରି।
ଚାରିମେଘ ଚାରିଦିଗେ ଆଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ କରିବେ,
ମହାଘୋର ବିପତ୍ତିକୁ ଆଣି ଘଟାଇବେ।
ଅନ୍ଧାର ହୋଇବ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ବଢ଼ିବ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ନକ୍ଷତ୍ର ଉଲକା ଛିଣ୍ଡିବ ନାହିଁ ଆଦି ଅନ୍ତ।
ଭୈରବୀ ରଡ଼ିବେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ କାନ୍ଦିବ ସକଳ,
ପାପମାତ୍ରା ପ୍ରବଳରେ ହେବ ଏହି ଫଳ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kālabhābe mahāshakti khaṇḍa praḷayare,
Sṛṣṭi nāsha kari līḷā kare pāpa bhāre.
Mātra mahāpraḷayaku rahithibu ṭāki,
Se kāḷare charāchara na rahibe bāki.
Chaṇḍī Chāmuṇḍā sahita saṃhāriṇī shakti,
Khaṇḍā khapara hātare buli dibārātri.
Roga shoka duḥkha jāla saṃsāre pātibe,
Para āpaṇāra bheda kichhi na rakhibe.
Aṇachāsha pabana je rudra mūrtti dhari,
Upāḍiba Sumeru sahita jete giri.
Chārimegha chāridige āchhanna karibe,
Mahāghora bipattiku āṇi ghaṭāibe.
Andhāra hoiba pṛthwī baḍhiba utpāta,
Nakṣatra ulakā chhiṇḍiba nāhiñ ādi anta.
Bhairabī raḍibe pṛthwī kāndiba sakaḷa,
Pāpamātrā prabaḷare heba ehi phaḷa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Awoodda Sare ee qaabka Waqtiga (Kala) waxay keeni doontaa burbur qayb ah oo ay burburin doontaa adduunka. Waqtiga burburka, dhammaan noolaha waa la saameyn doonaa. Chandi-Chamunda iyo ciidamada burburka waxay wareegi doonaan habeen iyo maalin iyagoo sita seefo (Khanda) iyo madaxyo (Khapar). Xanuun, silica, cudur, iyo murugo ayaa qabsan doona adduunka oo dhan. Dhammaan kala duwanaanshaha u dhexeeya 'kuwaaga' iyo 'shisheeyaha' waa la waayi doonaa. Afartan iyo sagaalka dabaylood, oo qaadanaya qaabka daran ee Rudra, waxay rujin doonaan dhammaan buuraha oo ay ku jiraan Sumeru weyn. Afarta daruur, oo kala ah Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, iyo Drona, waxay hareereyn doonaan afarta jiho oo ay abuuri doonaan masiibooyin cabsi leh. Madow qoto dheer ayaa qabsan doona Dhulka; qorraxda iyo dayaxa ma soo bixi doonaan. Waqtigaas, meteors ayaa ka soo dhici doona cirka oo noqon doona sababta burburka. Madowgaas, Hooyo Bhairavi waxay si daran u qaylin doontaa oo ay burburin doontaa dembiilayaal aan la tirin karin.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, isagoo soo xiganaya ereyada Sayidka, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

କାହିଁ ଜଳ ରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ଜଗତ ନାଶିବି,
କାହିଁ ପବନ ରୂପରେ ସକଳ ଗ୍ରାସିବି।
କାହିଁ ଯୋଗମାୟା ନେଇ ସଂହାର କରିବି,
କାହିଁ ନିଜେ ବିଶ୍ଵରୂପ ଧାରଣ କରିବି।
କାହିଁ ମଉନ ବ୍ରତ ମୁଁ ଧାରଣ କରିବି,
କାହିଁ କାଳରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ପାପୀଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kāhiñ jala rūpe muhiñ jagata nāshibi,
kāhiñ pabana rūpare sakaḷa grāsibi.
kāhiñ yogamāyā nei saṃhāra karibi,
kāhiñ nije bishwarūpa dhāraṇa karibi.
kāhiñ mauna brata muñ dhāraṇa karibi,
kāhiñ kāḷarūpe muhiñ pāpīṅku nāshibi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Sayid Kalki wuxuu adduunkan ku keeni doonaa burbur weyn qaababkiisa kala duwan. Meelaha qaar, wuxuu qaadan doonaa qaab biyo ah oo wuxuu quusin doonaa wax walba; meelaha kale, wuxuu wax walba ku afuufi doonaa burbur dabayl ah. Mararka qaarkood, wuxuu keeni doonaa burbur isagoo ilaalinaya nidar aamusnaan ah, iyo meelaha kale, wuxuu dili doonaa kuwa xunxun qaabka Waqtiga. Meelaha qaar, wuxuu qaadan doonaa Qaabkiisa Caalamiga ah, iyo meelaha kale, wuxuu burburin doonaa awooddiisa Yogamaya.

Sida ku cad Bhabishya Malika, Sayid Kalki wuxuu burburkan ku keeni doonaa fatahaado biyo ah oo daran, dhul gariir waaweyn, duufaano dabayl ah oo burburin ah, fatahaado dab ah, musiibooyin hawada sare ah, faafitaan cudurro halis ah, iyo dagaal nukliyeer ah oo adduunka ah.

11.1 Dhul Gariir

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ହିମାଳୟ ଏଭରେଷ୍ଟ ଟଳି ଯେ ପଡିବ,
ଯେବେ ଏହି ଧରା ପରେ ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Himālaya Everest ṭaḷi je paḍiba,
Jebe ehi dharā pare bhūmikampa heba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, intii lagu jiray falalka burburka ee Kalki dhammaadka Kali Yuga, dhul gariirro xooggan ayaa ku dhici doona Dhulka ilaa xitaa 'Himalayas' iyo buuraha sida 'Everest' ay burburi doonaan. Cibaadaysanayaasha ayaa nabad gali doona, halka dembiilayaasha la burburin doono.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପେ ପ୍ରକମ୍ପିତ ହୋଇବ ଧରଣୀ,
ମାଟି ତଳେ ଲୁଚିଯିବ କୋଠାବାଡି ପୁଣି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhūmikampe prakampita hoiba dharaṇī,
māṭi taḷe luchijiba koṭhābāḍi puṇi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Dhulka oo dhan wuxuu ku gariiri doonaa dhul gariir, iyo dhul gariirradaas awgood, dhismayaal waaweyn ayaa lagu aasi doonaa ciidda hoosteeda.

Mahapurushku wuxuu ku qoray mid kale oo ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ପାତାଳେ ବାସୁକୀ ଟେକିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ,
ତିନିଥର ଯେ କମ୍ପିବ ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Pātāḷe Bāsukī ṭekiba muṇḍa,
tini thara je kampiba brahmāṇḍa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, culayska xad dhaafka ah ee falalka dembiga ah awgeed dhammaadka Kali Yuga, masaska Vasuki, oo Dhulka madaxiisa ku haya, wuxuu madaxiisa ruxi doonaa marar badan. Sababtaas awgeed, saddex dhul gariir oo waaweyn ayaa ka dhici doona Dhulkan.

Awli weyn oo ku dhashay Odisha qarnigii 20aad, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ ଅପାର,
ନାଶ ହୋଇବେ ନାରୀ ନର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhūmikampa heba apāra,
nāśha hoibe nārī nara.

Macne:
Taasi waa, weerarka ugu dambeeya ee Kali Yuga, waxaa jiri doona dhul gariirro aan la tirin karin kuwaas oo ay ku dhiman doonaan rag iyo dumar aan la tirin karin.

11.2 Fatahaad Biyo ah (Jala Pralaya)

Isagoo ka hadlaya cabsida fatahaadda biyaha, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତକୁ ଛାଡି ଦେଲେ ସବୁ ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଯିବ, ମହାସାଗର ଜଳରେ ଡୁବି ମରୁଥିବ।
ଧରଣୀ ଦେହରେ ଜଳ ମାରିବ ଲହରୀ, ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇଯିବ ଚଉଦିଗ ଭାରି।
ଅଛଇ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ଉତ୍ତର ମେରୁରେ, ତରଳିବ ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କ ରଶ୍ମିରେ।
ରୌଦ୍ରତାପେ ଯେତେବେଳେ ମେଦିନୀ କମ୍ପିବ, ସେତିକିବେଳେ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ତରଳିବ।
ବହିବ ପାଣିର ସୁଅ ସମୁଦ୍ରକୁ ପୁଣ, ଆଟଲାଣ୍ଟିକ ବଢ଼ିବ କାହିଁ କେତେ ଜାଣ।
ସମୁଦ୍ରର ପାଣି ଧୀରେ ବଢ଼ି ବଢ଼ି ଯିବ, ସାଗର ମାତିବ ଶେଷେ ଆଉ ଯାହା ଥିବ।
ଅନାଉ ଅନାଉ କାହିଁ ଜଳ ମାଡିଯିବ, ମାଟିର ଲକ୍ଷେ ଘନ ଫୁଟ ଉପରେ ରହିବ।
ଭୂଗୋଳର ସ୍ଥିତି ବଡ଼ ବିଚିତ୍ର ତୁ ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ରହିଛି ସର୍ବ ଉର୍ଦ୍ଧ୍ଵରେ ତ ପୁଣ।
ସୃଷ୍ଟିର କର୍ତ୍ତା ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତପତି ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ଭୂମିରେ କରିବେ ବୈକୁଣ୍ଠ ସ୍ଥାପନ।
ପାଣିର ଘେରରେ ଆଉ ସବୁ ମହାଦେଶ, ଧୀରେ ଜାଣ ବୁଡିଯିବ ସେ ମହାବିନାଶ।
ଦେଖିବ ଏ ଦୁଇ ଆଖି ସମୟ ଆସିଛି, ସାମନା କରି ପାରିଲେ ସମୟକୁ ଇଛି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bhāratku chāḍidele sabu rāṣṭra jiba, Mahāsāgara jalare ḍubi maruthiba.
Dharaṇī dehare jala māriba laharī, Jalārṇaba hoijiba chaudiga bhāri.
Achai baraphagaḍa uttara merure, taraḷiba dhīre dhīre sūryāṅka raśmire.
Raudratāpe jetebeḷe medinī kampiba, setikibeḷe baraphagaḍa taraḷiba.
Bahiba pāṇira sua samudruku puṇa, Āṭlāṇṭika baḍhiba kāhiñ kete jāṇa.
Samudrara pāṇi dhīre baḍhi baḍhi jiba, sāgara mātiba śeṣe āu jāhā thiba.
Anāu anāu kāhiñ jala māḍijiba, māṭira lakṣhe ghana phuṭa upare rahiba.
Bhūgoḷara sthiti baḍa bichitra tu jāṇa, Bhārata rahichi sarba urdhware ta puṇa.
Sruṣṭira kartā prabhu jagatpati jāṇa, Bhārata bhūmire karibe Baikuṇṭha sthāpana.
Pāṇira gherare āu sabu mahādeśa, dhīre jāṇa buḍijiba se mahābināśa.
Dekhiba e dui ākhi samaya āsichi, sāmnā karipārile samayaku ichhi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, marka laga reebo Hindiya, ku dhawaad dhammaan waddamada waxaa quusin doona biyaha badweynta. Waxaa jiri doona fatahaad biyo ah oo Dhulka ah, oo dhammaan afarta jiho waa la quusin doonaa. Kulaylka qorraxda oo sii kordhaya awgeed, barafka ku yaal Cirifka Waqooyi wuxuu bilaabi doonaa inuu dhalaalo, kaas oo inta badan saameyn doona Badweynta Atlaantik. Heerka biyaha badaha wuxuu sii wadi doonaa inuu kordho, isagoo sababaya in magaalooyinka xeebaha ah la quusiyo. Meelaha qaar, hirarka tsunami oo buuraha la mid ah ayaa soo bixi doona. Juqraafiyada oo dhan waa isbedeli doontaa. Laakiin Sayidka Weyn wuxuu ilaalin doonaa Hindiya, iyo saameynta fatahaaddan biyaha ah waxay ku yaraan doontaa Hindiya. Marka laga reebo Hindiya, dhammaan qaaradaha kale waxay noqon doonaan dhibanayaasha fatahaadda biyaha.

11.3 Fatahaad Dabayl ah (Pavan Pralaya)

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Patta Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମହୀନ ହେବେ ସକଳ ଏ ଜନ ଧର୍ମାଧର୍ମ ନ ରହିବ,
ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ବହିବ ଏମନ୍ତ ପୃଥିବୀ ଟଳଟଳ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dharmahīna hebe sakaḷa e jana dharmādharma na rahiba,
aṇachāśha bāyu bahiba emanta pruthibī ṭaḷaṭaḷa dhruba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dhammaadka Kali Yuga, qof walba wuxuu noqon doonaa mid aan xaq ahayn; qofna ma yeelan doono diin/waajib u hadhay. Tani waxay noqon doontaa sababta Sayidka Weyn Kalki uu u aasaasi doono Dharma. Afartan iyo sagaalka dabaylood waxay u dhici doonaan si ay Dhulka u gariiraan.

Mahapurushku wuxuu ku qoray mid kale oo ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ପବନ ବହି ଅଣଚାଶ,
କ୍ଷିତିକି କରିଦେବ ଧ୍ଵଂସ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Pabana bahi aṇachāśha,
kṣhitiki karideba dhwaṃsa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, intii lagu jiray ciyaarta burburka ee Sayid Kalki, afartan iyo sagaalka dabaylood ayaa dhici doona oo ay burburin doonaan Dhulka.

Awli indhoole ah oo ku dhashay Odisha qarnigii 19aad, Bhima Bhoi, sidoo kale wuxuu ka qoray mawduucan qoraalkiisa Malika. Mahapurushku wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପବନ ବହିବ ପର୍ବତ ଦୋହଲିଯିବ,
ଚାରିମେଘ ମିଳି ବର୍ଷିବେ ଚାରିଦିଗ ଅନ୍ଧାର ଘୋଟିବ।
ମୋ ଭାଇ, ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦେଖାଯିବ ନାହିଁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Aṇachāśha khaṇḍa pabana bahiba parbata dohalijiba,
chārimegha miḷi barṣhibe chāridiga andhāra ghoṭiba.
Mo bhāi, chandra sūrya dekhājiba nāhiñ.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dhammaadka Kali Yuga, waxaa iman doona waqti ay afartan iyo sagaalka dabaylood ay dhici doonaan oo xitaa buuraha iyo kuraha ay gariiri doonaan. Afarta daruur oo kala ah Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, iyo Drona ayaa si wadajir ah u da'i doona. Qorraxda iyo dayaxa lama arki doono cirka, iyo madow qoto dheer ayaa qabsan doona dhammaan afarta jiho.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳଟଳ ହେଉଥିବ ସପ୍ତଦ୍ଵୀପ ମହୀ,
ସେ ବେଳେ ଥଳକୂଳ ରହିବଟି ନାହିଁ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ହୋଇ ପବନ ବହିବ,
ସରକାର ବୁଦ୍ଧିବିଦ୍ୟା ଉଭେଇ ଯିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ṭaḷaṭaḷa heuthibā saptadwīpa mahī,
se beḷe thaḷakūḷa rahibāṭi nāhiñ.
aṇachāśha khaṇଡa hoi pabana bahiba,
sarakāra buddhibidyā ubhei jiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, intii lagu jiray aasaaska Dharma dhammaadka Kali Yuga, waxaa jiri doona fawdo adduunka oo dhan oo ay ku jiraan toddobada qaaradood. Iyagoo ku dhacaya xanaaq aad u sarreeya, afartan iyo sagaalka dabaylood waxay burburin doonaan dhammaan dhismayaasha. Waqtigaas, qofna ma nabad gali doono. Awoodda dawladda iyo xikmaddeeda waxba ma tari doonaan. Kaliya kuwa raaca jidka Dharma oo si buuxda isu dhiibay Sayidka Weyn ayaa heli doona ilaalin.

Odia Sloka:

କର ପତ୍ର ଯୋଡି କହଇ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଗୁରୁପାଦେ ଶିର ଦେଇ,
ପ୍ରଳୟ କାଳର ବାରଣ କହିବା କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ ଯହିଁ।
ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଏମନ୍ତ କାଳ ଯେ ଦିନେ ଆସି ପହଞ୍ଚିବ,
ଦଶ ଗ୍ରାମ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଜଣେକ ରହିବେ ଦେବତାଙ୍କ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ।
ସେ କାଳେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ଭାଦ୍ରବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣିମା ଦିନ,
ଏମନ୍ତ ପବନ ବହିବ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ସକଳ ହୋଇବ ଲୀନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kara patra joḍi kahai Bāraṅga gurupāde śira dei,
praḷaya kāḷara bāraṇa kahibā kaḷi śeṣha heba jahiñ.
Śuṇare Bāraṅga emanta kāḷa je dine āsi pahañchiba,
daśha grāma madhye jaṇeka rahibe debatāṅka ābirbhāba.
Se kāḷe Bāraṅga aṇachāśha bāyu Bhādraba pūrṇimā dina,
emanta pabana bahiba Bāraṅga sakaḷa hoiba līna.

Macne:
Markii uu ardayga Baranga weydiiyay ku saabsan ciyaarta burburka ee Kalki oo dhacaysa dhammaadka Kali Yuga, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika in aadanuhu ay arki doonaan maalin ay hal qof oo kaliya, sida ilaah, uu ka badbaadi doono 10 tuulo. Waqtigaas, afartan iyo sagaalka dabaylood waxay ku dhici doonaan xawaare aad u daran oo ay burburin doonaan dhammaan dhismayaasha. Taasi waxay noqon doontaa taariikhda dayaxa buuxa ee bisha Bhadra.

11.4 Daadadka Dabka (Agni Pralaya)

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee cinwaankiisu yahay 'Gupta Kheda Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଧନ, ସମ୍ପତ୍ତି ଓ ଟଙ୍କା ଯାହା ଅଛି ଜମା,
ସବୁଯିବ ପୋଡି ଭାଇ ନିଆଁର ଗଞ୍ଜମା।
ଚଉଦିଗେ ନିଆଁ କାହୁଁ ଦଗ୍ଧି ଏ ଧରଣୀ,
ଓଦା କଞ୍ଚା ନ ମାନିବ ସବୁ ଖାଏ ଅଗ୍ନି।
ଅଗ୍ନିର କରାଳ ରୂପ ଜୀବାତ୍ମା ଦେଖିବ,
ହାଁ ହାଁ ହୋଇ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଛାଡିଯିବ।
ସମଗ୍ର ବିଶ୍ଵରେ ଧ୍ଵଂସ ଲୀଳୋ ଯେ ଚାଲିବ,
କେଉଁଠି କମ୍ ବେଶୀ ଜାଣେ ତା ଦଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dhana, sampatti o ṭaṅkā jāhā achi jamā,
sabujiba poḍi bhāi niāñra gañjamā.
Chaudige niāñ kāhuñ dagdhi e dharaṇī,
odā kañchā na māniba sabu khāe agni.
Agnira karāḷarūpa jībātmā dekhiba,
hāñ hāñ hoi tāra prāṇa chāḍijiba.
Samagra biśware dhwaṃsa līḷā je chāliba,
keuṇṭhi kam beśī jāne tā daiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, qoob-ka-ciyaarka dabka ayaa lagu arki doonaa dhammaan afarta jiho. Dhulka wuu guban doonaa. Dad badan ayaa dhiman doona. Hantida, maalka, iyo lacagta ayaa guban doona oo dambas noqon doona. Falalkan burburka ah waxay noqon doonaan kuwo caalami ah, meelaha qaarkoodna way ku yaraan doonaan, meelaha kalena way ku badnaan doonaan.

11.5 Musiibo Hawada/Cirka ah

Odia Sloka:

ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ଯାକ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

nānā utpāta jāka antarikhye heba,
bhakta binā anya kehi jāni na pāriba.

Macne:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee 'Chakada Madana' in wakhtiga ugu dambeeya ee Kali Yuga, marka aasaaska Dharma uu socdo, ay jiri doonaan qalalaase badan oo hawada sare ah. Waxaa jiri doona isbeddello dhowr ah oo ku yimaada meerayaasha iyo xiddigaha, iyo sidoo kale meelahooda iyo xawaarahooda. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in marka laga reebo cibaadaysatada, dadka caadiga ah aysan awoodi doonin inay ka ogaadaan arrimahan.

Mahapurush-ku mar kale ayuu arrintan ka qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷବେଳେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ରଣ ଗୋଳ ସେ କାଳେ ହୋଇବ ବହୁତ।
ଚାରିଦିଗମାନଙ୍କରେ ଲାଞ୍ଜାମାନ ପଡି,
ସେ କାଳେ ବହୁତ ଶବ୍ଦ ହେବ ଘଡ଼ଘଡ଼ି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷiyuga śheṣhabeḷe pruthbī utpāta,
raṇa goḷa se kāḷe hoiba bahuta.
chāridigamānaṅkare lāñjāmāna paḍi,
se kāḷe bahuta śhabda heba ghaḍaghaḍi.

Macne:
Taasi waa, wajahadda ugu dambeysa ee Kali Yuga, waxaa jiri doona qalalaase badan iyo dagaallo ba'an oo Dhulka ka dhaca. Waxaa jiri doona dhagxaan cirka ka soo dhacaya/asteroids oo ka soo dhacaya dhammaan afarta jiho, waxaana dhawaaqyo ka imaan doonaan meel bannaan.

11.6 Cudurrada iyo Faafitaanka Cudurrada

Mahapurushes-ku waxay ku qoreen qoraallo dhowr ah oo Malika ah in dhammaadka Kali Yuga, cudurro badan iyo faafitaanka cudurrada ay ku qasbi doonaan bulshada bini'aadamka inay jilba dhigto. Malika waxay si cad u sheegtay in dhakhaatiirtu ay ku guuldarraysan doonaan ogaanshaha iyo daawaynta cudurradaas iyo faafitaanka cudurradaas, dawooyinka ay bixiyaanna ay noqon doonaan kuwo aan waxtar lahayn.

Mahapurushes-ku waxay sidoo kale sheegeen in xaqiiqda, cudurradan iyo faafitaanka cudurrada ay keeni doonaan ciidamada hooyo sida Yogamaya, Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, Dakinis, Pisachinis, iwm. Ciidamadan hooyo waxay ku wareegi doonaan adduunka oo dhan qaabab qarsoon oo ah cudurradan, waxayna gali doonaan jidhka dadka.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Bigyanakalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷ ପଡିବ ଲୋକେ ଯିବେ ବହୁ ମରି,
ଚଉଷଠି ରୋଗ ଭ୍ରମୁଥିବେ ଫେରି ଫେରି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Durbhikṣha paḍiba loke jibe bahu mari,
chauṣhaṭhi roga bhramuthibe pheri pheri.

Macne:
Taasi waa, wakhtiga aasaaska Dharma ee dhammaadka Kali Yuga, waxaa jiri doona abaar iyo biyo la'aan, taasoo keeni doonta dhimashada dad badan. Lixdan iyo afar nooc oo cudurro ah ayaa ku faafi doona adduunka oo dhan.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das mar kale ayuu ku qoray qoraalka Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ବାଡି ବେମାରୀରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Bāḍi bemārīre aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dhammaadka Kali Yuga, cudurro badan ayaa noqon doona sababta dhimashada dad aan la tirin karin.

Mahapurush-ku mar kale ayuu ku qoray ‘Jaiphula Malika’ iyo ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚିହ୍ନା ରୋଗ ଘୋଟିବ,
ବଇଦର ବୁଦ୍ଧି ବଣା ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୌଷଧି କାମ ନ କରିବ।
ଅଚିହ୍ନା ବ୍ୟାଧି ଯେ ଜଗତ୍ରେ ଘୋଟିବ ବଇଦ ବଣା ହୋଇବେ,
ରୋଗ ନ ଚିହ୍ନିଣ ବଣା ହେଉଥିବେ ଅନେକ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ମରିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Achinhā roga ghoṭiba,
baidara buddhi baṇā hoiba lo jāiphula,
mahauṣadhi kāma na kariba.
Achinhā byādhi je jagatre ghoṭiba baida baṇā hoibe,
roga na chinhiṇa baṇā heuthibe aneka prāṇī maribe.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dhammaadka Kali Yuga, waxaa imaan doona cudurro aan la garanayn oo xikmadda dhakhaatiirta ay joogsan doonto. Cudurradan lama aqoonsan doono, dawooyinkuna ma noqon doonaan kuwo waxtar leh daawayntooda, taasoo keeni doonta in dad aan la tirin karin ay dhintaan.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu ku qoray mid kale oo ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ରୋଗ ରୂପେ ଯୋଗମାୟା ଶରୀରେ ଖେଳିବ,
କାହାର ଆୟତ ପୁଣି ସେଠାରେ ନ ଥିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Roga rūpe Yogamāyā sharīre kheḷiba,
kāhāra āyata puṇi seṭhāre na thiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dhammaadka Kali Yuga, Hooyo Yogamaya waxay gali doonta jidhka dadka qaab cudur ah.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu sii qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଦ ମହୌଷଧି କରିବନି କାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ମାଡି ଆସିବଟି ରୋଗ,
ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣିବୁ ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀକୁ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଯୋଗ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Gada mahauṣhadhi karibani kārjya māḍi āsibaṭi roga,
Se kāḷe jānibu alpa dine pruthbīku praḷaya yoga.

Macne:
Taasi waa, marka ay yimaadaan cudurro-faafitaanno noocaas ah oo dawooyinka ay noqdaan kuwo aan waxtar lahayn daawayntooda, markaas waa in la fahmo in wakhtiga burburka Dhulka uu dhow yahay.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ୟାଧି ହୋଇ ପିଶାଚୀ ଆସିବେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ନାଶିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Byādhi hoi piśhāchī āsibe,
prāṇīmānanku gupte nāśhibe.

Macne:
Taasi waa, ciidamada hooyo waxay si qarsoodi ah u gali doonaan jidhka qaab cudur ah waxayna burburin doonaan dad badan. Laakiin arrintan la socota, Mahapurush-ku wuxuu sidoo kale ku xusay qoraalkiisa Malika habka bulshada bini'aadamka ay uga baxsadaan cudurradan iyo faafitaanka cudurrada.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chausathi Patala’:

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମାରେ ଯେଉଁ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ରହିବେ,
ରୋଗ କବଳରେ କେବେହିଁ ସେମାନେ ନ ପଡିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Satya śhānti dayā kṣhamāre jeuñ prāṇī rahibe,
roga kabaḷare kebehiñ semāne na paḍibe.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dadkaas oo qaata fadliyo sharaf leh sida runta, nabadda, naxariista, iyo cafiska dhaqankooda, waxay ka xorow doonaan cudurradan.

11.7 Dagaalkii Adduunka

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗିବ ମହାସମର,
ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅତି ପ୍ରବଳ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ନାରଖାର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Lāgiba mahāsamara,
deśha deśha madhye ati prabala lo jāiphula,
pruthibī hoijiba nārakhāra.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaal weyn ayaa ka dhici doona Dhulka. Waxaa jiri doona dagaal ba'an oo u dhexeeya waddamo, taasoo keeni doonta burburka dhulka.

Achyutananda Das ji wuxuu mar kale ku qoray ‘Jaiphula Malika’ lafteeda:

Odia Sloka:

ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ପୁଣ,
ଭୟଙ୍କର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏହା କଳି ଶେଷ ବୋଲି ଜାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Deśha deśha madhye puṇa,
bhayaṅkara juddha heba pramāṇa lo jāiphula,
ehā kaḷi śeṣha boli jāṇa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, wajahadda ugu dambeysa ee Kali Yuga, dagaallo aad u ba'an ayaa ka dhex qarxi doona waddamo kala duwan. Si kale haddii loo dhigo, marka dagaal cabsi leh uu ka bilowdo waddamo u dhexeeya, waa in loo fahmo inuu yahay dhammaadka Kali Yuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣୁ ପରମାଣୁ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରି ଭୟଙ୍କର,
ଗୋଟିଏ ଅଣୁ ବୋମାର କରାମତି ସାର।
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଅଣୁବୋମା ଗର୍ଜିବ ମହୀରେ,
କାନ୍ଦିବ ଧରଣୀ ରାଣୀ ହା ହୁତାଶରେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Aṇu paramāṇu juddha bhāri bhayaṅkara,
goṭie aṇu bomāra karāmati sāra.
lakṣha lakṣha aṇubomā garjiba mahīre,
kāndiba dharaṇī rāṇī hā hutāśhare.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaal nukliyeer ah ayaa ka dhici doona Dhulka kaasoo noqon doona mid aad u cabsi badan. Dhulka oo dhan ayaa gariiri doona iyadoo la isticmaalayo bambooyin nukliyeer ah oo aan la tirin karin. Hooyo Dhulka ayaa ooyi doonta.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sheegay in wakhtigan xaadirka ah, dagaal ba'an uu ka dhici doono Hindiya iyo Pakistan. Sida uu sheegay Mahapurush-ku, dagaalkan u dhexeeya Hindiya iyo Pakistan wuxuu markii dambe u rogi doonaa dagaal adduun sababtoo ah waddamo badan ayaa taageeri doona Pakistan dagaalkan, halka waddamo kalena ay u dagaalami doonaan dhanka Hindiya. Dagaalkan, waddamo waaweyn ee adduunka ayaa u qaybsami doona laba garab.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ଭାରତ ସପକ୍ଷ ହେବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଜାଣ,
ଇରାକ ଯେ ସମର୍ଥନ ଦେବ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chīna Pākisthāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa,
Ruṣhiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmāna Jāpāna.
Bhārata sapakṣha hebe mahāśhakti jāṇa,
Irāka je samarthana deba Pākisthāna.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaalkan, waddamo sida Shiinaha, Ameerika, iyo Ciraaq ayaa taageeri doona Pakistan, halka quwado sida Ruushka, Jarmalka, iyo Jabaan ay u dagaalami doonaan dhanka Hindiya.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu ku qoray qoraalka Malika ee cinwaankiisu yahay ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ,
ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପୁଣି ଚୀନ୍ ସଇନୁ ତୁର୍କୀ ଇରାନ ଜାଣ,
ବ୍ରିଟିଶ ଯେ ସଇନ୍ୟ ସବେ ମିଳିଣ।
ପାକସ୍ଥିାନକୁ ବଳ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ସର୍ବେ ଆବର,
ପାକିସ୍ଥାନକୁ ଦେବେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଲାଗିବ ଘୋର।
ଏହା ଦେଖି ରୁଷିଆ ଆସି ହୋଇବ ଠିଆ,
ଭାରତ ସମରକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ସେ ଏକୁଟିଆ।
ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ଶସ୍ତ୍ର ସଇନ୍ୟ ଯୋଗାଇ ଦେବ ଜାଣ,
ଭାରତ ସହିତ ମିଶି କରିବ ରଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākisthānṭi raṅge,
mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Puṇi Chīna sainu Turkī Irāna jāṇa,
Briṭisha je sainya sabe miḷiṇa.
Pākisthānku baḷa astra sarbe ābara,
Pākisthānku debe juddha lāgiba ghora.
Ehā dekhi Ruṣiā āsi hoiba ṭhiā,
Bhārata samarakṣetre se ekuṭiā.
Astra shastra sainya jogāi deba jāṇa,
Bhārata sahita mishi kariba raṇa.

Macne:
