Mahapurush-ku wuxuu qoray in waddan lagu magacaabo Pakistan uu dagaal ba'an la geli doono Hindiya. Dagaalkan, waddamo sida Shiinaha, Turkiga, Iiraan, Ingiriiska, iyo Ameerika ayaa caawin doona Pakistan waxayna bixin doonaan taageero milatari. Marka la arko waxaas oo dhan, saaxiibka ugu fiican ee Hindiya, Ruushka, ayaa ugu horrayn oo keligiis u imaan doona inuu caawiyo Hindiya. Wuxuu siin doonaa Hindiya dhammaan noocyada hubka, iskaashiga dhaqaale, iyo taageerada milatari, wuxuuna dagaal ba'an la geli doonaa waddamada cadowga ah dhanka Hindiya.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu qoray in aan ahayn Ruushka oo keliya, laakiin sidoo kale Jabaan iyo Jarmalka ay u dagaalami doonaan dhanka Hindiya dagaalkan. ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ lafteeda, wuxuu sii qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଜାପାନର ରାଗ ଅଛି ହିରୋଶିମା କଥା,
ପକାଇଥିଲା ବୋମା ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଜାଣିଥା।
ସେହି କ୍ରୋଧ ଲିଭିନାହିଁ ଜାପାନର ଜାଣ,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଭାରତ ପଟ ନେବେ ସେହି ପୁଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Jāpānara rāga achi Hirośhimā kathā,
pakāithilā bomā juddhe jāṇithā.
Sehi krodha libhināhiñ Jāpānara jāṇa,
juddhe Bhārata paṭa nebe sehi puṇa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, Jabaan ma awoodi doonto inay ilowdo soo ridista bambada atamka ee Ameerika ay ku ridday. Cadhooyinka Jabaan ee Ameerika ma qaboobi doonaan. Ololkii aargoosiga ayaa sii guban doona qalbigiisa, taasoo keeni doonta in Jabaan ay ka dagaalanto Ameerika dhanka Hindiya inta lagu jiro dagaalkii saddexaad ee adduunka. Codsi Jabaan, Jarmalka ayaa sidoo kale u dagaalami doona dhanka Hindiya.

12 Dagaalkii Hadhay ee Mahabharata ee Kali Yuga

Panchasakhas-ku waxay ku qoreen qoraallada Bhabishya Malika in Dagaalkii Adduunka ee ka dhacaya dhammaadka Kali Yuga uu asal ahaan noqon doono dagaalkii hadhay ee Mahabharata. Mahapurushas-ku waxay caddeeyeen in dagaalkii 18-ka maalmood ee Mahabharata ee dhammaadka Dwapara Yuga aan si buuxda loo dhammaystirin sababtoo ah, wakhtigaas, Lord Krishna wuxuu go'aansaday inuusan qaban qaybta ugu dambeysa ee dagaalka markaas, laakiin uu ku dhammaystiro dhammaadka Kali Yuga.

Intaa kaliya maaha, Mahapurushas-ku waxay si faahfaahsan uga qoreen dagaalkan qoraalladooda Malika waxayna shaaca ka qaadeen waxyaabo badan, sida goorta uu dhici doono, inta qof ee ku dhiman doonta, muddada uu qaadan doono, qaabka uu yeelan doono, hoggaanka uu ku dhici doono, cidda ka qayb qaadan doonta dhinaca Hindiya, halka uu ku dhammaan doono, iyo waxa dhici doona dagaalkan ka dib, iwm.

12.1 Goorma ayuu dagaalkan dhici doonaa?

Odia Sloka:

ଯୁଗଶେଷ କଳି ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଯମ ମାଆ ସାତ ଭଉଣୀ ଧରିଥିବେ ଖପର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Yugaśheṣha kaḷi Bhārata juddha hoiba ghora,
Yama māā sāta bhauṇī dharithibe khapara.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaalka ka hadhay Mahabharata wuxuu ku dhammaan doonaa dhammaadka Kali Yuga. Dagaalkan, "Yama Maa Sata Bhauni" ama Toddobada Hooyo Ilaahyo (Saptamatruka) ee ku yaalla Biraja Khetra (Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, iyo Narasimhi) ayaa baabbi'in doona dembiilayaasha.

Isagoo sharxaya goorta uu dagaalkan dhici doono dhammaadka Kali Yuga, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna Shani āsi deuthibā ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
Dasha aṇā jiba chha-a aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaalka ka hadhay Hindiya (Mahabharata) wuxuu bilaaban doonaa inta lagu jiro isku-xirnaanta Meena-Shani (Pisces-Saturn), kaas oo ay ku dhiman doonaan dad aan la tirin karin.

12.2 Qaabkee ayuu dagaalku u dhici doonaa oo xaggee lagu dagaallami doonaa marxaladda ugu dambeysa?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଓଳିଏ ବାକି ଅଛି ଜାଣ,
କଳିଯୁଗରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇ ପୂରଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mahābhārata juddha oḷie bāki achi jāṇa,
Kaḷiyugare biśhwajuddha hoi pūraṇa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, ogow in qayb ka mid ah dagaalkii Mahabharata uu hadhay, kaas oo lagu dhammaystiri doono dagaal caalami ah oo ka dhaca Kali Yuga.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu sii qoray ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ପୁଣି ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କହେ ଯବନ ବାହି ଆସିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha,
Oḍiśhā deśhare puṇi hoiba lo jāiphula,
kahe jabana bāhi āsiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaalka ka hadhay Mahabharata wuxuu ka dhici doonaa Odisha, muddadaasna, askar badan oo ka socda waddamada cadowga ah ayaa soo geli doona gudaha Hindiya.

Aw Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray mid kale oo ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଳିଯୁଗରେ ମହାଭାରତଟି ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳରୁ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପତ୍ତି ହୋଇବ ଠିକେ ଠିକେ ଏହା ଶୁଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

E kaḷiyugare Mahābhārataṭi Utkaḷa bhūiñ pramāṇa,
Utkaḷaru sabu utpatti hoiba ṭhike ṭhike ehā śhuṇa.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaalka ka hadhay Mahabharata wuxuu lagu dagaallami doonaa Kali Yuga, kaas oo ka dhici doona dhulka Utkala ama Odisha.

12.3 Waa maxay muddada dagaalkan?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sidoo kale ku sii sheegay qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’ in dagaalkan ka hadhay Mahabharata uu socon doono wadar ahaan 13 bilood.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଯେ ରହିଥିବ ଶୁଣ ହେତୁ ଦ୍ୱାରେ,
ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ସେତିକି ବେଳରେ।
ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ,
ସେ ଓଳିକେ ତେର ମାସ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Olie je rahithiba shuṇa hetu dwāre,
Bhārata juddha hoiba setiki xelare.
Sehi olikare nātha kali shesha hexa,
Se olike tera māsa bhoga je hoiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaalka ka hadhay Mahabharata wuxuu socon doonaa wadar ahaan saddex iyo toban bilood, oo marka uu dagaalkaas dhammaado, saamaynta Kali Yuga ee Dhulka ayaa si buuxda u dhammaan doonta.

12.4 Yaa ka qayb qaadan doona dhinaca Hindiya marxaladda ugu dambeysa?

Mahapurushas-ku waxay qoreen in qaybta ugu dambeysa ee dagaalkan, halka dhinac ay jiri doonaan ciidamo waaweyn oo ka socda waddamada cadowga ah, dhinaca kale, dhammaan geesiyaashii dagaalyahannada ee xilligii Mahabharata iyo kumanaan awliyo, saajin, iyo aragti-yahanno ah ayaa joogi doona hoggaanka Sayid Kalki laftiisa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ବେଳେ ଭାରତ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହାରିଯିବ ତ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ତ।
ଭାରତ ନରନାରୀ ସର୍ବେ ଡାକିବେ ହରି,
ରକ୍ଷାକର ପ୍ରଭୁ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଗଲୁଟି ସରି।
ଆହେ କୁଞ୍ଜବିହାରୀ ବାରେ ଶୁଣ ଗୁହାରି,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ତେଜି ଥରେ ଆସ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ଦେଖ ନେତ୍ର ଫେଡ଼ିତ ପୁଣ୍ୟଭୂମି ଭାରତ,
ବିଦେଶୀଙ୍କ ହସ୍ତେ ହୁଏ କି ହନ୍ତସନ୍ତ।
ଭାରତର କାଳର ଶୁଣି ସେ ଆଦିମୂଳ,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ପୁର ତେଜି ହେବେ ବାହାର।
କଳ୍କୀ ଦେବ ନାମ ବହି ସେ ନାରାୟଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳ ଦେଶ କଟକରେ ଦେବେ ଦର୍ଶନ।
ସେହି ବେଳରେ ଜାଣ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବଳରାମ,
ଭୀମ ସହ ବାହାରିବେ ବୀର ଅର୍ଜୁନ।
ଭୀଷ୍ମ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଆବର ଅଶ୍ୱତ୍ଥାମା ଯେ ବୀର,
କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଶଲ୍ୟ ଜୟଦ୍ରଥ ହେବେ ବାହାର।
ବେଲାଲସେନ ବୀର ପୁଣି ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବାଳବୀର ଅଭିମନ୍ୟୁ ଘେନି ସଙ୍ଗର।
ବୀର ବବ୍ରୁବାହନ ବାହାରିବଟି ଜାଣ,
ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ଘଟୋତ୍କଚ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନିଣ।
ସର୍ବେ ହୋଇଣ ମେଳି ବିଦେଶୀୟଙ୍କୁ ଦଳି,
ଦେବେ ମୁହୂର୍ତ୍ତକ ମଧ୍ଯେ ଲଗାଇ କଳି।
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଶକତି ବଳେ ଅପଲକ ନେତ୍ରରେ,
ଚାହିଁ ବିଜ୍ଞାନ ଶକ୍ତିକି ଜିଣିବେ ହେଳେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ehibeḷe Bhārata nishche hārijiba ta,
hāhākāra paḍijiba chāriāḍe ta.
Bhārata naranārī sarbe dākibe Hari,
rakhākara prabhu nishche galuṭi sari.
Āhe Kuñjabihārī bāre shuṇa guhāରି,
Baikuṇṭha teji thare āsa Shrī Hari.
Dekha netra pheḍita puṇyabhūmi Bhārata,
bideshīṅka haste hue ki hantasanta.
Bhāratara kāḷara shuṇi se ādimūḷa,
Baikuṇṭhapura teji hebe bāhāra.
Kalkī deba nāma bahi se Nārāyaṇa,
Utkaḷa desha Kaṭakare debe darshana.
Sehibeḷere jāṇa Shrī Kṛṣṇa Baḷarāma,
Bhīma saha bāhāribe bīra Arjuna.
Bhīṣma Droṇa ābara Ashwatthāmā je bīra,
Karṇa Shalya Jayadratha hebe bāhāra.
Belāḷasena bīra puṇi hebe bāhāra,
bāḷabīra Abhimanyu gheni saṅgara.
Bīra Babhrubāhana bāhāribaṭi jāṇa,
Ekalabya Ghaṭotkacha saṅge gheniṇa.
Sarbe hoiṇa meḷi bideshīyaṅku daḷi,
debe muhūrttaka madhye lagāi kaḷi.
Mantra shakti baḷe apalaka netrare,
chāhi bijñāna shaktiki jiṇibe heḷe.

Macne:
Taasi waa, inta lagu jiro Dagaalkii Saddexaad ee Adduunka, iyagoo arkaya weerarrada afarta dhinac ah ee cadowga shisheeye ku hayaan Hindiya, dadka Hindiya waxay noqon doonaan kuwo cabsi qaba oo ay ku qaylin doonaan dhibaato. Iyagoo aan waxba qaban karin, dhammaantood waxay xusuusan doonaan Sayidka waxayna ka baryi doonaan ilaalin. Waxay odhan doonaan—Sayidow, maqal baryadayada, na badbaadi, ka tag Vaikuntha oo u kaalay Dhulka si aad noo ilaaliso.

Isagoo ka xumaaday baaqan naxariista leh ee cibaadaysatada oo arkaya duullaanka Hindiya ee gacanta cadowga shisheeye, Brahman-ka Sareeya Sayid Narayana (Maha Vishnu) laftiisa, muuqaalkiisa Kalki, ayaa ka soo muuqan doona Cuttack, Odisha, wuxuuna siin doonaa darshan cibaadaysatada.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu qoray in Sayid Kalki oo ay weheliyaan isla waqtigaas, Bhima, Arjuna, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babhruvahana, Ekalavya, iyo Ghatotkacha, oo horey u dhashay, ay ka dagaallami doonaan dhinaca Hindiya oo ay si xun u jabin doonaan waddamada cadowga ah. Awoodda mantra-da rabbaaniga ah ee dagaalyahannadan geesiyaasha ah ayaa ka sarreysa awoodda casriga ah ee sayniska ku salaysan ee cadowga.

Mawduucan la midka ah, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରସ୍ତାବିତ କଳି ଭାରତ ସରିବ ହେବ ଭାରତ ସମର।
ତିନିଯୁଗ ଯୋଦ୍ଧା ଏକ ଯୁଗରେ ମହାସମର ଲାଗିବ,
କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକ୍ତ ମନୋସ୍କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Prastābita kaḷi Bhārata sariba heba Bhārata samara.
Tinijuga joddhā eka jugare mahāsamara lāgiba,
kahai Achyuta bhakta hebe mukta manoskāmanā pūriba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaalkii Mahabharata wuxuu lagu dagaallami doonaa Kali Yuga, kaas oo ay ka qayb qaadan doonaan geesiyaashii dagaalyahannada ee dhammaan saddexdii da' ee hore.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sii qoray ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ସମର ହେବ ଦେଲି ଚେତାଇ,
ସ୍ୱୟଂ ପରଂବ୍ରହ୍ମ ମୂରତି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତସାଇଁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Oḷie Bhārata samara heba deli chetāi,
swayaṃ Parambrahma mūrati prabhu Jagatasāiñ.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaalka ka hadhay Mahabharata wuxuu ka dhici doonaa hoggaanka Brahman-ka Sareeya Sayid Narayana (muuqaalka Kalki) laftiisa.

Mawduucan, mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, wuxuu sidoo kale ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Nidagha Nighanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ରହି ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ,
ଆଦେଶ ଦେବେଟି ମାଧବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Oḷie rahi juddha heba,
ādeśha debeṭi Mādhaba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, dagaalka ka hadhay Mahabharata wuu dhammaan doonaa, Madhava laftiisa, Sayid Kalki ee Weyn, ayaa amri doona in dagaalkan la bilaabo.

Aw weyn oo ka yimid Odisha oo dhashay qarnigii 20aad, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, wuxuu sidoo kale mawduucan ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ତୋପର ତପର ସମର,
ହୋଇବ ଅତି ଭୟଙ୍କର।
ତପକୁ ତୋପ ନ ପାରିବ,
ତପର ବଳ ବେଶୀ ହେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Topara tapara samara,
hoiba ati bhayaṅkara.
tapaku topa na pāriba,
tapara baḷa beśhī heba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, waxaa jiri doona dagaal adag oo u dhexeeya awoodda madaafiicda ee waddamada cadowga ah ee Hindiya iyo awoodda ascetic-ka (Tapasya) ee awliyada iyo saajinnada aan la tirin karin ee Hindiya, kaas oo awoodda gantaalada casriga ah ee cadowga ay noqon doonta mid aan waxba qaban karin marka loo eego xoogga ascetic-ka Hindiya, ugu dambayntana Hindiya ayaa ka soo bixi doonta guul dagaalkan weyn.

12.5 Xaggee lagu dhammaystiri doonaa dagaalka Hindiya?

Mahapurushas-ku waxay ku qoreen qoraallada Malika in marka ciidamada shisheeye ay gaaraan Odisha, ay ku dagaallami doonaan meelo kala duwan oo Odisha ah, sida ugu muhiimsan Birat Gadi ee Cuttack, Choudwar, Jobra, Barabati, iyo Kaliaboda; Puri; Jajpur; Gohira Tikiri ee Bhadrak; Paradip; Balasore, iwm.

Inkastoo dagaalkan uu ka dhici doono dhammaan meelahan Odisha, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu si cad u sheegay inuu si buuxda ugu dhammaan doono Khandagiri ee Bhubaneswar marka Sayidka Weyn laftiisa uu baabbi'iyo dhammaan askarta waddamada cadowga ah isagoo isticmaalaya Sudarshana Chakra.

Mahapurush-ku wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kalpatika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ ବହୁତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରୂପ ଧରି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକ୍ର ଧରି ବିନାଶିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Oḍiśhā deśhare Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre bahuta juddha hoiba,
Ananta Keśharī rūpa dhari prabhu chakra dhari bināśhiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, waxaa jiri doona dagaal aad u daran oo ka dhaca Khandagiri ee Odisha. Isla meeshaas, Sayid Kalki ee Weyn ayaa baabbi'in doona askar aan la tirin karin isagoo isticmaalaya Sudarshana Chakra.

12.6 Maxaa ka dhici doona dagaalkan iyo maxaa dhici doona ka dib?

‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray aayad qaybteeda hore ay tahay sidan:

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Sehi oḷikare nātha kaḷi śheṣha heba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, isla marka uu dagaalkan Mahabharata dhammaado, saamaynta Kali Yuga ayaa dhammaan doonta.

Kadibna Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu sidoo kale ku qoray aayad qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’, kaas oo sadarka koowaad uu leeyahay:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଯେ ସରିବ ପୃଥିବୀ ଶାନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha je sariba pruthibī śhānta hoiba.

Macne:
Taasi waa, marka uu dhammaado dagaalka ka hadhay Mahabharata, Dhulka wuxuu noqon doonaa mid nabad ah. Kadibna dhammaan buuqa Dhulka wuu dhammaan doonaa.

12.7 Sharaxaadda xaaladda quwadaha waaweyn sida Ameerika iyo Yurub ee Dagaalkii Adduunka

Panchasakha Mahapurushas waxay ku qoreen qoraalladooda Malika in Dagaalkii Saddexaad ee Adduunka uu noqon doono mid aad u daran oo dhammaan waddamada ay saameyn doonto. Dhammaan waddamada maanta loo arko inay yihiin quwadaha ugu awoodda badan adduunka, dagaalkan dhammaadka Kali Yuga ah ayaa xitaa iyaga ka tagi doona kuwo burburay.

Isagoo sharaxaya xaaladaha Ameerika ee dagaalkan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଭାଇ ମୂଳୁ ଧ୍ୱଂସ ଯିବ,
ଗର୍ବରେ ଜଳୁଛି ରାଜା ବିଶ୍ୱ ମୁଖ୍ୟ ହେବ।
ଧରାକୁ ମଣୁଛି ସରା କହେ ମୁହିଁ ଶ୍ରେଷ୍ଠ,
ଉଡ଼ାଇବି ପୃଥିବୀକୁ କରେ ମୁଁ ଶପଥ।
କାହାରି ଗର୍ବ ସହି ନ ପାରିବେ ଧାତା,
ଜଳାଇ ଦେବେ ତାହାକୁ ଉଡ଼ାଇବେ ମଥା।
ନ୍ୟୁୟର୍କ ସହର ଧ୍ୱଂସ ପଦା ହୋଇଯିବ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଅଥର୍ବ ନିର୍ବେଦ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Āmerikā rāṣhṭra bhāi mūḷu dhwaṅsa jiba,
garbare jaḷuchi rājā biśhwa mukhya heba.
dharāku maṇuchi sarā kahe muiñ śhreṣhṭha,
uḍāibi pruthibīku kare muñ śhapatha.
kāhāri garba sahi na pāribe dhātā,
jaḷāi debe tāhāku uḍāibe mathā.
New York sahara dhwaṅsa padā hoijiba,
hāhākāra paḍijiba atharba nirbeda.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, waddan la yiraahdo Ameerika ayaa isu arki doona inuu yahay hoggaamiyaha adduunka. Isagoo ku dhex milmay islaweyni iyo kibir, wuxuu isu magacaabi doonaa kan ugu fiican wuxuuna ku hanjabi doonaa inuu burburiyo waddamada kale ee dagaalka. Mahapurush wuxuu intaa ku daray in Sayidka Weyn uusan u dulqaadan karin islaweynida qofna, sidaas darteedna uu burburin doono kibirka Ameerika. Magaalada New York ee Ameerika waa la burburin doonaa, waddankaasna wuxuu la kulmi doonaa burbur dhammaystiran isagoo markhaati ka ah burbur ba'an.

‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ dhexdeeda, Saint Achyutananda Das wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ଇଉରୋପ ମହାଦେଶ ଜାଣ,
ସ୍ଥିତି ହରାଇବ ତାର ବିଷାକ୍ତ ପବନ।
ସବୁରି ଧ୍ୱଂସର କାରଣ ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ନିଶ୍ଚିହ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଗର୍ବି ଅଟେ ପୁଣ।
ନିଜର ଇଜ୍ଜତ ପୃଥିବୀ ପରେ ଦେଖାଇଲା,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧକୁ ତ ଜୀବନରେ ସିଦ୍ଧହସ୍ତ କଲା।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Āmerikā Iuropa mahādesha jāṇa,
Sthiti harāiba tāra biṣākta pabana.
Saburi dhwaṃsara kāraṇa Āmerikā jāṇa,
Nishchinha hoiba juddhe garbi aṭe puṇa.
Nijara ijjata pṛthwī pare dekhāilā,
Juddhaku ta jībanare siddhahasta kalā.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, sababta Dagaalkii Saddexaad ee Adduunka waxay noqon doontaa waddan la yiraahdo ‘Ameerika’. Waddankaas wuxuu dhulka oo dhan ka dhisi doonaa sharaf iyo kibir u gaar ah wuxuuna ku guuleysan doonaa xirfadda dagaalka. Mahapurush wuxuu intaa ku daray in kibirkan awgiis, magaciisa iyo raadkiisaba la tirtiri doono Dagaalkii Saddexaad ee Adduunka. Dagaalka ka dib, cimilada halkaas waxay noqon doontaa mid sun ah, taasina awgeed, dad aan la tirin karin ayaa ku dhiman doona qaaradaha Ameerika iyo Yurub.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶେଷେ ଇଉରୋପ ଦେଶ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ହୋଇବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ,
ବଞ୍ଚି ରହିବେ କେବଳ ଭକ୍ତ ଅଂଶ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Śheṣhe Iuropa deśha juddhe hoiba dhwaṅsa,
bañchi rahibe kebaḷa bhakta aṅśha.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, dagaalkan, qaaradda Yurub gebi ahaanba waa la burburin doonaa. Marka laga reebo cibaadaysanayaasha Sayidka, cidna kama badbaadi doonto waddamadaas.

‘Jaiphula Malika’ dhexdeeda, Saint Achyutananda Das wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଦେଶ,
ହାଡ଼ ମାଳ ହୋଇଯିବ ବିଶେଷ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୀ ଦୁଲୁ ଦୁଲୁ ହେବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Paśhchima uttara deśha,
hāḍa māḷa hoijiba biśheṣha lo jāiphula,
mahī dulu dulu heba dhwaṅsa.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, waddamada ku yaal dhinacyada Galbeed iyo Waqooyi ayaa lagu burburin doonaa Dagaalkii Adduunka.

Achyutananda Das ji wuxuu mar kale ku qoray ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଜର୍ମାନୀ ରୁଷିଆ ଆଦି,
ଇଟାଲୀ ଜାପାନ ତୁରସ୍କ ଗାଦି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସେହି ସରବେ ହୋଇବେ ରଦ୍ଦି।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Jarmānī Ruṣhiā ādi,
Itālī Jāpāna Turuska gādi lo jāiphula,
sehi sarabe hoibe raddi.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, Jarmalka, Ruushka, Talyaaniga, Jabaan, iyo Turkiga, iwm., waddamadaas waxaa si weyn u burburin doona Dagaalkii Adduunka.

Sida aan horay u sheegnay, dhammaan dhacdooyinka kor ku xusan waxay inta badan dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro xilliga Meena-Shani yoga, taas oo ah, inta u dhaxaysa sanadaha 2025 iyo 2028.

Bishii Maarso 29, 2025, Saturn wuxuu galay calaamadda Pisces (Meena). Mararka qaarkood wuxuu ku socdaa dib u socod (Vakri) ama dhaqdhaqaaq gadaal ah, mararka qaarkoodna wuxuu ku socdaa dhaqdhaqaaq toos ah (Margi) ama dhaqdhaqaaq qumman, Saturn wuxuu ku sii jiri doonaa calaamadda Pisces ilaa Febraayo 23, 2028. Dhanka kale, laga bilaabo Juun 3, 2027, ilaa Oktoobar 20, 2027, waxaa jiri doona isbeddel gaaban oo Saturn ah oo ku yimaada calaamadda Aries (Mesha).

Hadda su'aal muhiim ah ayaa halkan ka soo baxaysa. Waxaa laga joogaa qiyaastii 10 bilood tan iyo markii ay bilaabatay Meena-Shani yoga, laakiin dhacdooyinkan burburka ah weli ma bilaaban. Maxay sidaas u tahay? Dabcan, masiibooyinka dabiiciga ah ayaa ka dhacaya adduunka oo dhan, dagaallo ayaa sidoo kale ka socda waddamo dhowr ah, laakiin ma dhacayaan heerka ballaaran ee ay saadaaliyayaasha Panchasakhas ku sheegeen qoraallada Malika! Su'aal ayaa si isdaba joog ah uga soo baxda maskaxda qaar badan oo naga mid ah goorta ay heerka dhacdooyinkan sii xoogaysan doonaan!

Goorma ayuu si sax ah u bilaaban doonaa Burburka Weyn? Maxay Bhabishya Malika ka leedahay arrintan? Malika waxay hubaal ah in ciyaaraha burburka ah ay ka dhici doonaan Meena-Shani, laakiin Malika waxay bilowga ciyaarahan burburka ah ku tilmaamaysaa qaybta ugu dambaysa ee sanadka 2026 halkii ay ka ahaan lahayd sanadka 2025.

Mid ka mid ah Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, wuxuu ku qoray qoraalladiisa Malika ee ku saabsan rabshadaha aan horay loo arag ee ka dhici doona adduunka oo dhan inta lagu jiro xilliga Meena-Shani wuxuuna bixiyay macluumaad ku saabsan waqtigooda saxda ah. Mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika, wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଅକାଳେ ମଡ଼କ ପଡ଼ିବ ସେହି ସମୟରେ ଜାଣ।
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପରେ କମ୍ପିବ ସକଳ ମେଦିନୀ,
କାଳ ଭୈରବୀ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଯେ ରଡ଼ି।
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ତୃତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଜଳ ପ୍ରଳୟ ହୋଇବ ଭଲେ ତୁହି ଜାଣ।
ହୁତୁ ହୁତୁ ହୋଇ ଜଳୁଥିବେ ହିଙ୍ଗୁଳା ମାତିବେ,
ବଡ଼ ବଡ଼ କୋଠା ଘର ଜାଳି ଯେ ଦେବେ।
ଚୋର ଟାଉଟର ସଂଖ୍ୟା ପ୍ରବଳ ବଢ଼ିବ,
ସେଥିପାଇଁ ଦେବୀଙ୍କର କୋପେ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ନ ଦେଖିଲା ନେତ୍ର ପୁଣି ସେ ସମୟେ ଦେଖିବ,
ତ୍ରାହି ତ୍ରାହି ବୋଲି ପୁଣି ଡାକ ଛାଡୁଥିବ।
ହରି ଆଶ୍ରା କର ତୁହି ନିସ୍ତାର ପାଇବୁ,
ଦାସ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ କହେ ବିହନ ହୋଇବୁ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Mīna śhani yogare je dwitīya charaṇa,
akāḷe maḍaka paḍiba sehi samayare jāṇa.
bhūmikampare kampiba sakaḷa medinī,
kāḷa bhairabī chāḍibe śhūnyare je raḍi.
mīna śhani yogare je tṛutīya charaṇa,
jaḷa pralaya hoiba bhale tuhi jāṇa.
hutu hutu hoi jaḷuthibe hiṅguḷā mātibe,
baḍa baḍa koṭhā ghara jāḷi je debe.
chora ṭāuṭara saṅkhyā prabala baḍhiba,
sethipāiñ debīṅkara kope nāśha jiba.
na dekhilā netra puṇi se samaye dekhiba,
trāhi trāhi boli puṇi ḍāka chāḍuthiba.
Hari āśhrā kara tuhi nistāra pāibu,
dāsa Yaśhobanta kahe bihana hoibu.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, labada sano iyo badh ee Meena-Shani yoga, inta lagu jiro wajiga labaad (sanadaha 2026-2027) iyo wajiga saddexaad (sanadaha 2027-2028), dhimasho aan waqtigeeda ahayn ayaa ku dhici doonta tiro badan, fawdana waxay ka talin doontaa meel kasta. Dhul gariir ba'an ayaa dhici doona, dhulka oo dhan ayaa gariiri doona. Waxaa jiri doona oohin Bhairavi ah, taas oo ah, Hooyo Bhairavi ayaa si lama filaan ah uga soo saari doonta guux cabsi leh meel bannaan. Argagixisada daadadka biyaha ayaa lagu arki doonaa Dhulka. Cadhooyinka Hooyo Hingula awgeed, guryo waaweyn, qasriyo, iwm., ayaa si ba'an u guban doona. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in dhammaan dadka xunxun ay noqon doonaan dhibanayaasha cadhada Hooyooyinka Ilaahyada. Waqtigaas, dadku waxay arki doonaan wax kasta oo indhahoodu aysan arki karin ilaa markaas, taas oo ah, wax walba waxay u dhici doonaan si aan horay loo arag. Dhammaan dadku waxay u qaylin doonaan naxariis. Kuwa isu dhiiba cagaha Sayid Shri Hari oo magangalyadiisa qaata, Mahapurush wuxuu yiri iyaga kaliya ayaa ka heli doona badbaado dhammaan waxyaalahan waxayna noqon doonaan abuurka Satya Yuga.

Aayadda kor ku xusan, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das wuxuu sheegay in ciyaarahan burburka ah ay si dhab ah u bilaaban doonaan wajiga labaad ee Meena-Shani yoga, taas oo ah, sanadaha 2026-2027.

Inta lagu jiro Kali Yuga, Mahapurushas badan ayaa ku dhashay Odisha marba marka ka dambaysa. Qarnigii 20aad, Mahapurush ku dhashay Odisha wuxuu shaaca ka qaaday saadaalinta uu sheegay Sayidka Weyn laftiisa, Hooyo Yogamaya, iyo ilaahyada iyo ilaahyadaha kale ee faa'iidada cibaadaysanayaasha. Mahapurush wuxuu qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଆଦେଶ ଦେବୀଙ୍କୁ ହେବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Binśha chabisha sāla sariba,
Śhrī Hari ādeśha debīṅku heba.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, ka dib marka ay timaado qaybta ugu dambaysa ee sanadka 2026 ama sanadka 2026 uu dhammaado, Sayid Kalki wuxuu amri doonaa dhammaan quwadaha rabbaaniga ah (Hooyo Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, sagaal crore Katyayanis) inay bilaabaan burburka.

Mahapurush wuxuu markaa intaa ku daray:

Odia Sloka:

ସମୟ ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ ଯେବେ,
ସବୁ ଦେବା ଦେବୀ ବାହାରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Samaya binśha chabisha sāla sariba jebe,
sabu debā debī bāhāre biśhwajuddha karibe.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, ka dib sanadka 2026, dhammaan ilaahyada iyo ilaahyadaha kale waxay ku dhawaaqi doonaan baaq dagaal oo loogu talagalay dagaalka Deva-Asura. Bilowga dagaalka (Dagaalkii Adduunka) ayaa ka dhici doona adduunka.

Waxaa xusid mudan in labada aayadood ee kor ku xusan, Mahapurush uu si cad u qoray “Sanadka 2026”.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଛଅ ସାତ ମିଶାଇଣ ତେର ଯୋଗ କର,
ଏହିଠାରୁ ଘୋର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରତ ବକ୍ଷର।
ସେ ବେଳେ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟଟି ହେବ ଜାଣ,
ଛିନ୍ନଛତ୍ର ଏ ପୃଥିବୀ କହିଲି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chha-a sāta mishaiṇa tera yoga kara,
ehi thāru ghora juddha bhārata bakṣhara.
se beḷe khaṇḍa pralayaṭi heba jāṇa,
chhinnachhatra e pruthibī kahili pramāṇa.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, isku dar lix (6) iyo toddoba (7) ka dibna ku dar saddex iyo toban (13) wadarta, taas oo ah, 6+7+13=26, taas oo dagaalka ba'an uu ka bilaaban doono Hindiya. Waqtigaas ka dib, dhacdooyinka burburka qayb ahaan (Khanda Pralaya) ayaa dhici doona, dhulkuna wuxuu bilaabi doonaa inuu burburo.

Waxaa xusid mudan in halkan Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, si ka duwan labada aayadood ee kor ku xusan, uusan qorin “Sanadka 2026” laakiin uu kaliya qoray 26. Waxaa suurtogal ah in halkan, 26, tilmaanta Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ay tahay sanadka 2026 oo kaliya.

Mahapurush ku dhashay qarnigii 20aad wuxuu mar kale qoray:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ପରେ ତ କରିବୁ ବିଚାର,
ମନେ ନ କରିବୁ ଯଦି ଯିବୁ ଯମପୁର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Binśha chabisha pare ta karibu bichāra,
mane na karibu jadi jibu Yamapura.

Macne:
Aayadda kor ku xusan, Hooyo Yogamaya waxay leedahay ka dib sanadka 2026, qof walba waa la xukumi doonaa. Dhammaan kuwa aan rumaysadka lahayn, xunxun, dembiilayaasha, iwm., dhammaantood waa la burburin doonaa.

Sidaa darteed, waa in si cad loo fahmo in ciyaaraha burburka ah ee Kalki ee Malika lagu sharaxay ay ka dhici doonaan xilliga Meena-Shani yoga lafteeda, laakiin kuwan ma dhici doonaan sanadka 2025 laakiin waxay ka bilaaban doonaan qaybta ugu dambaysa ee 2026.

Immisa sano ayay guud ahaan ciyaarahan burburka ah socon doonaan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu arrintan ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Kaliyuga Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚବିଶ ଠାବରୁ ଅଣତିରିଶ ଯାଏ ପ୍ରମାଦ ଅଛି ଅପାର,
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେଟି ହୋ ଗରୁଡ଼ ମହାକଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଭିତରେଟି ଗରୁଡ଼ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସରିବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Chabiśha ṭhābaru aṇatiriśha jāe pramāda achi apāra,
aṇatiriśha aṅkareṭi ho Garuḍa mahā Kaḷkī abatāra.
aṇatiriśha bhitareṭi Garuḍa sabu utpāta sariba.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, ciyaaraha burburka ah waxay ka bilaaban doonaan lambarka 24 waxayna socon doonaan ilaa lambarka 29. Dhammaan ciyaaraha burburka ah waxay ku dhammaan doonaan lambarka 29.

Xusuusnow in aayadda kor ku xusan, Mahapurush uu ka hadlay lambarka 24, ee ma aha sanadka 2024. Laakiin Mahapurush wuxuu si cad u sheegay in ciyaarahan burburka ah, oo ka bilaabanaya lambarka 24 oo soconaya ilaa lambarka 29, ay sii socon doonaan wadar ahaan 6 sano. Mahapurush ma caddayn sanadka ay ka bilaaban doonaan iyo sanadka ay ku dhammaan doonaan, laakiin wuxuu sheegay in laga bilaabo lambarka 24 ilaa lambarka 29, taas oo ah, wadar ahaan 6 sano, ciyaarahan burburka ah ay sii socon doonaan.

Maadaama aayadaha kor ku xusan aan horay uga ogaanay in ciyaarahan burburka ah ay ka bilaaban doonaan qaybta ugu dambaysa ee sanadka 2026, waxaan hadda si fudud u gaari karnaa gabagabada ah in lambarka 24 ee ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ uu tilmaamayo sanadka 2026-2027. Sidaas darteed, lambarka 29 ee taxanahan isku midka ah ee qoraalkan Malika wuxuu tilmaamayaa sanadka 2031-2032.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, isagoo adeegsanaya Malika Pothi, wuxuu mar kale sharaxay –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହା ତ ହେଉଛି କାଳର ଭୟ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ତହିଁ,
ଏହା ତ କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳଟି ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳ କରୁଅଛନ୍ତି ହେତୁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଆଉ ବା ଏକତିରିଶ ସେ ତ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ।
ମହାମାରୀ ସେ ତ ଆସିବ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଆସ,
ତେତିଶି ମସିହା ସୁଦ୍ଧାଟି କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଠା ଠିକଣା ଯେ ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହି,
ଏଭଳି ସମୟ ଆସିବ ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କାହାରି ପତା ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ,
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନୀମାନେ ବାଟଟି ପାଇବେ ନାହିଁରେ ଜାଣ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Ehā ta heuchhi kāḷara bhaya Achyutī tahiñ,
ehā ta Kaḷkī kheḷaṭi dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kaḷkī kheḷa karuachhanti hetu pramāṇa,
āu bā ekatiriśa se ta heture jāṇa.
Mahāmārī se ta āsiba kahe Achyutī āsa,
Tetiriśhi masihā suddhāṭi kahe dīna Achyuta.
Ṭhā ṭhikaṇā je rahiba nāhiñ Achyutī kahi,
ebhaḷi samaya āsiba dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kāhāri patā rahiba nāhiñ heture jāṇa,
Baijñānīmāne bāṭaṭi pāibe nāhiñ re jāṇa.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, Sayid Kalki wuxuu qaban doonaa ciyaartiisa (ciyaaraha burburka ah). Ciyaarahan burburka ah waxay sii socon doonaan ilaa sanadka 2033. Inta lagu jiro, cudurro faafa oo badan ayaa imaan doona, kuwaas oo sababi doona fawdo bulshada aadanaha. Waqti ayaa imaan doona oo fawdo ay ka jiri doonto meel kasta. Dhammaan dadku waxay ku jiri doonaan xaalad murugo leh. Xitaa aqoonta saynisyahannada ma noqon doonto mid wax ku ool ah.

Si kastaba ha ahaatee, Achyutananda Das ji wuxuu sidoo kale ku sheegay ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ in hawlaha burburka ee ugu muhiimsan ay ku dhammaan doonaan lambarka 27.

Mahapurush wuxuu qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ସତାଇଶ ଅଙ୍କେ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଦିଗରୁ ସମୁଦ୍ର ଆସିବ ମାଡ଼ି,
ଉତ୍ତରରୁ ଗଙ୍ଗା ଉଛୁଳି ପଡ଼ିଣ ତହିଁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଦେବ ଧାଡ଼ି।
ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଦିଗରୁ ଜଳ ସ୍ରୋତ ଏକ ଆସି ତହିଁ ଯିବ ମିଶି,
ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟର ସୃଜନା କରିବ ପାପୀ ତହିଁ ଯିବେ ଭାସି।
ପୁଣ୍ୟାତ୍ମାମାନଙ୍କୁ ସ୍ୱୟଂ ବଳଦେବ ଉଦ୍ଧାର କରି ରଖିବେ,
ଭାରତର ଗିରି ପର୍ବତ ପାହାଡ଼େ ନେଇ ଯତନେ ପାଳିବେ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Satāisha aṅke dakhina digaru samudra āsiba māḍi,
uttararu Gaṅgā uchhuḷi paḍiṇa tahiñ saṅge deba dhāḍi.
Pashchima digaru jala srota eka āsi tahiñ jiba mishi,
khaṇḍa praḷayara sṛujanā kariba pāpī tahiñ jibe bhāsi.
Puṇyātmāmānaṅku swayaṃ Baḷadeba uddhāra kari rakhibe,
Bhāratara giri parbata pāhāḍe nei jatane pāḷibe.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, lambarka 27, hirarka badda ayaa ka imaan doona Koonfurta waxayna la kulmi doonaan webiga Ganges ee buux dhaafay ee Waqooyiga. Isla mar ahaantaana, hirar ba'an oo tsunami ah ayaa sidoo kale ka imaan doona Galbeedka waxayna abuuri doonaan burbur. Burburkan qayb ahaan, dembiilayaal aan la tirin karin ayaa dhiman doona. Kuwa quduusiinta ah iyo nafta wanaagsan, Baladev laftiisa ayaa badbaadin doona oo si taxaddar leh ugu hayn doona buuraha iyo kuraha Hindiya.

Sida uu sheegay Mahapurush, tani waxay noqon doontaa masiibada ugu dambaysa ee masiibooyinka waaweyn ee dhici doona lambarka 27, taas oo ah, sanadka 2029-2030 sida ku cad xisaabta kor ku xusan.

Hadda aan isku dayno inaan fahanno jadwalkan waqtiga ah anagoo adeegsanayna aayado kale. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray ‘Bhabishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ସତୁରି ଯାଏ ଠୁଳ,
ଲାଗି ରହିବ ତୃତୀୟ ମହାସମର।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଚମକ ପଡିବ ସ୍ଵର୍ଗ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ପାତାଳ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Pāñcha barṣha kāḷa saturi jāe ṭhuḷa,
lāgi rahiba tṛutīya mahāsamara.
pāñcha barṣha kāḷa juddha hoiba ghora,
chamaka paḍiba swarga martya pātāḷa.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, Dagaalkii Saddexaad ee Adduunka wuxuu socon doonaa shan sano. Waxaa jiri doona dagaal qaraar oo shan sano ah; Jannada, Dhulka, iyo Hooseba way gariiri doonaan.

Achyutananda Das ji wuxuu ku qoray qoraal kale oo Malika ah –

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କହନ୍ତି ବାସବ ଶୁଣ,
ଚବିଶ ଠାରୁ ସତେଇଶ ଜାଣ।
ଏଥି ଭିତରେ ଯେ ହୋଇବ ଗୋଳ,
କାହାରି ନ ରହିବ ଥଳକୁଳ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Śhrī Hari kahanti Bāsaba śhuṇa,
chabiśha ṭhāru sataiśha jāṇa.
ethi bhitare je hoiba goḷa,
kāhāri na rahiba thaḷakuḷa.

Macne:
Aayadda sare, Sayid Shri Krishna, isagoo la hadlaya Indra, wuxuu leeyahay waxaa jiri doona dagaal qaraar oo ka bilaabmaya lambarka 24 ilaa lambarka 27.

Waxaa ka cad labada aayadood ee kor ku qoran in dagaalka ugu weyn ama Dagaalkii Saddexaad ee Adduunka uu socon doono qiyaastii 4-5 sano. Taas macnaheedu waa, inuu bilaabmayo 2026-2027 oo uu ku dhammaanayo 2029-2030.

Sidaa darteed, burburkii ugu dambeeyay ee ugu weynaa ee uu Mahapurush ku sheegay ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ lambarka 27 ama sanadka 2029-2030 wuxuu si fiican ula jaanqaadayaa labada aayadood ee kor ku xusan.

Doodaha kor ku xusan, haddii gabagabo kooban laga soo saaro:

Sanadka 2026 – 2027: Xilligii burburka ayaa bilaaban doona.
Sanadka 2029 – 2030: Dhacdooyinka ugu daran iyo kuwa ugu xun ayaa dhammaan doona.
Sanadka 2031 – 2032: Dhammaan dhacdooyinka burburka ah way dhammaan doonaan.

Fiiro gaar ah: Hawlaha burburka ee ugu waaweyn ee dhammaanaya sanadka 2029 waxaa ka mid ah dhammaan kuwa inta badan laga hadlo, sida – dhul gariir waaweyn, burbur biyo oo daran, masiibooyin dabaylo, masiibooyin dab, dhicitaanka meteoro, cudurro-faafid, dagaalka Hindiya ee wadamada cadowga ah, Dagaalka Adduunka, dagaalka Kali-Mahabharata, burburka Hirakud iyo biyo-xireenada kale, mugdi todoba maalmood iyo todoba habeen ah, Bhairavi Dak, iwm.

13 Bilowga Satyayuga

Si loo galo Satyayuga, waxaa lagama maarmaan ah in bini'aadamku ku socdo jidka Dharma. Waa inuu mar walba runta sheegaa oo uu raacaa runta oo keliya; waa inuusan qaadan jidka waalida laakiin jidka nabadda. Waa inuu u qabaa dareen jacayl, naxariis, iyo damqasho dhammaan noolaha. Waa inuusan wax xanuun ah u geysan wax noole ah maskaxdiisa, hadalkiisa, iyo dhaqankiisa. Waa inuu noqdaa mid u wanaag doona dhammaan makhluuqa sabab la'aan, dhaqankiisu waa inuu noqdaa mid cafis badan oo dadka kale ah, waa inuu ahaadaa mid dabeecad fudud oo toosan, waa inuusan cidna colaad u qabin, waana inuu ogaadaa sida loo xakameeyo dareenkiisa. Waa inuu ahaadaa cibaadaysane Ilaah, mar walba maskaxdiisa ku hagaajiyaa Ilaah, waqti ku qaataa heesaha, magacyada Ilaah ku celceliya, ka qaybqaataa kulamada ruuxiga ah (Satsang), tukada salaadaha maalinlaha ah saddex jeer (Trisandhya), raacaa qoraallada quduuska ah, oo akhriyaa Shrimad Bhagavat Mahapurana (oo loo yaqaan Mahapurana ka mid ah 18ka Purana) maalin kasta. Waa inuu ahaadaa mid akhriya ama maqla ciyaaraha Ilaah. Waa inuu cunaa cunto nadiif ah oo Sattvic ah (khudaar). Iyadoo uu gudanayo waajibaadkiisa adduunyo, waa inuu sidoo kale ku hawlanaadaa Yoga, magac-ku-celcelin, iwm. Waa inuu mar walba xushmeeyaa waalidkiis iyo waayeelkiisa, jacayl u muujiyaa kuwa yaryar, oo uu dhiso dareen saaxiibtinimo oo uu la yeesho faciis.

Mid kasta oo naga mid ah wuxuu rabaa inuu galo Satyayuga isagoo ka baxsanaya hawlaha burburka ee soo socda, masiibooyinka dabiiciga ah, iyo Dagaalka Adduunka, iyo isagoo helaya nimcada Sayid Kalki. Laakiin ma u fududahay in la galo Satyayuga? Maya. Gelitaanka Satyayuga ma aha sida in xal lagu sameeyo koob oo la cabbo si qof kasta oo sidaas u cabba uu hubaal u heli doono gelitaan Satyayuga!

Si loo galo Satyayuga, qof kasta oo bini'aadam ah wuxuu u baahan yahay inuu isbeddel aasaasi ah ku sameeyo noloshiisa. Waxaa lagama maarmaan ah in isagoo gudanaya hawlaha iyo waajibaadka adduunyo, uu bilaabo inuu nolol ruuxi ah ku noolaado.

Isagoo sharxaya sida xilliga soo socda uu u noqon doono cibaadaysanayaasha, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika ee ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ହଟି ଯାଇ ସତ୍ୟର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଭକତମାନେ ରହିବେ ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଉଲ୍ଲାସ।
ବହୁ କଷ୍ଟ ପାଇଛନ୍ତି ଭକତ ମୋହର,
ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନରେ ଦୁଃଖ କଷ୍ଟ ଯିବ ଦୂର।
ଏ କଳି ହଟିବ ଯୁଗ ଅନନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରଣ ହୋଇବ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Kaḷiyuga haṭi jāi satyara prakāśha,
bhakatamāne rahibe atyanta ullāsa.
bahu kaṣhṭa pāichhanti bhakata mohara,
alpa dinare duḥkha kaṣhṭa jiba dūra.
e kaḷi haṭiba juga ananta hoiba,
dīna Achyutī kāmanā pūraṇa hoiba.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, cibaadaysanayaashu waxay ku dhibtoodeen wax badan Kaliyuga, laakiin wax yar ka dib marka Kaliyuga dhammaato oo iftiinka da'da weligeed ah (Ananta Yuga) uu soo baxo, cibaadaysanayaashu waxay noqon doonaan kuwo aad u faraxsan. Markaas dhammaan murugadooda iyo xanuunkooda way baaba'i doonaan.

Saint Bhima Bhoi wuxuu ku qoray mid ka mid ah qoraalladiisa Malika ee ‘Stuti Chintamani’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ନିବାରିବେ ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିବେ ପାଳିବେ ଜନ ପରଜା,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ ଅନାଦି ହୋଇବେ ରାଜା।
ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ ଭକତ ସକଳ ହରି ହରିବେ ଯେ ଦୁଃଖ,
ହରିବୋଲ ହୁଳହୁଳି ଶବଦରେ ପୂରିବ ଏହି ତ୍ରୈଲୋକ୍ୟ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Duṣṭa nibāribe santhaṅku rakhibe pāḷibe jana parajā,
Kaḷiyuga sheṣe satya upujiba Anādi hoibe rājā.
Haraṣa hoibe bhakata sakaḷa hari haribe je duḥkha,
Haribola huḷuhuḷi shabadare pūriba ehi traiḷokya.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, Sayidka Sare wuxuu burburin doonaa kuwa xunxun wuxuuna ilaalin doonaa awliyada iyo cibaadaysanayaasha, wuxuuna daryeeli doonaa dadka dhulka. Kaliyuga way dhammaan doontaa oo Satyayuga ayaa imaan doonta, Sayid Kalki laftiisu wuxuu noqon doonaa taliyaha adduunka oo dhan. Sayidku wuxuu ka qaadi doonaa murugada dhammaan cibaadaysanayaasha wuxuuna ka dhigi doonaa kuwo faraxsan. Cibaadaysanayaashu way farxi doonaan, dhammaan saddexda adduunyo waxay la dhawaaqi doonaan magaca 'Hari'.

13.1 Goorma ayuu Satyayuga bilaaban doonaa?

Bhabishya Malika waxay sheegaysaa in Ananta Yuga ama Adi Satyayuga ay bilaaban doonto sanadka 2033. Mahapurush wuxuu ku qoray qoraalkiisa Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ଡାହାଣରେ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ରଖ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ଯବନ ଗଣ ବିନାଶଟି ଧ୍ରୁବ,
ଦୁଇ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କରୁ ଆରମ୍ଭ।
ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ଅସତ୍ୟ ମାନ ହଟି ଯାଉଥିବ,
ଅଧର୍ମର ନାଶେ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରଭାବୀ ହୋଇବ।
ତେତିଶିରେ ଚାରି ପାଦ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମା ତହୁଁ ବିକଶିବ।
ସରଳ ସଜ୍ଜନ ସାଧୁ ସନ୍ଥ ଜନ ମେଳ,
ହେବା ପାଇଁ ଏ ଧରା ରେ ଆସିଗଲା ବେଳ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Dui ḍāhāṇare shūna eka tini rakha,
sampūrṇṇa satya prakāsha hoiba pratyakṣa.
Mlechha jabana gaṇa bināshaṭi dhruba,
dui shūna eka tini aṅkaru ārambha.
Anyāya asatya māna haṭi jāuthiba,
adharmara nāshe dharma prabhābhī hoiba.
Tetishire chāri pāda dharma prakāshiba,
satya shānti dayā kṣamā tahuñ bikaśhiba.
Saraḷa sajjana sādhu santha jana meḷa,
hebā pāiñ e dharā re āsigalā beḷa.

Macne:
Mahapurush wuxuu qoray in laga bilaabo sanadka 2013, burburka Kaliyuga iyo iftiinka Runta ay si tartiib tartiib ah u bilaaban doonaan. Laga bilaabo waqtigaas, burburka kuwa xunxun iyo dembiilayaasha ayaa bilaaban doona. Awoodda beenta, cadaalad darrada, iyo xumaanta ayaa yaraan doonta, saamaynta Dharma-na waxay bilaabi doontaa inay kordho. Mahapurush wuxuu intaa ku daray in sidaas oo kale, sanadka 2033, iftiinka dhammaan afarta tiir ee Dharma uu soo bixi doono, taas oo macnaheedu yahay in Dharma si buuxda loo dhisi doono. Laga bilaabo waqtigaas, wanaagyada Runta, Nabadda, Naxariista, iyo Cafiska ayaa bilaabi doona inay si buuxda u kobcaan. Waxaa jiri doona midow dhammaan dadka sharafta leh, naxariista leh iyo kuwa dabeecadda awliyada leh, awliyada, iyo xikmadleyda.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଗୁପତୁ ଗୁପତ, ତେତିଶି ଅଙ୍କେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ଭକତମାନେ ଯେ ଜାଣିବେ, ସମସ୍ତେ ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ।
ସଂସାର ଉଶ୍ଵାସ ହୋଇବ, କାଳର ଭୟ ଯେ ଛାଡିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ବସ୍ତ୍ରରେ ସୁଖୀ ହେବେ, ଧର୍ମ ପଥକୁ ଆଚୋରିବେ।
ଶସ୍ୟ ଯେ ବହୁତ ହୋଇବ, ଲକ୍ଷ୍ମୀ କୃପାରୁ ସବୁ ହେବ।
ସତ୍ୟରେ ସର୍ବେ ଆତଜାତ, ସତ୍ୟ କଳି ଯେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ପୁରାଣ ଭାଗବତ ମାନ, କରିବେ ଗାନ ସଂକୀର୍ତ୍ତନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶ ଯିବ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
କହନ୍ତି ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ କରତା, ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନଥିବେ ଏକ ଜାଣ, ଭକ୍ତ ଭଗବାନ ମିଳନ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Atyanta gupatu gupata, Tetishi aṅke heba khyāta.
Bhakatamāne je jāṇibe, samaste haraṣa hoibe.
Saṃsāra ushwāsa hoiba, kāḷara bhaya je chhāḍiba.
Anna bastrare sukhī hebe, dharma pathaku āchoribe.
Shasya je bahuta hoiba, Laxmī kṛpāru sabu heba.
Satyare sarbe ātajāta, satya kaḷi je heba khyāta.
Purāṇa Bhāgabata māna, karibe gāna saṅkīrttana.
Kaḷi kalmaṣa nāsha jiba, Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Kahanti Brahmāṇḍa karatā, ananta jugara byabasthā.
Mlechha nathibe eka jāṇa, bhakta Bhagavān milana.

Macne:
Mahapurush wuxuu yiri arrintani waa mid aad u qarsoon oo waxay dhici doontaa lambarka 33aad (sanadka 2033). Kaliya cibaadaysanayaasha ayaa awoodi doona inay ogaadaan tan. Aad bay ugu farxi doonaan ogaanshaha tan. Adduunku wuxuu ka xoroobi doonaa dembiga; ma jiri doono cabsi ama jahawareer qofna maskaxdiisa. Qof kasta wuu farxi doonaa, qof kasta wuxuu heli doonaa cunto iyo dhar ku filan, qof kasta wuxuu raaci doonaa jidka Dharma. Markaas waxaa jiri doona goosasho iyo wax-soo-saar aad u wanaagsan, tanina waxay noqon doontaa nimcada Hooyo Lakshmi. Qof kasta wuxuu ku socon doonaa jidka Runta; da'da weligeed ah ayaa imaan doonta. Qof kasta wuxuu akhrin doonaa Puranas iyo Bhagavat wuxuuna samayn doonaa heeso iyo ku celcelin. Kaliyuga si buuxda ayaa loo burburin doonaa; hadalkan ma libdhi doono gabi ahaanba. Mahapurush wuxuu intaa ku daray in tani ay noqon doonto da'da weligeed ah tanina ay noqon doonto habka da'da weligeed ah (Adya Satyayuga). Ma jiri doono hal qof oo aan xaq ahayn, xun, ama mlechha ah dhulka. Kaliya cibaadaysanayaasha ayaa sii jiri doona dhammaantoodna waxay la midoobi doonaan Ilaah.

Mahapurush dhashay qarnigii 20aad (oo aan hore uga soo hadalnay), isagoo soo xiganaya Sayidka Sare, Hooyo Yogamaya, iyo ilaahyada iyo ilaahadaha, wuxuu qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତିନି ଚାରି ତିନି ଧାଡିରେ ତ ଜାଣ,
ଏହି ଦିନ ଠାରୁ ହେବ ସତ୍ୟର ନିୟମ।
ଗୁରୁବାର ଦିନ ଥିବ ଶୁଣୁଥାଅ ନର,
ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ସତ୍ୟ ଭୋଗ ତ ଆମର।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Tini tini chāri tini dhāḍire ta jāṇa,
ehi dina thāru heba satyara niyama.
Gurubāra dina thiba śhuṇuthāa nara,
sehi dinathāru satya bhoga ta amara.

Macne:
Halkan Mahapurush wuxuu codsanayaa in la qoro “3” afar jeer oo isku xigta. Taasi waa 3333. Mahapurush wuxuu leeyahay laga bilaabo maanta, Satyayuga ayaa ka bilaaban doonta Dhulka. Halkan 3333, Mahapurush wuxuu ula jeedaa – taariikhda saddexaad, bisha saddexaad, iyo saddex iyo soddon sano, taas oo ah, Maarso 3, 2033. Mahapurush wuxuu intaa ku daray in maalintaas ay noqon doonto Khamiis oo Satyayuga ay ka bilaaban doonto maalintaas.

Mahapurush wuxuu mar kale qoray –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତାରିଖ ତ ତିନି ମାସ ବିଂଶ ଶାଳ,
ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କ ତ ପରେ ସତ୍ୟ ସତ୍ୟ ବୋଲ।

Qoraal-u-beddelid:

Tini tārikha ta tini māsa biṃśha śhāḷa,
tetishā aṅka ta pare satya satya bola.

Macne:
Taas macnaheedu waa, ka dib taariikhda saddexaad ee bisha saddexaad ee lambarka saddex iyo soddonaad ee sanadka labaatanaad, taas oo ah, Maarso 3, 2033, Satyayuga ayaa imaan doonta.

Sidaa darteed, hadda ma jiro wax shaki ah in xilli cusub (Ananta Yuga ama Adi Satyayuga) uu bilaaban doono sanadka 2033. Waxay noqon doontaa xilliga cibaadaysanaha iyo Ilaah oo keliya. Si kale haddii loo dhigo, kaliya cibaadaysanayaasha ayaa awoodi doona inay galaan xilligan. Sayidku wuxuu dhulka ku ilaalin doonaa Runta, Dharma, iyo Cadaaladda, dhammaan cibaadaysanayaashuna waxay ku dhaqmi doonaan Runta, Nabadda, Naxariista, iyo Cafiska noloshooda.

14 Soo Koobid

Bhabishya Malika waa ururin qoraallo badan oo ay qoreen Panchasakhas oo dhashay qiyaastii 600 sano ka hor iyo qaar ka mid ah awliyada kale ee Odia iyo ragga waaweyn ee dhashay qarniyadii 17aad, 18aad, 19aad, iyo 20aad. Qoraallada Malika waxaa inta badan qoray Panchasakhas ee Sayid Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Panchasakhas-kan waxay u muuqdaan inay yihiin wehelada dhow ee Sayidka da' kasta oo leh magacyo kala duwan, iyo Kaliyuga sidoo kale, qiyaastii 600 sano ka hor, Panchasakhas-kan waxay heleen dhowaanshaha muuqashada Sayidka, Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Magacyada Panchasakhas-kan ee ku dhashay Kaliyuga waa – Achyutananda Das, Balaram Das, Jashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, iyo Jagannath Das.

Panchasakhas waxaa loo tixgeliyaa inay yihiin muuqashooyin qayb ah (Anshavatar) oo Sayidka ah, qiyaastii 600 sano ka horna, qoraallada Malika waxaa qoray iyaga oo keliya amarka Sayidka. Ujeedada kaliya ee qorista qoraallada Malika waa daryeelka cibaadaysanayaasha – in la kiciyo miyirka hurda ee cibaadaysanayaasha dhammaadka Kaliyuga iyo in la badbaadiyo iyaga oo la hagayo. Panchasakhas waxay si cad ugu qoreen qoraalladooda in qoraalladan aan loo muujin karin cid kale oo aan ahayn cibaadaysanayaasha. Panchasakhas waxay sidoo kale caddeeyeen in kaliya kuwa aqbali doona ereyada Malika ay yihiin kuwa cibaadaysane u ahaa Sayidka dhalashooyinkii iyo da'dii hore.

Maadaama Malika ay tahay codka Mahaprabhu laftiisa oo ay qoreen Panchasakhas oo keliya amarka Sayidka, sidaa darteed eray kasta oo Malika ah wuxuu la mid yahay xariiq dhagax lagu xardhay oo aan waligiis khaldami karin oo aan waligiis la beddeli karin. Ma jirto wax la yaab leh oo ku saabsan in saadaalo aan tiro lahayn oo Malika ah oo loo qoray waqtigan xaadirka ah ay noqdeen 100% run. Tusaale ahaan – saadaasha madaxbannaanida Hindiya, saadaasha dhalashada Gandhiji, saadaasha samaynta waddan la yiraahdo Pakistan, saadaasha dhismaha biyo-xireenka Hirakud ee Odisha, iwm.

Bhabishya Malika waxay si faahfaahsan uga hadlaysaa mowduucyo ay ka mid yihiin dhammaadka Kaliyuga, Kalki Avatar, burburinta kuwa xunxun iyo dembiilayaasha, ilaalinta cibaadaysanayaasha, dhismaha Dharma, imaatinka Ananta Yuga, iwm. Malika waxay leedahay da'da Kaliyuga, oo ahayd 4,32,000 sano, waxaa loo dhimi doonaa kaliya 5,000 sano sababo la xiriira falal dembi ah oo kala duwan oo lagu galay Kaliyuga. Ka dib marka 5,000 sano ay dhaafaan, Sayid Jagannath wuxuu ka tagi doonaa Nilachala Dham, Puri, wuxuuna ku dhalan doonaa jidh bini'aadan ah Biraja Kshetra, Jajpur ee Odisha, ka dibna wuxuu degganaan doonaa Khandagiri, Bhubaneswar. Sida ku cad Malika, tani waxay noqon doontaa Kalki Avatar ee Ilaah.

Malika waxay sheegaysaa in dhammaadka Kaliyuga, cibaadaysanayaashu ay ka heli doonaan calaamado badan oo noocan oo kale ah macbadka Puri Jagannath iyo kaniisadaha kale iyo macbadyada Hindiya, iyagoo arki doona taas oo ay ku fahmi doonaan in Kaliyuga ay dhammaatay oo Sayid Kalki uu dhashay. Tusaale ahaan, duulista calanka ugu weyn ee macbadka Jagannath, dhicitaanka dhagaxyada oo soo noqnoqda ee macbadka, Nilachakra oo janjeerta, fadhiisashada joogtada ah ee gorgor korka macbadka, iwm. Waxaa la og yahay in dhammaan calaamadahan ay arkeen cibaadaysanayaashu sannadihii la soo dhaafay.

Malika waxay tilmaamaysaa dhalashada Sayid Kalki sanad u dhexeeya 2005-2008. Sida ku cad Malika, ujeedada Kalki Avatar waa in la burburiyo kuwa xunxun, la ilaaliyo cibaadaysanayaasha, iyo in dib loo dhiso Dharma dhulka. Malika waxay sheegaysaa in Yuga-parivartana-dan ay bilaaban doonto 2026-2027 inta lagu jiro Meena-Shani yoga ee Sayid Kalki oo ay si buuxda u dhammaan doonto 2031-2032. Inta lagu jiro, dhammaan hawlaha burburka ee waaweyn waxay dhici doonaan sanadka 2029.

Malika waxay sidoo kale leedahay in waqtiga uu Sayidku dhulka ku dhisi doono Dharma, magaca Boqorka Puri uu noqon doono Shri Dibyasingha Deb, taas oo macnaheedu yahay in dhammaan ciyaaraha burburka ee Sayidka ay dhici doonaan inta lagu jiro xukunka Boqorka Puri Dibyasingha Deb. Sida ku cad Malika, ciyaarahan burburka ah waxay inta badan noqon doonaan iyada oo loo marayo burburka Shan Cunsur (Panchabhuta) iyo Dagaalka Adduunka.

Malika waxay sheegaysaa in ka dib marka dhammaan hawlaha burburka ay si buuxda u dhammaadaan sanadka 2031-2032, Adi-Satyayuga ama Anantayuga ay bilaaban doonto sanadka 2033.
